OSINT brief · wb_watch companion research

РОЙ.МАРКЕТ: what is actually behind the “military marketplace”

A closed-access supply platform for unmanned-systems components, operated by a company wholly owned by a United Russia–sponsored defence-tech body, banking at Russia’s defence-procurement bank, wired into the Defence Ministry’s own payment infrastructure since launch — and built, deliberately, with almost no fixed physical infrastructure of its own.

Compiled 14 Aug 2026 · revised 16 Aug Primary sources: 30+ EGRUL/ФНС extracts, 25+ audited financial statements (ДАР FY2021–25, Наша Правда FY2024–25, Инвестментс FY2023–25, Орион FY2023–25, Winter Olympic Fund FY2023–25, Заволжское FY2022–25, Гарант Клуб FY2023–25, Финконсалтингк FY2023–25, Градислава FY2022–25), 8 Minjust annual/foreign-funding reports (ДАР, Наша Правда, Градислава, ЦБСТ), 8 platform legal documents, 2,889 Telegram posts Status: research complete, gaps flagged

Bottom line

Eighteen findings

  1. 01

    The Defence Ministry’s financial infrastructure was built into the marketplace from its first day. At the December 2024 launch, United Russia’s Sidyakin said so in as many words; ЦБСТ’s own doctrinal post the same day describes MoD maintenance units ordering parts paid “через финансовую инфраструктуру Министерства обороны”. This is not a volunteer supply drive that later attracted official interest. Primary doc

  2. 02

    РОЙ.МАРКЕТ has no warehouse network, by design. Its logistics are a distributed 4PL model in which stock stays with the seller until an order is placed. The only “РОЙ.МАРКЕТ warehouse” is a software scheme, not a confirmed building. Primary doc

  3. 03

    The operator ООО «МПТМ» banks at ПАО «Банк ПСБ» — Russia’s designated defence-procurement bank, and itself a co-founder of the platform’s owner. Account and BIC are printed in the platform’s own supplier offer. Primary doc

  4. 04

    The real physical build-out sits one level up: ЦБСТ has announced a network of “protected” technoparks, >100,000 m², >₽25bn — deliberately dispersed rather than centralised, explicitly for security reasons. Announced by Bezrukov personally on 5 August 2026. Primary doc

  5. 05

    The platform is not a public marketplace at all: it self-describes on its login screen as a “закрытая платформа инженерного снабжения”, with credentials issued only to vetted participants. Primary doc

  6. 06

    Its supplier-recruitment target is Wildberries and Ozon sellers — the exact population this project already tracks. All MoD technical-support units (ТЭЧ) were scheduled to connect by 1 September 2026, with other security agencies flagged to follow. Primary doc

  7. 07

    The governing body is a roster of the Russian state: the supervisory board is chaired by Federation Council First Deputy Chairman Vladimir Yakushev and seats the PSB chairman, a deputy industry minister, the Presidential representative for technological development, the head of the ОНФ executive, and the MoD’s head of innovation development. Primary doc

  8. 08

    The money above the chain is now traceable, and it leads somewhere significant: Фонд «ДАР» supplied ₽1.213bn of Наша Правда’s first ₽1.773bn — and ДАР and its sister fund «Градислава» are the two foundations identified by Navalny’s FBK, and by Western sanctions listings, as vehicles holding Dmitry Medvedev’s undisclosed assets. Press-reported

  9. 09

    ЦБСТ studied Ukraine’s military marketplaces in detail before scaling its own — publishing analyses of Brave1 Market, BraveOne and DOT-Chain Defence, down to commission rates and order volumes, months ahead of РОЙ.МАРКЕТ’s own expansion. Primary doc

  10. 10

    The group's real corporate footprint, pulled directly from ФНС/EGRUL, is much larger than press or Telegram sourcing showed: ЦБСТ owns an unreported Kursk production entity with radar/EW OKVED codes and two Rostec-registrar-serviced joint-stock companies; Наша Правда runs an internal lending vehicle that has moved ~₽133m to unnamed affiliates and just absorbed a debt-laden, weapons-coded shell company; and МПТМ's own ownership register was sealed from public view on 15.07.2026, weeks before this brief was compiled. Primary doc

  11. 11

    Наша Правда's own audited accounts itemise ~10,400 units of equipment — counter-drone jammers, thermal scopes, body armour, tactical headsets, FPV-drone components — held explicitly, in the fund's own words, “для безвозмездной передачи войсковым частям в зону СВО” (for free transfer to military units in the SVO zone). The same filing shows Наша Правда's sister fund «ДАР» disclosed its full governing board for three straight years, then began withholding it in 2025, citing possible impact on “the sources of donations and their volume.” Primary doc

  12. 12

    Наша Правда's own Minjust disclosures show a two-year, near-billion-ruble annual pass-through pattern: ₽1.04bn received "от российских некоммерческих организаций" in 2024, then ₽923.5m in 2025 — of which ₽900.96m was disbursed the same year, almost all (₽897.6m) to legal entities rather than individuals. The upstream and downstream counterparties are not named in these filings, but the scale and the two-year consistency match the fund's own equipment-procurement note (finding 11) far better than a one-off transfer would. Primary doc

  13. 13

    ДАР's own former management company, ООО «Орион», reads as a documented private-estate operation, not a normal property manager — leasing roughly 1,800 hectares in Приволжский district, Ivanovo oblast, for "hunting-economy" purposes, with a standing "содержание животных" (animal husbandry) cost line running ₽48–57m a year, its beneficial-ownership disclosure naming ДАР's own director. Following Орион's minor 1% stake in ООО «Гарант Клуб» leads, via ООО «Финконсалтингк», to the Фонд поддержки зимних олимпийских видов спорта — a ₽42.9bn treasury vehicle, larger than ДАР itself, that turns out to share Наша Правда and Градислава's exact office building at Kotelnicheskaya 25 and to be run by Бровченко Сергей Руфинович — documented brother of Наша Правда's own director, Александр Руфинович Бровченко. Three separate corporate paths converge on one building and one family. Primary doc

  14. 14

    «Усадьба Миловка» — the Milovka estate FBK alleges was transferred from ДАР to Градислава — is named literally, as a line item, in Градислава's own audited accounts. Its FY2023 capital-expenditure notes record ₽60.2m spent on "системы охранной сигнализации входных дверей с коваными элементами на объектах Усадьбы Миловка," alongside a named, itemised luxury complex: a Главный дом (Main House), Гостевой дом (Guest House), a 63-person hotel building, a mill, a gamekeeper's lodge, a checkpoint building, a pond, a building with its own swimming lane, a boiler house, gated security infrastructure, and dozens of custom solid-oak furniture pieces. Градислава's own OKTMO registration for this property names с. Миловка, Приволжский район, Ивановская область directly, and its own notes describe the underlying transactions as "financial stabilisation" of a subsidiary's property that is a registered regional architectural heritage monument — the legal vehicle dressing the estate's upkeep as heritage preservation. This converts FBK's Milovka claim from investigative allegation to something documented in the target's own primary-source accounting records. Primary doc

  15. 15

    ООО «Левит»'s own filings quantify, for the first time, exactly what ДАР's founding fortune is made of and what it costs the Russian state to have wiped out a piece of it once already. Левит's core asset is 222,202,526 shares of ПАО «НОВАТЭК», worth ₽264.65bn at end-2025 — Mikhelson (78.29%) and Simanovsky (21.71%) drew ₽15.88bn in dividends from it in 2025 alone, plus a further ₽6.86bn resolved in January 2026. The same filing puts a hard number on the Первобанк collapse already in this brief: ₽1.65bn written off as unrecoverable, tied explicitly to the share-exchange agreement that preceded Promsvyazbank's 2017–18 nationalisation. Primary doc

  16. 16

    A fourth foundation flagged only as a name in this brief — «Фонд поддержки национальных морских программ» — is now independently confirmed as real, and structurally identical to ДАР. Its own Minjust filing names Устратов Дмитрий Владимирович as General Director since 30.04.2021 — the same name already sitting on Наша Правда's own Наблюдательный совет under the employer field "Фонд Поддержки нац. морских проектов," now matched person-to-person. The fund holds ₽4.729bn in total assets, has no real estate of any kind, runs on just 3 staff plus 5 external совместители, and in FY2025 spent ₽716.1m — almost entirely (₽583m+) coded as "предоставление безвозмездной финансовой помощи некоммерческим организациям," financial aid passed on to other NGOs, naming no recipients. Separately, the Winter Olympic Fund's own Minjust filing — pulled for the first time this pass — independently corroborates its ₽42.9bn ГИР БО asset figure exactly, and adds a striking anomaly: its FY2025 spending (₽1.067bn total) is booked almost entirely (₽925.3m) as maintenance of "objects with a sporting purpose," yet the same filing's own property schedule discloses no sports facility at all — only a single 112.8 m² rented Moscow office. Primary doc

  17. 17

    ФКК's own founding charter names ДАР directly — settling, in ФКК's favour, a lead this brief previously carried as unconfirmed. Requested directly from ФНС (учредительные документы/founding-document copies), ФКК's original 2011 Устав is titled, on its own cover page: "Устав Общества с ограниченной ответственностью «Финансово-Консультационная Компания Фонда «ДАР»»" — approved 12 January 2011, registered 24 January 2011. This is a direct primary-source textual link between ФКК and ДАР, independent of and now confirming an OCCRP Aleph EGRUL lead this brief had explicitly flagged as unverified. It sits alongside, not instead of, the already-documented ownership chain (ФКК 100% owned by the Olympic Fund since 29 Dec 2012) — who held ФКК in the roughly two-year window between its January 2011 founding and the Olympic Fund's confirmed December 2012 stake is not stated in the charter itself (standard for post-2009 Russian уставы, which don't name participants in the body text), so the precise founder identity remains open, but the entity's own chosen name at birth leaves little doubt about its origin as ДАР's own financial vehicle. The charter also specifies a Наблюдательный совет — the same governance structure used by ДАР, Наша Правда, Градислава and the Olympic Fund. Primary doc

  18. 18

    The Defence Ministry's own seat on ЦБСТ's supervisory board is held by a Western-sanctioned former GRU cyber-operations commander. ЦБСТ's 14 Feb 2025 board announcement names «Александр Осадчук» as "начальник Главного управления инновационного развития Министерства обороны Российской Федерации" (head of the MoD's Main Directorate for Innovative Development, ГУИР) — a title independently documented under his name, at Major General rank, in the MoD's own КВЦ «Патриот» press material as early as 18 August 2022, more than two years of continuous tenure before the ЦБСТ seating. That exact title — "Head of the Main Directorate for Innovative Development of the Ministry of Defense of the Russian Federation, Major General" — is the one OpenSanctions (aggregating US OFAC, UK FCDO, New Zealand and Ukraine's own war-sanctions.gur.gov.ua registry) lists as the current role of Aleksandr Vladimirovich Osadchuk (b. 17.11.1962, Obninsk, Kaluga Oblast) — the FBI-wanted, US Treasury-designated former colonel who commanded GRU Unit 74455, the unit indicted by the US DOJ in July 2018 over the DNC hack, the DCLeaks site and the "Guccifer 2.0" persona used to disseminate stolen 2016 US-election material. This is not a namesake resemblance flagged as open in an earlier pass of this brief — it is the sanctions bodies' own listed identity for the man, matching ЦБСТ's own stated title exactly. Primary doc

Primary doc verified in an official extract, the platform’s own documents, or live technical response Press-reported attributed to a named outlet; wording not verified verbatim Open unresolved — do not publish as fact

What the channels changed

This was a Defence Ministry project from the start

A full historical pull of ЦБСТ’s own Telegram channel (2,291 posts, Aug 2024 – Aug 2026) and the Наша Правда fund’s channel (598 posts, May 2023 – Aug 2026) moved the single most important fact in this brief from inference to record. The earlier draft treated the 1 September 2026 connection of MoD maintenance units as a new development. It is not new. It is the completion of a design stated publicly at launch, twenty months earlier.

On 25 December 2024, announcing the “военный маркетплейс”, United Russia’s Alexander Sidyakin described the mechanics himself:

Мы сделали «военный маркетплейс», в нем достаточно просто авторизоваться: нужно пройти двухфакторную аутентификацию и «положить в корзину» необходимые комплектующие. Винты, рама, движки, система связи, все, что необходимо, чтобы собрать аппарат. Сегодня запускаем эту систему впервые после тестов, уже все работает. Вся информация обезличена, никакие данные военных не раскрываются, и сторонний человек доступ туда получить не сможет. Безусловно, финансовая инфраструктура Министерства Обороны встроена в этот процессАлександр Сидякин, руководитель ЦИК «Единой России» — @ANO_CBST, 25.12.2024 — verbatim

The same day, the channel published a doctrinal companion piece — “ТЭЧ 2.0 и военный маркетплейс: новая модель снабжения войск” — setting out the concept in full. It argues for replacing Soviet-era technical-support units (ТЭЧ) with thousands of front-line “field workshops” doing customised serial production, supplied through a marketplace interface:

То есть каждый ТЭЧ сможет самостоятельно формировать заказы, которые оплачиваются через финансовую инфраструктуру Министерства обороны. Поставки происходят быстро и своевременно, а логисты координируют закупки.«ТЭЧ 2.0 и военный маркетплейс: новая модель снабжения войск» — @ANO_CBST, 25.12.2024 — verbatim
Why this reframes the story

Every public description of РОЙ.МАРКЕТ since August 2026 has emphasised its civilian-marketplace character — sellers, baskets, catalogues, an invitation to Wildberries and Ozon merchants. The December 2024 record shows the payment rail was the Defence Ministry’s from the first working version. The consumer-marketplace surface is the newest layer, not the foundation. A piece that describes this as “a Russian Amazon for soldiers” gets the direction of travel backwards.

Bezrukov’s August 2026 statement of where it goes next

At the 5 August 2026 supervisory board, Bezrukov set out the scaling plan directly: the pilot phase is over, and the platform becomes a full IT system with distributed logistics, independent supplier operation, end-to-end order processing and access control. The channel states that all MoD ТЭЧ were to be connected by 1 September 2026, and that other security agencies (“другие силовые ведомства”) may gain access afterwards. Bezrukov repeated the latter point on 13 August: “Следующий этап — подключение к платформе новых силовых ведомств, помимо Минобороны, развитие B2B-направления и комплексных решений резидентов ЦБСТ”.

Kommersant’s 13 August report adds a catalogue target of more than 10,000 SKUs by 2027, extending beyond drone components into body armour and personal protection, electronics and field uniform — categories that overlap almost exactly with this project’s existing Wildberries dataset.

Bezrukov confirmed pilot status directly, five months earlier

A TASS interview published 29 December 2025 — five months before the August 2026 “pilot phase is over” announcement above — has Bezrukov describing РОЙ.МАРКЕТ’s status in his own words, on the record, as an on-again pilot with connected military units already ordering through it:

Первый военный маркетплейс — роймаркет.рф — находится в статусе пилотного проекта. Маркетплейс запущен, он работает. Пилотные военные части, которые к нему подключены и с которыми мы запускали этот проект, постепенно осуществляют там свои закупки. Мы в ЦБСТ решили с самого начала, что одна из наших ключевых задач — запускать и развивать проекты, которые настолько сложны, что их сложно даже решиться сделать.Андрей Безруков, председатель правления АНО «ЦБСТ» — ТАСС, интервью, 29.12.2025 — verbatim

The same interview gives ЦБСТ’s self-reported scale for 2025: “ЦБСТ за 2025 год удвоился” (doubled in size), supporting more than 400 companies, with those companies drawing more than ₽1bn in ФПИ (Advanced Research Foundation) grants and saving more than ₽500m in taxes via regional small-tech-company regimes. Bezrukov also gives a launch timeline for the ₽12bn ЦБСТ/PSB fund already documented in the table below — “в начале следующего года” (early 2026), with documentation “в завершающей стадии” as of the interview date — and states MoD contract volume for ЦБСТ-supported companies at “от 35 млрд рублей”, plus separately that more than 30 resident companies work with the Russian Navy, 16 with firm contracts.

The same interview is the first primary-source confirmation found of a named, in-service ЦБСТ counter-drone air-defence system, donated free (as an NCO) to Kursk region and “в конце декабря” placed into official combat use there, with named hardware: a radar-plus-targeting pairing called «Ступор», interceptor drones «Скворец ПВО» and «Архангел», a Starlink-signal-detection system called «Калинка», and an unmanned boat «Катран» — Bezrukov states the system has already downed a number of Ukrainian drones (“есть уже определенное количество сбитых украинских беспилотников”) and that ЦБСТ intends to offer the same system turnkey to other Russian regions. Note the name overlap, not yet confirmed as the same product, between «Скворец ПВО» here and the «Скворец» drone already documented above riding on the «Тень» comms base delivered to «Барс-Курск» (14.05.2025) — both are ЦБСТ-network Kursk-theatre air systems, but this brief treats them as unconfirmed-same pending a clearer description of either.

The Ukrainian model was studied first

ЦБСТ’s channel tracked Ukraine’s equivalents closely and analytically well before its own expansion. It covered the Ministry of Defence’s DOT-Chain Defence pilot (9 July 2025), profiled Brave1 Market as a platform that lets soldiers order directly from manufacturers and “обходит классическую военную бюрократию” (11 May 2025), and on 18 January 2026 published a breakdown of BraveOne Market’s 2025 results — roughly 148,000 orders worth ₽15.5bn, with the platform’s own revenue estimated from an assumed ~3% commission. Whatever else РОЙ.МАРКЕТ is, it is not an independent invention, and its operators have not pretended otherwise internally.

Question one

Physical infrastructure: there is deliberately very little

The central finding of this pass is a negative one, and it is more interesting than a warehouse address would have been. РОЙ.МАРКЕТ is architected so that it does not need warehouses — and the reason is stated openly in Russian press coverage: security.

The platform disclaims being a warehouse operator

The agent offer (the contract suppliers actually sign) is explicit that the operator is not a logistics company:

Агент не является перевозчиком, экспедитором, складским оператором или лицом, самостоятельно оказывающим услуги доставки, если иное прямо не согласовано Сторонами в письменной формеАгентский договор-оферта о сотрудничестве с Поставщиком, ред. 3.0, в силе с 01.08.2026 — verbatim

The separate warehouse regulation then defines two schemes, only one of which involves the platform touching goods at all:

• Склад РОЙ.МАРКЕТ: площадка учитывает принятый товар, резервирует его, комплектует и передаёт перевозчику. • Склад продавца: поставщик поддерживает актуальный остаток, выполняет сборку и передаёт отправление логистическому партнёру в установленный срок.Регламент склада и отгрузки, ред. 1.0, действует с 20 июля 2026 — verbatim

No address, no capacity, no location is given for “Склад РОЙ.МАРКЕТ” in any of the eight published legal documents. Press coverage of the 13 August 2026 relaunch describes the model as distributed 4PL, with goods remaining with the seller until an order is placed — which is consistent with the documents and, in practice, means the “warehouse” is an inventory-accounting construct rather than a place. Press-reported

The real estate is at ЦБСТ level, and it is being dispersed on purpose

Kommersant reported on 5 August 2026 that ЦБСТ — the platform owner’s parent — plans a network of specialised protected technoparks totalling more than 100,000 m², at an investment of more than ₽25bn, over roughly five years. The reporting states the single-large-campus model was rejected on security grounds, in favour of a distributed network of production floors and engineering offices spread across the country. Individual regions were not named in that report.

Why this matters for the article

This is the same lesson Wildberries learned the hard way. A war-supply chain whose warehouses are large, fixed and known is a target set; one whose stock sits dispersed across hundreds of individual sellers, feeding a 4PL routing layer, is not. The Russian war-goods marketplace that emerged in the weeks after Ukrainian drones hit Wildberries’ warehouses is being built, explicitly, without the thing that got hit.

The registered address is an office, not a facility

МПТМ’s registered address — printed in its own supplier offer — is 125167, г. Москва, аллея Нарышкинская, д. 5, стр. 2, помещ. 1/1. That building is a class B+ business centre of roughly 4,500 m² with about 26 tenant organisations, pass-controlled, near Petrovsky Park. It is an office address.

Four entities of interest in this investigation are registered in that one building: МПТМ, ООО «Аэротехальянс», ООО «Оупен Лоджистикс», and both Azimut companies. In a 26-tenant building that concentration is notable — but it is not proof of operational linkage the way a shared private facility would be, and it should not be written up as such. The Azimut group appears to be a genuine long-standing tenant there since 2016; МПТМ registered into the same building in 2025.

525 residents, and how many of them are under EU sanctions

ЦБСТ’s own channel, not press coverage, is the primary source for its residency numbers. On 5 August 2026, reporting the outcome of that day’s supervisory-board meeting — the same meeting Kommersant covered — the channel posted the underlying figures directly:

За 2,5 года работы Центр поддержал более 850 инноваторов и технологических предприятий, работающих на оборону и технологический суверенитет России. Среди них — резиденты ЦБСТ и участники Акселератора проектов специального и двойного назначения... На сегодняшний день ЦБСТ объединяет 525 предприятий спецтеха и народного ОПК. Почти две трети компаний демонстрируют положительную динамику развития. В 2025 году их совокупная выручка достигла 81,8 млрд рублей, что на 30% больше, чем годом ранее.@ANO_CBST, 05.08.2026 — verbatim

The residency figures are the easy part. The EU-sanctions count is where this project’s own earlier draft went wrong — it carried an uncited “4 EU-sanctioned” label with no traceable source. The correct source turns out to be ЦБСТ’s own channel again, in two separate posts, and the real picture is larger than four. First, reporting the EU’s 17th sanctions package:

Из всех высокотехнологичных компаний в сфере БПЛА, попавших под санкции ЕС, четыре являются резидентами ЦБСТ. Это свидетельствует об их международной значимости и признании: их техническое превосходство рассматривается как угроза в условиях военных действий.@ANO_CBST, 20.05.2025 — verbatim

That post frames the designation as validation and does not name the four. Eleven months later, covering the EU’s 20th package, the channel named six residents directly:

В 20-й пакет ограничительных мер Европейского союза были включены организации, входящие в контур Центра беспилотных систем и технологий. Среди них «Технодрон», «Гаскар Групп», СКБ «Пиранья», «Аэро-Хит», «Атлант Аэро» и «Архангел».@ANO_CBST, 24.04.2026 — verbatim

The same post adds a named individual sanctioned earlier in 2025 — the chief designer of ООО «Центр комплексных беспилотных решений» (ЦКБР), developer of the «Джокер» FPV drone. Taken together, ЦБСТ’s own channel — not a third party — documents at least ten sanctioned residents/individuals across two EU packages by April 2026, six of them named. The platform’s own framing is unambiguous: sanctions are treated as a badge of technical credibility (“свидетельствует об их международной значимости”), not something to obscure. Primary doc

All six of the EU’s 20th-package residents named above have now been traced to specific legal entities via EGRUL, independently confirming ЦБСТ’s own channel and, in press coverage of the package (Interfax-Ukraine, Kyiv Post), Ukraine’s own war-sanctions.gur.gov.ua registry. «Технодрон» is АО «Технодрон» (ИНН 9704241986, Moscow, reg. 03.05.2024, OKVED 30.30.6 — production of unmanned aviation systems), maker of the «Скворец» FPV-drone family including a naval variant commissioned by the Russian Navy per press coverage of the sanctions; EGRUL’s own open tax data show 133 employees and, for the year through 01.01.2026, ₽447.5m in VAT paid and ₽300.5m in profit tax paid — implying revenue in the billions of rubles, making this one of the largest single manufacturers identified in this brief. «Гаскар Групп» is ООО «Гаскар» (ИНН 9710112989, Moscow, OKVED “производство прочих летательных аппаратов”) — 477 employees, ₽10.02bn in declared income (СумДоход) for the same period, 100% owned by ООО «СИБНК» — full name ООО «Сибирская навигационная компания» (ИНН 4205376019), now investigated in full below. СКБ «Пиранья» is ООО «СКБ Пиранья» (ИНН 7300024069, Ulyanovsk — historically Симбирск, hence “Симбирское конструкторское бюро «Пиранья»” per ЦБСТ’s own HeliRussia coverage), maker of the «Пиранья 15»/«Пиранья 20» cargo-capable strike drones (up to 6kg payload, 32km range); its registered OKVED is software development, the same purpose/activity mismatch seen elsewhere in this network. GD and 40% owner Чернышов Павел Алексеевич, majority owner (60%) Корчагина Ольга Александровна; 23 employees, ₽895.4m in declared income against ₽626.5m in declared expenses for the year through 01.01.2026 — a substantial operating business, not a small design bureau despite the "СКБ" (special design bureau) branding. «Аэро-Хит» is ООО «Аэро-Хит» (ИНН 2700012608, Khabarovsk, 100% owned by ООО «УК Комакс»), part of an apparent regional franchise cluster — separately-registered “Аэро-Хит” units also exist in Sakhalin, Moscow, and occupied Kherson region (Геническ). «Архангел» is ООО «Архангел» (ИНН 9714079108, Moscow, reg. 05.08.2025, ОГРН 1257700345407), branded by ЦБСТ as an interceptor-drone maker and 2026 accelerator finalist; GD and 100%-owner-at-filing-time Шатракова Анастасия Артёмовна (ИНН 773126193790) brought on a second 50% co-owner, Галиев Руслан Раилович, just weeks before this pull (23.06.2026). Its own founding Устав (pulled directly from ФНС, 05.08.2025 — the only document on file, so it predates the Галиев co-ownership addition and doesn't cover that transition) and FY2025 БФО filing (only one on record, signed 18.03.2026, user-supplied directly) tell a more modest story: registered primary activity is ОКВЭД 85.30 «Обучение профессиональное» (professional training) — a boilerplate multi-purpose charter (training, children's education, generic equipment production, cargo transport, general R&D), ₽10,000 charter capital paid in full cash at registration, with no drone-specific OKVED found, the same registered-activity/public-branding mismatch already seen elsewhere in this network (СКБ «Пиранья», below). Financially it's a pre-revenue shell as of 31.12.2025: balance ₽165,000, the explanatory notes stating directly “финансово-хозяйственную деятельность в отчетном периоде 2025 года не велась” (no business activity conducted in 2025), funded by a ₽151,000 founder loan — consistent with a company registered less than five months before the filing date. The most significant entity in this cluster is «Атлант Аэро» — ООО «Атлант Аэро» (ИНН 6154165878, Taganrog, home of the Kronshtadt/«Орион» UAV programme; press coverage of the sanctions describes it as a maker of FPV drones and components for Orion-type UAVs plus EW/digital-integration systems). Its scale dwarfs every other entity in this brief: 854 employees, ₽59.08bn in declared income and ₽3.22bn in profit tax paid for the year through 01.01.2026. Its ownership is the most consequential finding of this pass: 51% is held by АО «Проминвест» (ИНН 9714004945) — which is itself, per EGRUL’s own disclosure, under trust management (ДовУпрЮЛ) by АО «Технодинамика» (ИНН 7719265496), a Ростех (Rostec) subsidiary specialising in aircraft components — meaning a sanctioned, ЦБСТ-resident drone manufacturer is majority-controlled, via a documented trust-management arrangement, by Russia’s state defense-industrial conglomerate. The remaining stake is split between GD Хутешен Алексей Александрович (39%, increased just days before this pull, 10.08.2026) and two smaller individual holders. Beyond the six named residents, the same 20.04.2026 post names a sanctioned individual — the chief designer of ООО «ЦКБР» (Центр комплексных беспилотных решений, ИНН 5040176793, Zhukovsky — Russia’s historic flight-test-institute city), Кузякин Дмитрий Васильевич (also a 25% co-founder), developer of the «Джокер» FPV drone that ЦБСТ credits with Russian special-forces’ first FPV combat use, on the Hostomel axis, 10 March 2022; ЦКБР shows ₽577.2m in declared income and 30 employees, and counts a state-linked institution, Фонд содействия развитию науки, инноваций и технологий, among its four founders (24%). The post’s two further named entities, АО «Котлин-Новатор» (ИНН 7811075899, St Petersburg — a long-established Soviet-era radar/radionavigation-equipment maker, run by a “Генеральный директор-главный конструктор,” Жук Виталий Александрович) and ООО «Клеверкоптер» (ИНН 5257183517, Nizhny Novgorod, sole owner/GD Гусев Сергей Владимирович, 6 employees, ₽13.6m declared income), are comparatively modest by contrast. A tenth name, “Аэромакс,” was initially left unmatched — the first EGRUL candidate checked (АО «Аэромакс», ИНН 7708407413) declares zero employees and an odd secondary OKVED in forestry nursery services, clearly wrong. A stronger candidate has since surfaced: ООО «Аэромакс» (aeromax-group.ru, ИНН 7725347482, ОГРН 1177746007900, reg. 09.01.2017) is a genuine UAS operator — its own site describes "комплексные решения с использованием беспилотных авиационных систем" (integrated drone-based solutions) for oil&gas, agriculture, forestry, construction and energy infrastructure — with 148 employees, ₽3.18bn charter capital, and primary OKVED 71.12.44 (aerial survey/geospatial services) plus 26+ secondary codes in electronics/aerospace manufacturing and software; wholly owned (100%) by a holding parent, ООО «ГК «Аэромакс»» (ИНН 7710974353), director Бессонов Сергей Николаевич. A direct EGRUL pull (18.08.2026) confirms this identity, unredacted — the only entity found in this network's egrul.nalog.ru extracts with no access-restriction markers at all: Бессонов Сергей Николаевич (personal ИНН 644914040799) was appointed director 11.09.2025; sole founder is ООО «Группа компаний «Аэромакс»» (ИНН 7710974353, ОГРН 1147748024269) at 100%, nominal stake value ₽3,177,522,000, recorded 14.07.2026; primary OKVED is confirmed as 71.12.44 ("создание топографических карт и планов" — topographic mapping), with a secondary forestry code (02.10) also present, echoing the same odd forestry-adjacent OKVED pattern seen on the discarded АО «Аэромакс» candidate — coincidental, not a sign these are related entities. Financially, disclosure appears stale: ФНС's bo.nalog.gov.ru open search shows only one filing on record, for FY2022 (revenue ₽256.86m, filed 31.03.2023) — no FY2023, 2024 or 2025 filing found, the same lapsed-disclosure pattern already documented at 3mx and elsewhere in this network. The ЦБСТ tie is now directly confirmed by ЦБСТ's own channel: its 24.04.2026 post on the EU's 20th sanctions package (t.me/ANO_CBST/2095) explicitly names «Аэромакс» among the sanctioned residents, alongside «Котлин-Новатор», «Клеверкоптер» and МФТИ (МФТИ listed for drone-developer partnerships, not as a résident itself) — the exact primary-source confirmation this thread lacked. A scan of the company's own Telegram channel (@aeromax_group, 1,769 subscribers, 848 posts) adds a much larger corporate story that this résident sits inside: АО «Кронштадт», maker of the «Орион-Э» combat drone (the same corporate family as this brief's «Атлант Аэро», 51%-controlled via a Технодинамика/Ростех trust-management arrangement documented above) and a recipient of state gosoboronzakaz contracts, rebranded to ГК «Аэромакс» on 18.05.2026, merging "Кронштадта, Аэромакса и ИКАР" (ИКАР being a former Airbus-Russia MRO subsidiary) into one platform — since described in the channel's own posts as developing a heavy Arctic cargo UAV helicopter (АЭРОМАКС В-700, under Минпромторг support), exhibiting at HeliRussia 2026 (В-200), and signing an MOU with Vietnam's NST Group (261 of 848 scanned posts mention "Кронштадт"). This makes «Аэромакс» one of the largest and most state-integrated entities in this brief's ЦБСТ-résident roster — not a small incubator startup but a top-5 civil-UAV group with existing gosoboronzakaz fulfilment and a second, independent Rostec-adjacent ownership link (via the Атлант Аэро/Технодинамика trust) sitting in the same corporate family. EGRUL identity: ООО «Аэромакс» (ИНН 7725347482, subsidiary of ГК «Аэромакс»/ex-Кронштадт). The EU's own listing text (Council Decision (CFSP) 2026/504, OJ L, 23.4.2026, entry 759, user-supplied primary document) is now pulled directly — "LLC 'AEROMAKS' develops software for drones to perform various aviation operation including aerial photography, collection, processing, and preparation of cartographic data and space information. It is also a manufacturer of helicopter type drones of SH-3000/-750/-450/-350, and in general develops 11 types of drones. In 2023 the company opened its own Aviation Training Centre approved by the Ministry of Labour of the Russian Federation. By the end of 2024 the company had 13 government contracts signed and Gazprom Neft, Russian Post and Moscow City Department of Information Technology were among its customers. Therefore, LLC AEROMAKS is materially supporting, and benefitting from the Government of the Russian Federation... [and] is forming part of and materially supporting Russia's military and industrial complex." — quoted verbatim, matching the company's own EGRUL details (ОГРН 1177746007900, ИНН 7725347482) exactly and confirming three named civilian/state customers (Газпром Нефть, Russian Post, Moscow City IT Dept.) alongside the 13 government contracts. The timing against the Кронштадт merger is notable: EU listing 23.4.2026, folded into the Кронштадт-led ГК «Аэромакс» rebrand less than a month later (18.05.2026) — not established as sanctions-evasion motivated (Кронштадт itself, via «Атлант Аэро», was already independently sanctioned/documented in this brief), but the sequence is flagged for the record rather than asserted as causal. None of these ten entities has had a БФО filing pulled directly in this pass — the figures above come from EGRUL’s own published tax-payment data, not audited financial statements — so revenue/profit totals should be treated as directional, not final, pending the same primary-document pull applied to every other entity in this brief. Primary doc

The full ownership picture behind this rebrand is a genuinely large, independently newsworthy story, reconstructed from a direct EGRUL pull on the new holding (ООО «ГК «Аэромакс»», ИНН 7710974353) and Kommersant's own reporting (kommersant.ru/doc/8671194). Кронштадт was АФК «Система»'s (billionaire Владимир Евтушенков) drone division, acquired for ₽4.8bn in 2015 when spun out of the St. Petersburg navigation firm Транзас; Sistema began exiting the group in 2023, and as of February 2026 full control passed to a closed-end investment fund, ЗПИФ «Технофонд», managed by УК «Мой капитал» — Kommersant reports the fund's "qualified investors" have no ties to Технодинамика, Sistema or other major corporations (Sistema's press office declined to comment), a claim worth flagging against this brief's own separate finding that a different part of the same corporate family — «Атлант Аэро», 51%-owned by АО «Проминвест», under a documented Технодинамика/Rostec trust-management arrangement — retains that tie; the two ownership chains inside the same broader Кронштадт/Аэромакс network are evidently not identical, and the "no ties" claim should be read as applying specifically to the new holding-company shareholders, not the group's subsidiaries as a whole. EGRUL confirms the holding's post-reorganisation cap table directly: ЗПИФ «Технофонд» and АО «Центр-Телко» (ИНН 7729150663, GD Синельщиков Андрей Валентинович — itself wholly founded by ООО «Группа компаний «Кронштадт»», meaning old-Кронштадт rolled a stake back into the new structure via this intermediary) each hold 49.659% (₽9.09bn nominal value apiece against an ₽18.3bn total charter capital), with a small remainder (0.682%) held by an individual, Кузнецов Павел Владиславович. The driver was a financial crisis, not routine expansion: RSBU net losses of ₽2.6bn (2024) and ₽4.5bn (2025) — Kommersant attributes this to "low profitability of defense contracts" and lower-than-planned demand for the flagship «Орион» heavy drone, plus over 300 resolved legal disputes (₽5.2bn) through 2024 and roughly 58 ongoing (₽700m+); the rebrand's stated rationale is pivoting toward civilian drone sales to escape the crisis, not a defense-sector retreat (military contracts continue in parallel per the brief's other Кронштадт/Орион material above). Primary doc

The registered address itself carries a layer of Soviet defense-industrial history — in the same broader redeveloped industrial area as, though a distinct footprint from, the historical plant site. ООО «Аэромакс»'s EGRUL address, 1-й Волоколамский проезд, д. 10, стр. 1, corresponds to the «Диапазон» business centre — Class B+, built out in 2006, ~48,265m² total — at 55°48'15.91"N 37°29'25.71"E, the company occupying a second-floor office per the user's own site visit; a corrected pull confirms this is farther from, not directly on, the former First Moscow Radio Components Plant (Первый Московский завод радиодеталей, Soviet Plant No. 29) footprint that gives the wider area its history — founded 1932, repairing tank/vehicle engines during WWII before becoming a dedicated state electronics facility from 1951, manufacturing resistors and hybrid microchips through the Cold War, closed in the 1990s. Company signage at the location still reads "Кронштадт," not "Аэромакс" — Yandex Maps' own business listing (yandex.com/maps/org/kronshtadt/52903985689) has not yet been updated to reflect the May 2026 rebrand, a small but concrete confirmation that the corporate transition documented above is very recent and still working through third-party records. Field observation

An OCCRP Aleph search (by ИНН, authenticated API, 19.08.2026) shows this is a multi-jurisdictional sanctions case, not just the EU: Aleph's Global Sanctions Database aggregation lists ООО «Аэромакс» under OFAC's SDN list (U.S. Treasury), independently confirmed by a direct pull of the listing (sanctionssearch.ofac.treas.gov/Details.aspx?id=50772) — programme RUSSIA-EO14024, matching ИНН 7725347482 and ОГРН 1177746007900 exactly, address Милютинский пер., д. 13 (a second registered address beyond the Диапазон business centre documented above), aliases "AEROMAX GROUP" and "AEROMAX LTD," and explicit secondary-sanctions risk language (referencing Section 11 of EO 14024 — the provision under which non-US parties doing business with the entity can themselves face US sanctions exposure). The same Aleph aggregation also surfaces a French Treasury asset-freeze registry entry (gels-avoirs.dgtresor.gouv.fr) and a Ukrainian-language name variant ("Товариство з обмеженою відповідальністю «Аеромакс»"), consistent with the company also appearing on Ukraine's own war-sanctions registry. One additional historical detail surfaced incidentally: a 2022 Marathon Group leak (DDoSecrets, unrelated Vinokurov/pharma-investment-firm material — the Аэромакс/Кронштадт mention appears only as one row in a large, apparently-generic bulk compliance/ownership-chain reference table attached to an unrelated Биннофарм-pharma email, not evidence of any Marathon Group ownership tie) records this same ИНН/ОГРН under a third historical name, «Кронштадт Аэро» — consistent with the entity being renamed at least twice (Кронштадт Аэро → Аэромакс, pre-dating the 2026 group-wide rebrand) before ending up as today's ООО «Аэромакс». Primary doc

Persons of interest, and a fuller product line, established via press coverage (Kommersant, CNews, AEX.ru, ВПК.name — user-directed follow-up research, 19.08.2026). The most significant individual is Борис Сергеевич Алёшин — a Russian Academy of Sciences academician, doctor of technical sciences, and chair of Кронштадт's board of directors until his 2023 departure, whose exit press coverage links directly to the wave of 300+ contract-dispute lawsuits that followed. He is separately EU-sanctioned since June 2023 over the war in Ukraine, and in September 2023 Finland seized two adjoining resort properties of his (combined value €2.5m+) under a Foreign Ministry enforcement request; he also serves as an adviser to ОАК (United Aircraft Corporation), the state holding whose subsidiaries build MiG and Sukhoi fighters — meaning the same individual sat atop both a ЦБСТ-adjacent drone group's board and a state fighter-jet manufacturer's advisory structure simultaneously. Current leadership, post-rebrand: Георгий Андреевич Скорятин, General Director of the holding ГК «Аэромакс», publicly framed the merger as building "структуру, способную обеспечивать полный жизненный цикл БАС" (a structure covering the full UAS lifecycle); Валерий Пащенко, Deputy General Director for Business Development, told AEX.ru that "Kronshtadt as a brand was historically associated with defense topics" — an explicit admission that the rebrand is image management, not just a merger; Бессонов Сергей Николаевич remains GD of the operating subsidiary ООО «Аэромакс» specifically (already documented above). Sergey Chemezov (Rostec CEO) is quoted in press coverage characterising defense-contract margins generally (2.2% actual vs 5–10% needed) — context for the group's financial crisis, not a Chemezov/Rostec ownership claim; separately, Rostec's Технодинамика reportedly considered acquiring the distressed group but did not, a detail that reinforces rather than contradicts this brief's earlier finding that the new ЗПИФ «Технофонд»/Центр-Телко ownership has no direct Технодинамика tie. Fuller product lineup: military — «Орион» (heavy strike/reconnaissance UAV) and «Д-20»/«Д-20К» (light aircraft-type); civilian — SH-750/SH-450 (heavy helicopter-type, alongside the SH-3000/SH-350 already confirmed via the EU listing above), В-200 (unmanned helicopter, presented summer 2026), В-700 ("Russia's largest unmanned helicopter," per AEX.ru, undergoing state certification — likely the same Arctic/Siberia heavy-cargo helicopter already noted from the company's own Telegram channel above), and А-50 (fully Russian-designed agricultural drone, launching summer 2026). The group states membership in the «АЭРОНЕКСТ» Association (a Russian civil-drone industry body) and reports a 33% civilian-market contraction in 2025 (₽15bn total sector revenue) as additional context for the crisis-driven rebrand. Press

Visual confirmation of АО «Технодрон» as a physical operation, not just a legal-entity/tax-data profile: a Readovka video report on ЦБСТ’s «Скворец» production line (t.me/readovkanews/92987, 5 Feb 2025) shows an assembly-floor worker wearing staff apparel printed “СКВОРЕЦ ТЕХНОДРОН” — a direct, on-camera pairing of the product brand and the manufacturer name this brief independently resolved via EGRUL. The same report includes field-test footage of assembled hardware and a named test pilot. On-camera speaker Александр Сидякин (already documented above) also appears in this report per its own lower-third caption, describing a standing joint MoD technical council that vets every product entering the ЦБСТ pipeline — the clearest on-record statement of a formal military product-approval process sitting behind the marketplace:

У нас есть техсовет совместно с Минобороны. Мы регулярно проводим испытания изделий, которые к нам заходятАлександр Сидякин, руководитель ЦИК «Единой России» — t.me/readovkanews/92987, 05.02.2025 — verbatim, partial (caption cut off mid-sentence in source video)

No full faces are usable for identification in this report — production-floor workers wear surgical masks throughout and the field pilot is capped/scarfed — so beyond the Sidyakin quote this is product- and brand-confirmation evidence, not personnel identification. Primary doc

Five more names, quoted in ЦБСТ's channel across late 2025–2026 but never previously resolved to a legal entity in this brief, have now been triangulated via EGRUL — using product description, city, and (where given) a named GD as disambiguating terms against otherwise generic brand names. «Площадь», the target-designation/navigation software platform ЦБСТ demonstrated to troops on 09.06.2026 (“программная платформа «Площадь» одноимённой компании... способна обеспечить навигацию, автоматизацию полёта/движения, обработку данных, целеуказание, захват целей”), is АО «Площадь» (ИНН 9723203841, Moscow, reg. 10.07.2023) — GD Маслак Никита Юрьевич, 33 employees, ₽135.9m declared income, OKVED covering information-security/protected-telecoms production and wireless-communications services; a search on the bare name “Площадь” returns 144 EGRUL hits, nearly all restaurants and real-estate firms, so the software-development/infosec OKVED was the deciding disambiguator. Площадь's own site (areasystems.tech) independently confirms this identification directly — its "О компании" page states the same ИНН 9723203841 and ОГРН 1237700463032 outright, alongside the exact registered address (Звёздный бульвар, д. 19, стр. 1, Moscow) and two named products, ПАК «Площадь.Р» (automated target-detection system) and ПАК «Площадь.К» (automated target-guidance system), describing GNSS-denied operation and machine-learning-based visual object identification with geolocation transmitted over wireless channels — matching ЦБСТ's own description of the platform closely. A full founding-document bundle (5 filings, 2023–2026, pulled directly via nalog.gov.ru, 20.08.2026) shows no ownership or purpose-clause changes, but real financial and governance evolution consistent with rapid growth: charter capital rose from ₽100,000 (1,000 shares) to ₽112,000 (1,120 shares) per a 10.11.2025 shareholder resolution, registered with the Central Bank's own securities registry 13.05.2026; more tellingly, the charter's own "major transaction" threshold — the value above which board/shareholder approval is required — escalated sharply across three successive amendments, from ₽25,000,000 (original 2024 charter) to ₽100,000,000 (18.03.2025) to ₽150,000,000 (current, per the 22.06.2026 restated charter), a 6× rise in under two years, consistent with the company's already-documented ₽135.9m declared income. A 18.03.2025 governance amendment also removed the prior carve-out requiring in-person shareholder meetings for board elections, auditor appointment and annual-report approval — every matter can now be decided by absentee/remote vote. No founder names are disclosed in any version (standard for a post-2009 Устав), but one individual surfaces as a meeting secretary on the 2025 amendment's signature block — В.А. Шашков (initials only per the document). Площадь's registered address, Звёздный бульвар, д. 19, стр. 1, is itself a Soviet defense-industrial design institute's own building — АО «Проектмашприбор» (founded October 1932 as ОАО «Институт по проектированию предприятий машиностроения и приборостроения»), which produced factory-relocation project documentation for 40+ evacuated defense enterprises during WWII and remains registered at the identical address today — the same pattern already documented at ООО «Аэромакс»'s Диапазон business-centre address (former First Moscow Radio Components Plant site, nearby but distinct footprint). Primary doc

«Рустехдрон» — named directly by ЦБСТ's own channel as one of three "компаний-резидентов ЦБСТ" ("ЦБСТ resident companies"), alongside «Стратим» and the product brand «Скворец» (Технодрон's drone, named by product rather than manufacturer), whose representatives received Минпромторг honorary certificates on 14.08.2025 — is ООО «НПО Рустехдрон» (ИНН 5003155499, ОГРН 1235000022179, Moscow, aircraft-manufacturing OKVED, reg. 27.02.2023); unusually, its GD and one legal-entity founder are both access-restricted in EGRUL (“ОгрДосСв,” restricted 12.03.2025 and 03.04.2026 respectively), so no name or ownership detail is available — the same redaction pattern already documented at Kaysant, Ушкуйник-СП and elsewhere in this network, here applied to a company that received a ministerial award in person. Its registered address, ул. Пехорская, д.1/3, sits a few doors from EU-sanctioned ООО «Гаскар»'s address (д.1Д, same street) — both in Moscow's Косино-Ухтомский district — a proximity that may reflect a shared drone-industry business-incubator building rather than a corporate link; not independently confirmed. A bo.nalog.gov.ru search finds no БФО filing at all under this ИНН — consistent with either a genuinely unfiled young company or a filing withheld from the public index, not distinguishable from the search API alone. A full 18-page EGRUL extract pulled directly (20.08.2026) adds real texture without breaking either redaction: charter capital is a specific, non-round ₽121,410,256, built up not in cash but via a sequence of in-kind contributions — four separate "АКТ ПРИЕМА-ПЕРЕДАЧИ ИМУЩЕСТВА" (property-transfer acts) in March 2023, followed by a further four "АКТ ПРИЕМА-ПЕРЕДАЧИ ИМУЩЕСТВЕННЫХ ПРАВ" (property-rights-transfer acts) in June 2023, each round backed by its own independent appraisal ("ОТЧЕТ ОБ ОЦЕНКЕ," a 3-volume report for the June round) — consistent with a spin-off funded by contributed IP/equipment rather than a cash-capitalized startup. Its OKVED list explicitly includes 84.22 "Деятельность, связанная с обеспечением военной безопасности" (activity related to ensuring military security) — a directly defense-coded classification not seen elsewhere in this brief's résident roster — added 27.02.2023, alongside the same sports/education filler codes (85.30, 85.41, 85.41.1, 85.42, 85.42.2) seen at КоптерЛаб and elsewhere. The company's licensing record is itself redacted — a "Сведения о лицензиях" section marked "Доступ к сведениям ограничен" — but the EGRUL change-log confirms a licensing authority reported granting it an actual license on 21.08.2024 ("Представление лицензирующим органом сведений о предоставлении лицензии"), meaning Рустехдрон holds a real regulatory license whose specifics are simply withheld from the public extract. Ownership changed twice very recently: the second, still-unnamed founder was added 03.04.2026, and the charter document itself was amended again on 18.08.2026 — two days before this pull — making this one of the most actively-changing ownership structures documented in this brief, with the changes themselves opaque due to the standing redaction. An independent investigative piece, published well before either redaction event, plausibly names the people behind that redaction: iStories' "Дрон Инферно" (19.01.2024) reports on a company by the exact same name, "НПО «Рустехдрон»," founded February 2023 in Vidnoe, Moscow region — matching this brief's EGRUL record's registration date (27.02.2023) closely enough to treat as a high-confidence match, though the article gives no ИНН/ОГРН to confirm it directly. Per the piece, ownership at the time was split between Илья Галаев (29.5% of the НПО, 40% of a related production enterprise, НИПП «Рустехдрон») — son of Виктор Галаев, an advisor to Rostec CEO Сергей Чемезов, a former logistics-bureau head at Uralvagonzavod (a Rostec military-hardware subsidiary), and head of the Федерация гонок дронов России (Russian Drone Racing Federation) — and Алексей Орджоникидзе (21% of both the НПО and the НИПП's parent holding company), a former Philip Morris executive and great-great-grandson of Soviet commissar Серго Орджоникидзе. That 29.5%+21% = 50.5% leaves roughly half the company unaccounted for in the article, consistent with this brief's EGRUL extract showing at least one further legal-entity founder. The Drone Racing Federation detail is a genuine cross-network link: it is the same federation in which Клёцкин Владислав Николаевич — GD of АО «АУРИН», КоптерЛаб's 80%-owning parent, documented above — serves as Vice-President, tying two separately-redacted ЦБСТ-résident ownership structures to the same small drone-racing-federation circle. None of this is EGRUL-confirmed for the current filing (the piece predates both redaction events by over a year, so the ownership it describes may not reflect who holds the stakes today), and is presented here as a strong lead requiring further verification, not a settled identification. Три арбитражных дела, pulled directly from kad.arbitr.ru, put real transactional flesh on the redacted entity. Twice in 2025 Рустехдрон sued and won against ООО «ДСГ» (ИНН 9724002908, GD Соколов Никита Дмитриевич — a different patronymic from РТД-Защита's Соколов Герман Олегович, so treated as a same-surname coincidence, not a confirmed family link, absent further evidence; ДСГ's own registered OKVED is "прочая деятельность в области спорта," the same sports-front disguise pattern already seen at Рустехдрон itself, АУРИН and КоптерЛаб): a debt claim (decided 25.12.2025, case А40-192834/25) establishes that Рустехдрон supplied ДСГ, in July 2023 — months after its own February 2023 founding — with 30 units of "Система сброса для гранаты типа «F1»" (F1 hand-grenade drop/release systems for FPV drones, ₽13,800/unit) and 50 GAONENG drone batteries, ₽714,000 total, unpaid; the court awarded the debt plus a ₽2.04m penalty. This is direct primary-source confirmation of Рустехдрон's actual hardware — grenade-drop mechanisms, not just the OKVED/product claims documented above. A second, parallel case (А40-192134/25, decided 24.09.2025, ДСГ's appeal rejected outright 06.04.2026) shows Рустехдрон also lending money to the same counterparty — ₽268,657 recovered in interest on an Aug–Oct 2023 loan. A third, far larger case shows the other side of Рустехдрон's own supply chain under real strain: А40-141771/26, filed against ООО «Специальные полезные технологии» (СПТ, ИНН 9715478264, reg. 21.03.2024, Moscow, electrical-equipment-manufacturing OKVED), sought to recover a ₽245,678,400 advance payment plus a ₽14.74m penalty under a 27.10.2025 supply contract that apparently went undelivered — resolved via a court-approved мировое соглашение (settlement, 08.07.2026) rather than a fraud finding, implying renegotiated terms. Рустехдрон's own follow-up appeal of an earlier ruling in the same case was bounced on a technicality (missing state-fee payment proof) on 07.08.2026. Taken together: a company genuinely transacting at real scale — a quarter-billion-ruble single advance payment, live supplier/lender relationships with named counterparties — while its own ownership remains formally redacted throughout. Its current registered address, ул. Пехорская, д.1/3, sits inside the Технополис Москва / Руднево special economic zone, confirmed against the technopark's own building directory (rudnevo.technomoscow.ru/#building) as корпус 15 — the ground-level entrance photo below shows the zone's shared entrance signage, with корпус 15 the building on the left; the numeral "16" visible on the neighbouring building's own facade in the same shot belongs to the adjacent corpus, not Рустехдрон's own. Руднево is also home to Moscow's own city-run UAV test site (i.moscow/bas, 55°43'34.62"N 37°54'58.03"E) — a separate facility within the same technopark, not part of Рустехдрон's own leased space, but establishing that this résident operates inside a park purpose-built with dedicated drone flight-test infrastructure on site. Rospatent's own open-data trademark and patent registries close the identity gap the EGRUL redaction leaves open. A direct grep of Rospatent's bulk open-data trademark-registry export (rospatent.gov.ru/opendata, no query limit, unlike the rate-capped interactive search) finds trademark registration №982517, filed 20.04.2023 — seven weeks after the company's own founding — and registered 01.12.2023 (valid to 2033): a combined grey/red mark filed by patent-attorney firm ООО ЮФ «АЙ ПИ ПРО» (Леонов Александр Владимирович), covering an unusually broad defensive spread of МКТУ classes (12 vehicles, 28 games/toys, 35 advertising, 39 transport, 41 education, 42 tech services, 45 legal/security — the class-45 goods list even names "услуги по наблюдению с помощью дрона," drone-surveillance services, directly) — the same cover-many-unrelated-lines pattern already seen in this cluster's OKVED filler codes. The applicant's address on the filing is Vidnoe, the same location as the dormant sister entity documented above, confirming the company started there before its later move to the Технополис Москва/Руднево building in Moscow (résidency confirmed from October 2023 per technomoscow.ru). Far more significant: the same applicant holds utility-model patent RU218475U1, "БЕСПИЛОТНЫЙ ЛЕТАТЕЛЬНЫЙ АППАРАТ" (Unmanned Aerial Vehicle), filed the identical day as the trademark (20.04.2023) and registered 29.05.2023 — pulled directly from FIPS's own document server. It describes a single-use kamikaze FPV drone: a rotor-frame UAV carrying an explosive payload with an electric detonator, triggered by a mechanical impact-rod mechanism (two microswitches clamp a rod that snaps on contact), with the frame and rod made of стеклотекстолит (fiberglass laminate) — a non-metallic material choice, the same design logic seen elsewhere in this brief (ЯРЛ's non-metallic sapper kit). The patent explicitly cites and differentiates itself, on cost grounds, from a real Western reference design: Canada's AerialX Drone Solutions Inc.'s "DroneBullet" kamikaze drone (patent RU2723203C1 / US2023088169A1, filed by AerialX founder Kenig Noam). The named inventors resolve a name this brief already has, and add two new ones: Дзема Вадим Вадимович, Свистунов Максим Валентинович, and — directly — Соколов Герман Олегович, already documented above as the sole owner/GD of the dormant sister entity (НИПП «Рустехдрон»/РТД-Защита); this is now primary-source confirmation he is personally tied to the parent НПО's own core product engineering, not just the dormant satellite company. One administrative wrinkle: the patent lapsed for non-payment of its maintenance fee on 21.04.2024 (recorded 07.07.2025), then was restored. The same three inventors filed a second utility-model patent the identical day (application 2023110165, 20.04.2023, registered 18.09.2023): RU220486, "УСТРОЙСТВО СБРОСА ПОЛЕЗНОЙ НАГРУЗКИ БПЛА" (UAV Payload-Drop Device) — pulled directly from FIPS, same applicant, same correspondence address/patent attorney. This is the exact engineering description of the physical mechanism behind the product this brief already documents via the ДСГ arbitration case above: a spring-loaded locking ring with an electric-drive release, explicitly designed to safely arm and drop Ф1 and РГД-5 hand grenades, and ВОГ-17/ВОГ-25 grenade-launcher rounds, from a UAV — directly matching the "Система сброса для гранаты типа «F1»" units sold to ДСГ in July 2023, three months after this patent's filing. The application cites and differentiates itself from several prior Russian and Chinese grenade/payload-drop patents (RU174729U1, RU201868U1, RU2733904C1, CN108371767A), arguing improved reliability and a swappable stop-plate that accommodates multiple munition shapes without redesign. Patent, court case and OKVED now converge on the same concrete finding: Рустехдрон is not a paper defense-industry shell but a functioning manufacturer of grenade-drop hardware for FPV drones, designed by named individuals now tied to this brief through two separate primary-source channels (patent + litigation). A third figure in this drone-racing-to-résident pipeline surfaces via a scanned 2021 championship protocol (user-supplied primary document): co-inventor Дзема Вадим Вадимович is listed as a "матчевый судья" (match referee) at the "СМ 37583 и 37584. Чемпионат России" — a 2021 Russian technical-simulator drone-racing championship organized by the Федерация компьютерного спорта России (Russian eSports Federation) — the same document's competitor roster also independently places Федукович Кирилл Олегович (КоптерЛаб's GD, documented above) in 2nd place, an unrelated cross-confirmation that the two men moved in the same pre-war competitive drone-racing circle years before either turns up in this brief's résident network. Дзема's own corporate history in the sport is directly documented too: he was 100%-sole-owner and GD, then liquidator, of ООО «ДРОН-РЕЙСИНГ» ("Drone Racing" LLC, ИНН 7728432295, ОГРН 1187746574696, Moscow, founded 18.06.2018, ₽20,000 charter capital, advertising-agency OKVED with 45 secondary codes) — liquidated 15.02.2021, just months before he appears as a referee at the June 2021 championship. Taken together with Клёцкин (АУРИН/КоптерЛаб) and Галаев (ФГДР/Рустехдрон), this is now the third documented figure whose path runs from the competitive drone-racing scene directly into ЦБСТ-résident manufacturing. «Аркодим» (Kazan), which ЦБСТ credits with neural-network target-tracking/ballistics-prediction algorithms for a “small-PVO” counter-drone system (12.05.2026), is ООО «Аркодим» (ИНН 1659101884) — a pre-war company, registered 08.04.2010, originally an industrial-robotics manufacturer, 50/50 owned by GD Барахтин Артём Владимирович and Соловьев Константин Юрьевич, 8 employees; EGRUL shows four affiliated “АРКОДИМ” entities at the same Kazan addresses (АРКОДИМ-ПРО, АРКОДИМ+, АРКОДИМ-М, АРКОДИМ-2), suggesting the counter-drone work is one line within a small existing engineering group repurposed toward military AI, not a new venture. «ЭйАрТэк», shown alongside СКБ «Пиранья» at HeliRussia as a fellow Ulyanovsk company demonstrating an alternative navigation system and BAS operator trainer, is ООО «ЭйАрТэк» (ИНН 7300000540, Ulyanovsk, reg. 07.04.2022, software-development OKVED) — four co-founders at 25% each (Жуков Андрей Викторович, Фомин Александр Николаевич, Перекатов Сергей Сергеевич, and GD Капустин Павел Евгеньевич), 7 employees, ₽61.9m declared income for the year through 01.01.2026. «Визионеро», named by ЦБСТ as an “IT-компания” resident (06.01.2026, GD quoted discussing early-stage scaling) without a named product, is ООО «Визионеро» (ИНН 5902056314, Moscow, reg. 27.11.2019) — GD Лысенко Андрей Олегович, 16 employees, mixed film/video-production and software OKVEDs (no drone/defence-specific code declared); one 37.5%-owner, Городничин Дмитрий Александрович, has his stake pledged as loan collateral to the Московский венчурный фонд (Moscow Venture Fund, a city-government-linked VC), a pledge recorded just weeks before this pull (29.06.2026) — the first instance in this brief of a ЦБСТ-adjacent equity stake used as collateral to a state-linked venture fund. Primary doc

ООО «СИБНК» — full name ООО «Сибирская навигационная компания» (ИНН 4205376019, Moscow, ул. Пехорская, д.1Д — the same building as its own subsidiary, EU-sanctioned ООО «Гаскар») — turns out to carry the single most serious domestic legal exposure found anywhere in this brief, independent of any Western sanctions action. Forbes reported (per Russia's own Investigative Committee) that Владислав Пыльский, head of a СИБНК project, was arrested 27 September 2024 on large-scale fraud charges (ст. 159 ч.4 УК РФ, up to 10 years) as part of an organised group, over military equipment supplied to the Russian Armed Forces in 2022 that allegedly failed to meet technical specifications and contract requirements — causing ₽164m in state damages, with ₽164m of company funds frozen by investigators. Pylsky separately runs ООО «ЭВР», 49%-owned by СИБНК (the remaining 51% held by “Центр управления городской аэромобильностью” — Center for Urban Air Mobility Management). Neither the FY2024 nor the FY2025 audited БФО notes (both pulled directly) disclose this criminal case anywhere — the FY2025 notes state flatly, for both year-ends, ”существенные претензии и судебные разбирательства отсутствуют” (“no material claims or legal proceedings exist”) — a direct contradiction of the Forbes/Investigative-Committee reporting, which named the company, dated the arrest, and quantified frozen funds precisely. Corporately, СИБНК's ownership has shifted very recently: 100% held by GD Ступников Андрей Генадьевич per the FY2025 filing's own related-party note, but EGRUL (pulled fresh) shows a new 26.25% stake carved out to Константинов Евгений Александрович — the same person who is now Гаскар's own GD — dated 29.05.2026, with Ступников's own GD appointment at СИБНК dated 29.06.2026, one month later; i.e., a leadership reshuffle across parent and subsidiary compressed into a five-week window, roughly 20 months after the Pylsky arrest. Its own FY2021–25 БФО filings (units confirmed Тыс. руб.) show explosive growth followed by a dramatic reversal: revenue of ₽9.7m (2021) → ₽147.3m (2022, the year of the alleged fraud) → ₽1.491bn (2023) → ₽2.077bn (2024, the arrest year, still highly profitable), then a swing to a ₽1.061bn net loss in 2025 despite revenue holding near ₽2.02bn — a ₽2.26bn profit-to-loss reversal year over year, unexplained in the notes beyond a cost-of-sales jump. The FY2025 revenue-segment breakdown shows the company is not primarily a drone manufacturer: ”Производство СКС” (structured cabling systems) accounts for ₽1.599bn (79%) of 2025 revenue against just ₽385.3m (19%) from “Производство БПЛА” (UAV production) — Гаскар's drone business is the minority segment of a larger telecoms-infrastructure manufacturer. The company is a resident of the «Технополис» Moscow special economic zone, paying a preferential 2% profit-tax rate. Its FY2025 related-party note names an unusually large affiliated-company cluster — 22 entities “controlled by or under significant influence of the same group of persons,” including two more Гаскар-branded siblings (ООО «Гаскар Интеграция», ИНН 7733894200; ООО «Гаскар Технологии», ИНН 9731070264), clustered around Skolkovo and a Ленинградское шоссе address shared by several “Аметист”-branded entities, plus one geographically striking outlier — ООО «Восточные ворота Забайкальск-Маньчжурия» (ИНН 7703420474), a border-logistics entity named for the Zabaikalsk–Manzhouli crossing on the Russia–China border. None of these 22 related entities has been individually investigated; flagged here as a substantial open thread. Primary doc

«Яким» turns out to name two different things, deliberately sharing a brand: ЦБСТ's channel (08.12.2025) describes it first as a combat detachment ЦБСТ formed on the basis of the “Барс-Курск” volunteer brigade, tasked with combat-testing residents' products, developing counter-drone defences, and battlefield analytics — named after a bogatyr companion of Alyosha Popovich in Russian folklore; there is no legal entity behind this sense of the name. Separately, at ПМЭФ-2026 (03.06.2026), ЦБСТ presented an automated air-threat-monitoring and interceptor-coordination system also called «Яким», shown on the stand of ООО «Конструкторское бюро «Синергия»» (ИНН 7842219445, St Petersburg, reg. 24.11.2023) — 100% owner Усачёв Павел Александрович, GD Некрасов Александр Викторович, 58 employees, ₽227.7m declared income against ₽261.6m declared expenses (a loss position) for the year through 01.01.2026. Three more names from the same “gift convoy” reporting have now been resolved. «Метаскан», maker of the «Арена 5М» demining detonator (“подрывные машинки”), handed over to Kursk-direction sappers 12.05.2025, is ООО «Метаскан» (ИНН 1660349248, Kazan, reg. 19.08.2020) — GD and 50% owner Поспехов Иван Александрович, the other 50% held by ООО «ЦЮС» (ИНН 1215226708, registered in Республика Марий Эл — the same region as ОПКБ «Экспломет» above; not established as connected), 4 employees. «Ментастеп», a mould/fungus-resistant tactical-footwear uniform project and 2025 ЦБСТ-accelerator special-prize winner (per Kommersant, via Наша Правда's fond channel, 04.06.2025), is ООО «Ментастеп» (ИНН 9715478320, Moscow, reg. 21.03.2024) — sole owner/GD Радченко Михаил Юрьевич, on the smallest available tax regime (АУСН). «Пехтра», developer of a muscle-signal (“myoelectric”) peripheral computer-control device for amputees and the same accelerator's other special-prize winner, is ООО «Пехтра» (ИНН 5261138034, Nizhny Novgorod, reg. 10.12.2024) — 100% owned by ООО «Стартап-студия университета Лобачевского» (Lobachevsky University's own startup-studio vehicle), GD Суховерхий Владимир Андреевич, zero employees, ₽415k in declared income — a pre-commercial university spin-out, not yet a manufacturer at scale. «ЯРЛ», maker of the fully non-metallic sapper's protective kit (body armour, helmet, visor and bag, dielectric to avoid mine-detector interference) handed over in the same Kursk convoy, is now resolved: bpryarl.ru (20.08.2026) identifies it as ООО «БПР ЯРЛ» (ИНН 9721200789, ОГРН 1237700170311, Moscow — ул. Ленинская Слобода, д. 26, Simono Plaza), certified to ГОСТ 34286-2017, making assault body armour across protection classes БР 1-5 plus modular protective systems and sapper-specific gear. list-org.com confirms sole founder/GD Ивлев Артём Юрьевич (100%, ₽5,000,000 charter capital, registered 02.03.2023) — the earlier "Roman" lead from volunteer-channel coverage was apparently a sales contact, not the legal director; the previous eight-candidate EGRUL sweep missed this entity because it's registered as «БПР ЯРЛ», not bare «ЯРЛ». Main OKVED is wholesale clothing/footwear trade, with textile and specialised-clothing manufacturing as additional codes — consistent with body-armour production. FY2025: ₽128m net profit, a real and profitable operation. Founding-document pull now made (nalog.gov.ru, user-supplied, 20.08.2026): the ФНС bundle covers three filings from 30.04.2025 onward — the original 2023 founding charter itself isn't included. A 30.04.2025 charter (Устав, "решением единственного участника ООО «БПР ЯРЛ» от 21.04.2025") sets charter capital at ₽2,000,000; Решение №7 (единственный участник, 30.07.2025, registered 06.08.2025) raises it to ₽5,000,000; and a 29.04.2026 restated charter carries that ₽5,000,000 figure forward — all three consistently sole-owner ("решением единственного участника"), matching list-org's 100%-Ивлев finding. No drone/EW/defense-specific purpose language in any version, the same pattern as every other founding document reviewed in this network. Primary doc

«Булат», the drone-detector line branded 3mx (3mx.ru), is this brief's first confirmed overlap between the РОЙ.МАРКЕТ/ЦБСТ network and Wildberries — the subject of this project's own parent monitoring effort — though a reseller-mediated one, not a direct manufacturer storefront: no Wildberries seller account carries ООО «Тримикс»'s own ИНН. That distinction matters methodologically, not just factually — an ИНН-keyed screening pass (the obvious first check) finds nothing here and would wrongly conclude this maker is absent from Wildberries; the overlap is real, but only visible by tracing the brand itself across reseller accounts. ЦБСТ's channel lists ”детекторы «Булат»” among the products its residents demonstrated at Дронница 2025 (08.09.2025), alongside «Гортензия», «Пиранья», «Буревестник» and others. On the Wildberries side, this project's own database shows the scale independently: 64 active listings for «Булат» detectors and accessories, 63 of them already classified strict_military by this project's own item classifier, sold across at least eight distinct reseller accounts, none of them the manufacturer's own — including two explicitly branded ”официальный дилер 3mx” (“official 3mx dealer”): ИП Артемьева В.М. (trademark “madro,” ИНН 350102453754) and ИП Коровкин А.А. (trademark “МАКС 911,” ИНН 780223272953) — plus six more accounts (ООО «ТСК Солид», ООО «Защитные технологии», ИП Прохоров, ИП Зверева, ИП Шестаков, ИП Миланович, ИП Кадыров, and ООО «Недвижимость Сервис») selling the same detector or compatible accessories under their own storefront brands. The manufacturer's legal identity resisted every normal identification route — EGRUL name search returned zero hits for “3mx,” “3МХ” or “Три Эм Икс”; WHOIS showed only a private-person domain registration; the site's own privacy policy, which Russian law requires to name the data controller, discloses no company anywhere — but it is now resolved: 3mx is ООО «Тримикс» (ИНН 7802942873, St Petersburg, reg. 21.07.2023). The link is not inference — it comes directly from EGRUL's own registered contact data for the company, which lists its email addresses as bank@3mx.ru and sales@3mx.ru and its website as update3mx.ru. True to the pattern seen repeatedly across this network, both the GD and all three founders/participants are access-restricted in EGRUL — a fresh direct EGRUL pull (18.08.2026) confirms the redaction covers three separate participant records, not one, plus the sole-director field, all effective 01.11.2024 (“Доступ к сведениям ограничен”) — the same redaction already documented at Kaysant, Ушкуйник-СП and Рустехдрон, here applied to a company whose products this project's own classifier has independently flagged as strict_military across 63 active Wildberries listings. Charter capital is ₽200,000 (increased from an unrecorded lower figure per a 27.02.2024 EGRUL entry). Registered OKVED covers electronic-component/communications-equipment manufacturing and wholesale (46.52 primary; 26.11, 26.30.17, 26.30.3 secondary) — consistent with RF-detection hardware. Only one БФО filing exists, for FY2023 — its first year of operation — despite obvious continued commercial activity since (ongoing WB sales, a «Булат v.4» firmware update cycle, a ЦБСТ showcase appearance in September 2025): revenue ₽45.6m, net profit ₽13.15m (a ~29% margin), balance ₽68.15m (₽30.6m cash, ₽24.8m inventory, ₽51.2m in payables — a fast, supplier-credit-financed inventory buildup consistent with the detector's genuine retail volume). No FY2024 or FY2025 filing has been made, despite the company plainly still operating — a non-disclosure pattern layered on top of the EGRUL identity redaction. This combination — real, substantial, classifier-confirmed strict-military sales volume on Wildberries, a ЦБСТ-showcase product credit, a redacted EGRUL identity, and lapsed financial disclosure despite ongoing trade — is exactly the seller profile РОЙ.МАРКЕТ's own “protected sales channel” pitch (see above) is aimed at capturing; whether 3mx/«Булат» migrates to or already lists on РОЙ.МАРКЕТ itself has not been checked. Primary doc

3mx's EGRUL redaction doesn't fully hold — secondary registry-mirror sites cached the founder data before it was hidden. Multiple independent aggregators (RBC Companies, Rusprofile, Zachestnyibiznes) agree on three founders, matching the three redacted participant records found above: Белоглазова Людмила Александровна, Шагиахметов Айрат Фатхутдинович and Шахматов Иван Анатольевич — charter capital ₽200,000 across all sources, matching the EGRUL figure exactly. Аggregator snapshots disagree on who currently holds the GD role (some show Шагиахметов, one shows Белоглазова) and on Шагиахметов's stake, reported as having fallen from 15% to 0% — consistent with a founder who has since exited the company, though the exact current cap table isn't independently confirmed and these are secondary, not primary, sources. Secondary source

The company has a real public-facing team distinct from its legally redacted ownership — 3mx's VK community page (vk.com/3mx.products, 7,700+ followers) lists four named staff under "Contact info": Sergey Shandobylo (Директор по развитию/Director of Development — the company's press spokesperson, see below), Alexander Alexandrovich (Старший менеджер/Senior Manager), Nikita Ivanova (SMM) and Sergey Fixov (Техническая поддержка/Technical Support). Шандобыло has been photographed and quoted at two separate public events: Interpolitekh 2024 (23.11.2024, per news.ru), discussing the «Булат» v.4 detector's shift to domestically-sourced components (explicitly no Chinese parts) and its companion wearable alert device «Таир» (individual 50m-radius or up to-30-device group notifications); and providing PMEF-2025 (Петербургский международный экономический форум) drone security for Экспофорум, per a TASS-sourced Деловой Петербург report (17.06.2025, via t.me/dprunews/55707, photo credit Сергей Ермохин/«ДП») — the same report independently confirms the company's founders are hidden in EGRUL, the last EGRUL change was October 2024, and staff headcount is 20. 3mx also runs its own operational-security posture publicly: a VK post (undated in this pull, positioned ~May 2026 in the feed) announces the company is cutting off phone contact, ending personal consultations and Telegram-chat support, and reducing YouTube/social-media activity, explicitly citing "the danger to our employees" and "internal security" as the reason — official contact is now email-only (sales@3mx.ru). Secondary source

A full sweep of 3mx.ru's own press-clippings archive ("СМИ о 3mx"/"Новости 3mx") turns up state-level access far beyond a Wildberries seller profile. Most significantly: Dmitry Medvedev, Deputy Chairman of Russia's Security Council, personally inspected 3mx's hardware on 27.05.2024 at the Песочненский military range in Leningrad Oblast — per TASS, he reviewed the «Айрат-100» anti-drone rifle, «Булат» v.3/v.4 detectors, and an integrated system called «Тамерлан», alongside St. Petersburg Governor Alexander Beglov and Deputy Defense Minister Nikolay Pankov. This is a direct addition to Наша Правда/ДАР's already-documented Medvedev connection elsewhere in this brief, now via a completely separate corporate path.
