Also found: a second, independent instance of Vladimir Yakushev (already on ЦБСТ's own supervisory board per the ГК «Логос» finding above) publicly praising «Булат» deliveries to Zaporizhzhia/Donetsk-front troops on Channel One's «Время» (09.04.2025); 3mx exhibiting its EW/counter-drone products at a CSTO (ОДКБ) military exercise at the Фахабад range in Tajikistan, in the presence of CSTO Secretary General Имангали Тасмагамбетов, CSTO Joint Staff Commander Андрей Сердюков, and Russian Deputy Defense Minister Юнус-бек Евкуров; a Технополис «ЭРА» (the Ministry of Defence's own military-innovation park) outdoor session near Novorossiysk, attended by Deputy PM/Industry Minister Денис Мантуров, First Deputy Defense Minister Руслан Цаликов, and Kurchatov Institute president Михаил Ковальчук (broadcast by Zvezda TV, aired 05.01.2024); and a Federation Council working meeting on critical-infrastructure security (03.07.2025), where 3mx presented its «Булат-Онлайн» early-detection complex. Press

3mx's own site also surfaces two former named officers and a possible personal-naming pattern in its product line. A company post (undated) announces the dismissal of Evgeny Polyakov (Chief Operating Officer) and Marina Vasilyeva (Financial Director), citing "работа на несколько компаний" (working for multiple companies) and unspecified audience complaints — the same post references the company's YouTube channel having been blocked in April 2024, corroborating the reduced-public-exposure pattern already noted above. Separately, the product catalogue's jammer/kit line uses a cluster of Tatar/Turkic personal names as codenames — «Айрат-100» (the rifle Medvedev inspected), «Тамерлан», «Сания», «Алия-350», «Айгуль-400» — a naming pattern worth noting given founder Шагиахметов Айрат Фатхутдинович's own first name matches the flagship jammer exactly. Secondary source

3mx's own "О компании" leadership roster appears to be fabricated from stock photos of unrelated foreign nationals — a separate, and more serious, layer of misdirection than the EGRUL redaction. The site (3mx.ru/company) names a leadership team — Александр Соколов (GD), Айрат Исламов (Chief Technology Director), Екатерина Звягина (CFO — note: distinct from Марина Васильева, the actual dismissed CFO named in the company's own news section above), Никита Зуев, Вячеслав Архипов, Надежда Юдина, Владимир Позов — that matches none of the other rosters independently found for this company (not the EGRUL founders, not the VK contact-info team, not the dismissed officers). A Telegram channel investigation (@DroneDetector, referencing a 29.04.2023 livestream where a "Макс DJI911" presented the 3MX team) alleges the photos themselves are stock images of unconnected people: "Айрат Исламов" is claimed to be Italian model Mario Perotto; the VP of Development photo is claimed to be a Turkish stock image credited "Anıl Varlıklı"; the COO photo is claimed to trace to an Amazon author page for one "Matt Hood"; and a company financial-director photo is claimed to be French national Patricia Martel (a specific LinkedIn profile is cited). This brief has not independently reverse-image-verified these specific claims, so it's reported as an allegation with named, checkable sources rather than a confirmed fact — but it fits, and would explain, the «Айрат»-branded product line: inventing a "Айрат Исламов" persona to match already-branded «Айрат» jammers is a more coherent explanation than coincidence. Secondary source, unverified

ООО НПО «КАЙСАНТ» (ИНН 7733393203) is a concrete candidate for the "manufacturer" side of this network, distinct from the marketplace-listing side documented above. Its ЦБСТ residency is directly sourced, not inferred: ЦБСТ's own channel names «КБ «Гермес», резидент ЦБСТ» twice — 14.11.2025, unveiling a high-speed interceptor drone against Ukrainian "Баба-яга" heavy attack UAVs (₽163k per unit vs. a ₽2m target cost), and again 13.03.2026, demonstrating the same interceptor ("Гусь," 10km range) at a ВДНХ security forum — plus a 29.12.2025 post congratulating "Гермес" on launching a satellite from Vostochny, describing it as "разработчик систем спецсвязи для БПЛА и робототехники," the same spec-comms description germesfpv.ru gives itself. Kaysant trades under two brands: «Гермес» (germesfpv.ru), the multi-band encrypted comms system for FPV drones/UAVs/ground robots pitched as a domestic ELRS/TBS Crossfire/FrSky competitor — evidently also a drone-interceptor developer per the ЦБСТ posts above, a broader footprint than the comms storefront alone suggests — and «Аргус» (kaysant.ru), a counter-UAS/anti-drone jammer line (АРПА-600 directional jammer, «Купол» omnidirectional suppression system). Note a name collision to avoid: ЦБСТ's channel (10.03.2026) separately names a different resident, Stratolink, as developer of an unrelated stratospheric solar/hydrogen UAV also called «Аргус» — do not conflate the two. Kaysant's own site states directly: "Успешно пройдены испытания на различных полигонах, включая Министерство Обороны Российской Федерации" — successful testing completed at various ranges, including the Russian Ministry of Defense. Founder and sole owner is Черепкова Нина Сергеевна (also GD since the company's 07.10.2022 registration); her identity was flagged "доступ ограничен" in EGRUL on 03.08.2026 — a restriction applied only days before this pull. Its own FY2023–25 БФО filings (pulled directly) show explosive real growth, not a shell: revenue ₽194.1m (2024) → ₽1.813bn (2025), a 9.3× jump, net profit ₽890.7m in 2025, large enough to trigger a mandatory-audit threshold for the first time. Its FY2025 notes disclose no related-party or customer detail (small-business exemption from ПБУ 11/2008 explicitly invoked), so the buyer(s) behind that revenue jump are not named in anything reviewed. Primary doc

ООО «Воздушный трактат» (ИНН 7100046925, Tula) is the legal entity behind «Овод» ("Gadfly"), a family of FPV strike drones that ЦБСТ's own channel repeatedly calls its "флагманский резидент" (flagship resident) — appearing by name in at least ten posts across 2025–26, including deployment at the "Запад-2025" strategic exercises (Мулино) and "Восточный бриз-2025" naval-robotics trials. On 05.02.2026 ЦБСТ reported serial production of a heavy strike variant, «Провод» (also called «Овод-Про») — fibre-optic-guided (RF-jam-resistant), 30km range, 4.5kg payload — with shipments "already going out, including deliveries to Ministry of Defense warehouses" ("отгрузки уже идут, включая поставки на склады Министерства обороны") and distribution to units handled by the military itself. Chief designer Андрей Иванов is quoted repeatedly; company founder and sole owner per EGRUL is Черезов Андрей Павлович (100%, also director since the company's 28.11.2023 registration). ЦБСТ's own 29.07.2026 post states plainly that Черезов "после начала СВО... основал в Туле компанию по производству FPV-дронов, вошедшую впоследствии в контур ЦБСТ" (founded the Tula FPV-drone company after the start of the SVO, later brought into ЦБСТ's orbit) — directly confirming ООО «Воздушный трактат» as «Овод»'s corporate home, and describing the drones as "одними из самых смертоносных отечественных камикадзе" (among the deadliest domestic kamikaze drones). The same post reports an assassination attempt on Черезов: shot three times in Tula by an unidentified attacker on or before 29.07.2026, hospitalized, condition unknown at time of posting — ЦБСТ frames it as an enemy operation against "нашего боевого товарища" (our comrade-in-arms). A second, separate assassination attempt is reported in the same channel on 05.08.2026, a car bombing near Yekaterinburg targeting Владимир Ткачук, GD of «Уралдронзавода» (another drone manufacturer, unrelated corporate entity) — his driver/bodyguard was killed; not established as connected to the Черезов attack beyond both being reported by ЦБСТ within a week of each other as attacks on drone-industry figures. Financially, «Воздушный трактат»'s own БФО filings (FY2023–25, pulled directly) show a striking mismatch with its "flagship resident" status: total balance only ₽2.67m (2024), shrinking to ₽802k (2025) with negative equity of -₽1.337m — a loss year, not the profile of a company shipping serial production to Ministry of Defense warehouses. No related-party or customer disclosure exists in the FY2025 notes (same small-business exemption pattern as Kaysant). Whether production/revenue is routed through a separate, unfiled entity, a state-contract structure outside normal commercial accounting, or the company is simply extremely lean is not established by anything reviewed. Primary doc

ЦБСТ's own 31.12.2025 New Year post named «НПЦ «Ушкуйник»» among its resident-company leadership alongside «Авирон», «Овод», АО «НПП», «Аляска» and «Визионеро» — but Ушкуйник turns out to be a parallel incubator in its own right, not just another resident. Its site, nvgushkuynik.ru, describes АНО «НПЦ «Ушкуйник»» (Veliky Novgorod, ИНН 5300010714, reg. 19.09.2023) as accredited by Russia's Ministry of Industry and Trade, hosting "30+ резидентов из 10 регионов России" across 2,500m² of production space plus a 140,000m² flight-test range, working on civil drone technology and counter-drone defence. Its own founder roster, pulled directly from EGRUL, is the clearest institutional link found in this brief between the Ushkuynik cluster and ЦБСТ's own governance: co-founders are the Novgorod Region Ministry of Industry and Trade (a regional government body), АНО «Платформа НТИ» (ИНН 7703469180) — the same organisation whose CEO, Dmitry Peskov, sits on ЦБСТ's own supervisory board (see executives table above) — АО «УК ИНТЦ «Интеллектуальная электроника — Валдай»» (a state-linked innovation-zone management company, listed on Ushkuynik's own site as the "I-Valday" partner), and АНО «ЦСАСД» (Центр стрелкового и авиационного спорта «Демянск», ИНН 5300008970, ОГРН 1235300001947 — a shooting/aviation sports centre; its own founder identity was restricted in EGRUL on 04.07.2025; only one БФО filing found, FY2023, indexed 25.03.2024, with no revenue figure disclosed — typical for a nonprofit sports АНО). Ushkuynik's GD is Чадаев Алексей Викторович — a well-known Russian political technologist and publicist, in post since 14.02.2024. A separate commercial arm, ООО «Ушкуйник» (ИНН 5300013923, wholesale trade OKVED, reg. 18.04.2024), had its own founder/director identity restricted in EGRUL on 15.09.2025 — the same recent-redaction pattern already seen at Kaysant, applied here to a second network entity. АНО «НПЦ «Ушкуйник»»'s own БФО filings (FY2023–25, pulled directly) show the same "sudden capitalization" signature already documented for Морских Программ: balance ₽2.73m (2023) → ₽12.6m (2024) → ₽1.127bn (2025), an ~90× jump in one year. Its FY2025 Отчет о целевом использовании средств records ₽1.046bn received in 2025 — ₽532.07m coded "целевые взносы" (targeted contributions) plus ₽514.3m "добровольные имущественные взносы и пожертвования" (voluntary donations) — against just ₽144.9m spent (mostly admin overhead and equipment purchases), leaving ₽908.5m unspent at year-end. Its own notes state funding comes from "добровольных взносов, пожертвований и государственных субсидий" — voluntary contributions, donations, and state subsidies explicitly — but names no specific donor or grantor anywhere in the filing. Press coverage independently confirms НПЦ «Ушкуйник» as a real drone manufacturer, not just an incubator shell: it developed the «Князь Вандал Новгородский» ("Prince Vandal Novgorod") kamikaze FPV drone, in serial production since January 2025 at roughly ₽80,000/unit, with Чадаев stating publicly that imported components were being fully replaced with domestic ones and manufacturing spread across several Russian sites; it has also tested a fibre-optic-guided FPV-drone-carrying unmanned surface vessel called «Скорлупа» (aviation21.ru). Neither ООО «Ушкуйник» nor ООО «Ушкуйник-СП» (ИНН 5300015906, reg. 04.09.2024, also 46.90 wholesale trade plus a secondary "производство измерительных приборов и контрольной аппаратуры" code, address on the same Большая Санкт-Петербургская street as ООО «Ушкуйник», a few doors down) has filed any БФО accounts — checked directly, neither turns up in ГИР БО (independently reconfirmed via bo.nalog.gov.ru's own search index, which returns no result for either ИНН). Ушкуйник-СП's own founders are also EGRUL-restricted (since 18.09.2025, one individual plus one legal entity, both withheld) and its director restricted since 26.06.2025 — the same recent-redaction pattern now appearing at a fourth network entity. Neither company surfaces in an OCCRP Aleph search by INN, and neither has any press or procurement mention found — treat both as inactive holding shells adjacent to the real operating АНО, not as independently active businesses, unless something further surfaces. АНО «НПЦ «Ушкуйник»» itself — not a subsidiary or holding shell — is a direct, sole-source government supplier of drones explicitly stated on the contract's own face to be for the war. Two zakupki.gov.ru contracts with a Новгородская область regional civil-defence agency (ГОКУ «Управление защиты населения от ЧС и по обеспечению пожарной безопасности Новгородской области») — №56, ₽4.25m, 21.11.2024, and №68, ₽3.11m, 18.12.2025 — both state the same contract subject verbatim: "Поставка беспилотных летательных аппаратов [и станций для их управления] для нужд проведения специальной военной операции и выполнения специальных задач по обеспечению обороны и безопасности государства" ("supply of unmanned aerial vehicles [and their control stations] for the needs of conducting the special military operation and carrying out special tasks of ensuring the defence and security of the state") — not press paraphrase or a euphemism this brief has translated, but the contract document's own operative language. Both are sole-supplier awards under Федеральный закон №44-ФЗ, ст. 93 ч.1 п.2 — procurement from a single supplier "определенного указом или распоряжением Президента Российской Федерации" (designated by a decree or order of the President of the Russian Federation) — meaning Ushkuynik was named as sole supplier by presidential-level designation, not selected competitively. Primary doc

Chadaev's public claim that «Князь Вандал Новгородский» has "imported components being fully replaced with domestic ones" doesn't hold up against a direct hardware teardown. Ukraine's GUR maintains a separate component-level teardown catalogue (distinct from its named-entity sanctions listings), and its own entry for this specific drone names 12 foreign electronic components across four countries: a Sigmastar processor and camera sensor (China), a Purui Semiconductor memory chip (China), a Unisonic reversible-motor driver (Taiwan), a Semtech laser driver (United States), a Realtek media converter (Taiwan), a Hangzhou Zhongke display driver (China), a CapXon electrolytic capacitor (China), a Winbond memory chip (Taiwan), and an STMicroelectronics STM32F405RGT6 microcontroller — traceable, per its own printed date code, to a specific production week (October 2024). This is a primary teardown record, not a marketing claim or an inference — it directly contradicts the drone manufacturer's own public statement about domestic-component substitution, made around the same period. Primary doc

Chadaev's own channel documents Ushkuynik's institutional footprint expanding well beyond the drone-incubator scope described on its own site — most significantly, a strategic agreement signed at ПМЭФ on 20.06.2025 to form a joint venture with АО «Концерн Калашников» in Veliky Novgorod: "«Ушкуйник» и «Калашников» создают совместное предприятие", with Chadaev quoted directly framing it as building "комплексную систему поддержки производителей" and enabling production of "новых типов вооружений, востребованных по всему миру." Концерн «Калашников» is Russia's flagship small-arms and precision-weapons manufacturer — this is the most senior state-defense-industrial partner named anywhere in this brief's Ushkuynik coverage, distinct from and larger than the Rostec trust-management link already documented at Атлант Аэро. Separately, Ushkuynik's own year-end 2023 summary (31.12.2023) names two further 2023-era partnership agreements not previously in this brief — АО «ПМЦ «Авангард»» (ИНН 7804302798, St Petersburg, airspace-monitoring/control-systems maker) and ООО «Зетакс» (ИНН 5300006066, Великий Новгород — press-confirmed FPV-drone developer/trainer, educational-drone activity) — and confirms АНО «Платформа НТИ» joining as a formal co-founder that same year, matching the founder-roster date already established via EGRUL. On 15.06.2025, Chadaev also reports the first demonstration flight of a heavy UAV built by АО «НПП «Радар ММС» (ИНН 7814027653, St Petersburg — a well-established Rostec-adjacent precision-guidance/avionics defence contractor, Ministry of Industry as its stated main customer) at Ushkuynik's own test range, timed to the Novgorod region's new "экспериментальный правовой режим" for heavy drones. Радар ММС and Зетакс are now EGRUL/press-confirmed as above; ПМЦ «Авангард» is EGRUL-matched (unique candidate, defence-industrial-process OKVED) but has no independent press corroboration yet, so treat its identification as probable rather than settled. Its own FY2021–22 БФО filings (pulled directly) show a real but modest engineering firm, not a large operation: revenue ₽1.543bn (2021, no FY2022 revenue disclosed — the FY2022 filing omits its Отчет о финансовых результатах entirely), net profit ₽14.8m (2021) and an implied ~₽29.0m (2022, backed into via the retained-earnings roll-forward since no P&L was filed), total balance growing from ₽2.339bn to ₽3.671bn, net assets ₽283.6m — thin single-digit margins on a modest capital base, with no state-secrets or defence-specific language in either filing. The Ushkuynik–Калашников joint venture itself remains unresolved as a distinct legal entity — no EGRUL match found under either party's name, and it may not be separately incorporated yet. Primary doc АО «ПМЦ «Авангард»»'s actual government-contracting record, mined from zakupki.gov.ru against its exact ИНН 7804302798, shows a real border-security-technology specialisation, not just the "airspace-monitoring/control-systems" self-description above. Its single largest contract — ₽2.969bn, dated 08.12.2025, still under execution and already amended four times — is with ФГКУ «Дирекция по строительству и эксплуатации объектов Росграницы» (Росгранстрой) for "техническое оснащение комплексами технических средств безопасности, связи и специального контроля" (security, communications and special-control equipment) at the «Хасан» road border crossing — Russia's border post with North Korea. The same customer also holds three further contracts with this network: two more with ПМЦ «Авангард» itself — ₽214.9m for the «Ивангород» crossing (Estonia border, Санкт-Петербургский филиал Росгранстроя) and ₽31.79m for the «Храброво» air border crossing at Kaliningrad, explicitly marked "в рамках ГОЗ" (under the state defence order) — and two with a separate, unrelated ЦБСТ-network entity, ООО «ЗИТ.ЦИ» (Отраслевой центр импортозамещения, ИНН 9726029133, wholly owned by ФГУП «ЗащитаИнфоТранс» of the Ministry of Transport): ₽227.5m plus a ₽131.35m follow-on for the «Верхний Ларс» crossing, Russia's border post with Georgia. One federal customer, four geographically distinct border crossings — Northeast Asia, the Baltic, the Kaliningrad exclave, the South Caucasus — split across two ЦБСТ-network suppliers with no direct corporate link to each other, reading as a genuine specialisation this segment of the network has with the Russian state rather than a coincidence of overlapping bids. Two further contracts, found in a follow-up review of ПМЦ «Авангард»'s remaining unwired zakupki cards, extend the pattern to a fifth border crossing and to two other federal border-security customers beyond Росгранстрой. A ₽129.5m contract (02.08.2017) with ФГУП «Росморпорт» — "выполнение работ по оснащению и обустройству морского пункта пропуска через государственную границу Российской Федерации" (outfitting/equipping a sea border checkpoint) — is this fifth crossing, this one maritime rather than road or air. Separately, three contracts totalling ₽36.08m (2011–2014) are with Пограничное управление ФСБ России по городу Санкт-Петербургу и Ленинградской области — the FSB's own regional border-guard directorate, procuring directly rather than through a civil-infrastructure intermediary like Росгранстрой; one card's own КТРУ classification code reads verbatim "услуги, связанные с охраной государственных границ" (services related to protecting state borders). Primary doc

The two channels' full scan also surfaces a separate, Ushkuynik-co-organised industry-conference track — "ExpoUAV" — that independently corroborates two of this brief's existing findings and adds several new names. A closed anti-drone-industry summit held at Ushkuynik's own Veliky Novgorod facility, 4–5 March 2025, was organised jointly by АНО «НПЦ «Ушкуйник»», «RuDrones. Беспилотные технологии» and the ExpoUAV platform, drawing 60+ representatives from 40+ organisations — Chadaev's own post lists them, including АО «НИИ «Вектор»» (ИНН 7813491943, ГК «Ростех») — a second, direct Rostec-subsidiary participant in this network alongside Атлант Аэро's trust-managed stake, confirmed via Rostec's own site as part of its Роселектроника holding — АО НПЦ «Элвис» (ИНН 7735582816, a long-established Zelenograd microelectronics/signal-processing firm, itself under active US/Japan/Canada/Switzerland/Ukraine sectoral sanctions), ООО «Тримикс» (3МХ) — explicit, independent corroboration from a source other than EGRUL's own contact field that Тримикс trades as 3MX/«Булат» — plus ООО «Элиарс», ООО «Лассард», АО «ФТК Интеграция», ООО «Скайгард», ООО «Хелиор», ООО «НПО «Кайсант»» (already resolved above), and — under its own standard government abbreviation — ГИУР МО РФ, i.e. the Ministry of Defence's Главное управление инновационного развития, the same directorate finding 18 above places under Осадчук. A related, earlier ExpoUAV conference (12.04.2024) names individuals for two of these companies for the first time in this brief: Тримикс's Director of Development, С.С. Шандобыло, and Элиарс's (ИНН 7735140825, Zelenograd) General Director, С.Н. Атливанчик — a name-to-EGRUL match independently confirmed, since Rusprofile's own record lists Атливанчик Сергей Николаевич as Элиарс's director — real, named people from an independent conference-programme source, standing in useful contrast to Тримикс's own EGRUL record, where both GD and founder are access-restricted. Separately, Chadaev's channel independently corroborates, via Interfax (20.05.2025), the exact EU sanctions round already dated in this brief for Ushkuynik ("EU-sanctioned May 2025") — and names two further sanctioned network entities in the same report: ООО «Беспилотные Системы» (not yet identified or EGRUL-checked) and ООО «Центр Комплексных Беспилотных Решений» (ЦКБР) itself, sanctioned as a company — this brief had previously only recorded ЦКБР's chief designer, Кузякин, as individually sanctioned; the company was evidently listed too. Primary doc

АО НПЦ «Элвис»'s zakupki.gov.ru contracting record — mined against ИНН 7735582816 — makes it this brief's single largest state-defence-R&D counterparty by contract value, and adds this entity's first-ever director_history entry. Eleven confirmed ОКР (opytno-konstruktorskaya rabota — applied R&D) contracts with the Ministry of Industry and Trade (Минпромторг), plus a 17-contract aggregate cluster (2013–2015, ₽3.75bn combined) found among previously-archived but unwired cards, total roughly ₽14.5bn — more than any other resident in this brief's zakupki coverage, entity or individual. The single largest is ₽5.59bn (11.12.2017) for шифр «Базис-Б3», a digital image/signal-processing microprocessor programme; the most recent, ₽5.59bn (12.03.2024, шифр «Процессор-ИМА-БК»), is R&D for a heterogeneous processor for the "integrated modular avionics of combat systems" — combat-avionics chip design, not general-purpose electronics. Other named programmes span radiation-hardened microchips for spacecraft onboard computers (шифр «Обработка-И1»), a multichip module explicitly for "identification and control of unmanned aerial vehicles" (шифр «Сложность-БПЛА»), and a combined GLONASS/GPS/Galileo/BeiDou navigation receiver SoC (шифр «Веста-У») — a UAV-identification and satellite-navigation R&D portfolio consistent with this brief's broader drone-industry focus, not incidental defence-adjacent work. The ₽5.59bn «Процессор-ИМА-БК» contract (reg 1770559633924000207) also supplies this entity's first-ever director_history entry: Семилетов Антон Дмитриевич, signing "действующего на основании Устава" as Генеральный директор, 03.12.2024 — the company's leadership had no primary-source citation anywhere in this brief before this contract card. Primary doc

A second, previously undocumented member of the Rogozin family surfaces in an early (24.01.2023) @dronnitsa repost — a critical guest opinion piece, not the channel's own editorial voice, arguing that Russia's aircraft-grade certification requirements for heavy civilian drones (₽100m+ for a 30kg+ airframe) protect state-linked incumbents at independent developers' expense. The piece names Алексей Рогозин — son of Dmitry Rogozin, already documented in this brief heading a "Барс-Сармат" drone-testing detachment at Дронница — as director of АНО "Центр развития транспортных технологий" (ИНН 9724066980, Moscow) and, separately, chairman of the advisory council of АО «Эколибри» (ИНН 7714473012, Moscow), a private aviation-engineering firm reported (Nov. 2022) to be developing Russia's first electric VTOL aircraft. Both roles are now independently press-confirmed: corporate-registry press coverage names Алексей Дмитриевич Рогозин directly as the АНО's head, and Vedomosti reports him as chairman of Эколибри's advisory council specifically, discussing drone-regulation policy — matching this critical source's framing exactly. Эколибри's own FY2021–25 БФО filings (pulled directly, FY2025 including explanatory notes) update and correct this picture: Rogozin's own current role, per the FY2025 notes' own governance disclosure, is a seat on the Board of Directors (Совет директоров), not merely an advisory-council chairmanship — alongside GD Павлова Татьяна Викторовна, Виталий Александрович Жук (a name matching this brief's already-documented Котлин-Новатор GD/chief designer — flagged as a possible cross-network connection, not independently confirmed as the same person), Алексей Николаевич Басов and Сергей Васильевич Исаенко. Ownership, per the FY2025 shareholder table, is majority-held by GD Павлова herself (52.4%), with ООО «ДЕДАЛ» (13.1%), ООО «АИТК» (12.5%) and Жук (12.5%) as the other major holders; Rogozin holds no disclosed equity — his only financial position in the filing is as a small long-term lender (₽302k at 6.5%, repayable on demand). The company is mid-buildout on a scale far exceeding its own revenue: total balance grew from ₽31.6m (2021) to ₽1.582bn (2025), driven by ₽1.24bn in "незавершенное производство" (work-in-progress inventory) against revenue of just ₽21.3m (2025, essentially flat on 2024's ₽10.9m) — the same "sudden capitalization without matching revenue" signature already documented elsewhere in this network (Ушкуйник, Морских Программ). Of the financing behind this, ₽130.0m (2025, up from ₽20.3m in 2024) is explicitly disclosed as recognised государственная помощь (state assistance); a further ₽115.3m sits on the balance sheet as deferred state-financing income. But the single largest liability — ₽1.272bn booked as "целевое финансирование" (targeted financing, счет 86) — has no counterparty or source named anywhere in the notes reviewed, well beyond what the state-assistance disclosure accounts for; treat its origin as unresolved, not assumed to be government money just because it sits in the same account category. Separately, the notes disclose a real operating group structure: Эколибри owns 51% of АО «МИКЦ» (ИНН 7714486438) and transacts heavily with ООО «Эколибри-БТ» (ИНН 9713001941, drone-production OKVED, already flagged in this brief's EGRUL sweep as a plausible related entity — now confirmed as one via this filing's own related-party note) — together, ₽434.3m in FY2025 intercompany purchases (₽282.7m from МИКЦ, ₽151.6m from Эколибри-БТ), consistent with Эколибри functioning as the design/holding layer of a small vertically-integrated drone group rather than a standalone R&D shop. Primary doc

ГК «Логос», trading via рэб.рус (xn--90a3a1b.xn--p1acf) and the email domain ewarfare.ru, is a ЦБСТ-resident electronic-warfare manufacturer selling drone detectors, vehicle-mounted and portable jammers, fixed-site anti-drone systems, and a collision-interceptor product called «Дрон-Таран» (~100km/h ramming speed). Its site states residency at both ЦБСТ and "НПЦ БАС," and claims "сотни изделий обеспечивают защиту" in the SVO zone. ЦБСТ's own channel independently confirms and dates this: a 04.07.2025 post reports that Фонд «Наша Правда» — this brief's central foundation — together with United Russia's Vladimir Yakushev (already documented on ЦБСТ's supervisory board) delivered RЭБ kits called «Дикобраз» ("Porcupine"), "разработанные группой компаний «Логос» — резидентом ЦБСТ," to war correspondents from ВГТРК, Первый канал, «Звезда» and НТВ and to troops of the "Центр" grouping on 3 July 2025, with "more than 400 units already in combat use" per the manufacturer. This is a direct, dated instance of Наша Правда itself distributing a named ЦБСТ-resident's hardware — not just sharing an office or a board seat with the network, but physically handling equipment delivery. The legal entity, confirmed directly via EGRUL (ИНН 3435149330), is ООО «Группа компаний «Логос»» — ОГРН 1253400004527, registered 16.06.2025 in Волжский, Волгоградская область, just under three weeks before the 3 July 2025 «Дикобраз» handover reported above. 100% owner: Гарькуша Владимир Владимирович. GD: Бурлака Павел Анатольевич, appointed 29.10.2025 — four months after founding, a separate person from the founder. Charter capital ₽100,000. Its registered OKVED is "Торговля оптовая химическими продуктами" (wholesale trade in chemical products) — a purpose/activity mismatch consistent with the pattern already documented elsewhere in this network (Кольская верфь, Боевые технологии, Винград). Its first-year БФО filing (pulled directly, covering roughly June–December 2025) shows a genuinely substantial operating business, not a shell: ₽112.26m in revenue against ₽114.31m in costs, a small net loss of -₽2.23m, ₽181.17m total balance, ₽120.58m in receivables. Real revenue at this scale in a six-and-a-half-month first year is consistent with the manufacturer's own "400+ units already in combat use" claim reported above. The filing's address — ул. 7-я Автодорога, д.27б, офис 2 — differs in street name from the EGRUL address (ул. им. Н.Д. Лопатникова, д.27Б, оф.2) despite matching building/office number exactly; likely a street renaming or dual-addressing quirk in Волжский rather than two locations, but not independently confirmed. ГК «Логос»'s founding charter and its one amendment have now been pulled directly (nalog.gov.ru, user-supplied, 19.08.2026), upgrading this entity's ownership finding to a primary document: the original charter (dated by "Решение №1" on 12.05.2025 — about five weeks before the 16.06.2025 EGRUL registration date already noted) confirms the ₽100,000 charter capital directly and, like every other founding document reviewed in this network, contains no drone/EW-specific purpose language whatsoever — a fully generic ООО charter. The amendment (decided 20.10.2025, recorded in EGRUL 29.10.2025 — the exact same date already documented for Бурлака's GD appointment) turns out to be a governance restructuring, not a cosmetic change: it rewrites the charter's single-director clause to formally define a "Генеральный директор" role (5-year term, full signing/banking/personnel authority, subordinate to the general meeting of participants) — meaning the original May 2025 charter evidently didn't have a fully specified GD structure, and Гарькуша had to amend the charter before he could formally install Бурлака as a distinct director four months after founding. The amendment is personally signed «/Гарькуша В.В./» as sole participant — a direct, primary-source signature match confirming his identity on the company's own paperwork, not just an aggregator listing. Primary doc

A full sweep of рэб.рус (19.08.2026, every real subpage — the site is a single-page Tilda build; "О компании"/"Контакты" are on-page anchors, not separate URLs) confirms no persons of interest are disclosed on the site itself — a direct entity/name-pattern scan of the raw page HTML found zero named individuals anywhere, consistent with the access-restriction/anonymity pattern already documented across this network. The catalog page adds only generic category groupings (Обнаружение, Подавление, Комплексы, Комплектующие) with no individual product names beyond «Дрон-Таран» already covered above — no new products found. One new lead surfaced: the company's own Telegram channel, @Logosrus ("РЭБ РУС," bio: "Группа Логос, производим комплексы РЭБ для защиты от БПЛА"), linked from the site but not previously catalogued in this brief — its static preview is privacy-gated (no public post history via t.me/s/Logosrus), so establishing persons of interest beyond the EGRUL-confirmed founder/GD already documented (Гарькуша Владимир Владимирович, Бурлака Павел Анатольевич) requires this project's own authenticated scrape rather than open-web access; added to config/channels.txt for the next scan — checked directly and found empty (no public posts), a dead end for now. Field check

Two priced, certified product pages exist on рэб.рус that aren't linked from the main navigation (found via search indexing, not the site's own menu) — real, in-stock listings rather than generic catalog copy. «Дикобраз М6» (auto-mounted, the same product line ЦБСТ credited with 400+ combat units above): ₽650,000 ex-VAT, 10 units in stock, 6-channel 400–7200MHz jamming, 50W/channel, ~500m video-jamming radius, 390L auto-box housing, 60-minute battery runtime, 12-month warranty — and explicitly marked "Проверено НИИИ РЭБ" ("verified by NIIIRЭБ," a real Russian Ministry of Defence electronic-warfare research institute) — a concrete, checkable government-testing claim not present anywhere else on the site. «ЛОГОС С8» (stationary, the entity's own namesake product): ₽2,900,000, 1 unit in stock, 200–8800MHz detection/jamming across combined modules, >1000m jamming / 3000m detection radius, IP66-rated, continuous 24/7 operation, holding a ГКРЧ frequency-authorization decision (Russia's State Radio Frequency Commission) and registered in Russia's software/computing-device registry — infrastructure-grade certification distinct from the field-improvised profile of some other network entities. A second company tied to the same founder resolves the brief's earlier OKVED/address anomaly: Гарькуша Владимир Владимирович is also director of ООО «Промсинтез» (ИНН 3435084957, ОГРН 1073435002013, registered 20.03.2007 — pre-dating «Логос» by 18 years), a wholesale chemical-products business (OKVED 46.75 — the exact same code registered for ГК «Логос»), based at ул. 7-я Автодорога, д.27 — the same street this brief's own БФО-filing pull already flagged as differing from ГК «Логос»'s EGRUL address (ул. им. Н.Д. Лопатникова, д.27Б). This resolves both anomalies at once, plausibly: Гарькуша appears to have reused his existing, decades-old chemical-trading company's registration template (OKVED code and office address) when founding «Логос» in 2025, rather than registering a purpose-specific EW-manufacturer entry — an administrative-convenience explanation, not evidence of concealment, though it doesn't rule that out either. Secondary source

ООО «Смарт Текнолоджис» (ИНН 7715934509, ОГРН 1127746712752, originally registered 2012) is a ЦБСТ resident named directly by the channel's 25.02.2025 post — "резидент ЦБСТ... компания ООО «Смарт Текнолоджис» из Пензы" — as developer of «Метафора», an inter-network radio relay that unifies incompatible military and civilian radio systems (including the widely-used «Азарт» military radio) for encrypted voice/text over any available channel (USB/SIM modems, Ethernet, Wi-Fi), with automatic channel-switching for jam resistance. ЦБСТ's post states field/combat testing has confirmed reliability; Izvestia independently corroborated the device and developer in its own coverage (iz.ru) — a rare instance in this brief of a ЦБСТ-resident claim getting independent mainstream-press confirmation, not just the platform's own channel. Corporately, the company is registered at Kievskoe Shosse km 22, влд.6 стр.1, Moscow — inside the Comcity («КомСити») office park (430,000m² of office space, built 2014, no production floors) — (despite the "из Пензы" framing — likely reflecting where it actually operates versus where it's legally domiciled) and is 100% owned by АО «УК СМАРТ» (ИНН 7751264401, a non-public JSC registered 22.08.2023 at the same address, shareholder register held by its own registrar and not disclosed in EGRUL, as is standard for an АО). GD Кот Олег Петрович, appointed 11.11.2025. Charter capital ₽5.5m. Its own FY2021–25 БФО filings (pulled directly) show this is a substantial company, not a small IT consultancy: revenue of ₽4.44bn (2022), ₽2.81bn (2023), ₽2.92bn (2024) and ₽3.93bn (2025), net profit ₽475.3m (2023), ₽33.8m (2024), ₽222.0m (2025), balance consistently ₽3.9–5.1bn across the period, and a workforce of 166 employees (2025, up from 155 in 2023) — a systems-integrator/reseller revenue-and-margin profile (thin single-digit margins on billions in throughput), not a boutique consultancy. Its FY2025 related-party note names the ultimate beneficial owners as Ефремов Антон Вениаминович (50%) and Кот Олег Петрович (50%), controlling indirectly through АО «УК СМАРТ»; GD at the time of that filing was Гончаренко Антон Александрович — a different person from Кот, who EGRUL shows becoming GD himself only later, 11.11.2025. The same note discloses «Смарт Текнолоджис» acting as an internal group treasury: a ₽1.3934bn long-term receivable from its own 100% parent АО «УК СМАРТ» (due 31.12.2028), plus large intercompany flows with unnamed "дочерние общества" (subsidiary companies) — ₽97m in loans received and ₽100m repaid in 2025, ₽200m in dividends receivable from subsidiaries in 2025 (₽150m in 2024), and in 2024 alone ₽176.37m lent to subsidiaries, ₽216.57m in loan repayments received back, and ₽146.378m in sales to unnamed "other related parties." No subsidiary or related-party names are given anywhere in the filing. «УК СМАРТ»'s own FY2023–25 БФО filings (pulled directly) resolve most of the ambiguity above and reveal a wider sibling-company group. The ₽1.3934bn long-term liability on the parent's own balance sheet is confirmed as the mirror image of Смарт Текнолоджис's receivable — the parent's ₽1.384bn "Финансовые вложения" (financial investments) line is explicitly identified in its own notes as "вклад в уставный капитал ООО «Смарт Текнолоджис»" (the equity stake in Смарт Текнолоджис itself), meaning УК СМАРТ's acquisition of its own 100%-owned operating subsidiary was financed by a long-term loan borrowed back from that same subsidiary — a debt-financed, buyout-style structure in which the acquired company funds its own acquisition. УК СМАРТ's FY2025 related-party note names a cluster of sibling companies under the same ultimate control (Ефремов А.В. / Кот О.П., 50/50) beyond Смарт Текнолоджис itself: ООО «Цифровая платформа», ООО «Лабиринт», ООО «СТ СОФТ» (the only one with an actual disclosed transaction — service sales), ООО «ЦФА Информационная система» and ООО «ЦФА система» (both apparently digital-financial-assets/tokenised-securities platforms — a business line distinct from military communications), and СНТ «Усадьба Бергов» (a garden/dacha partnership sharing the same director as УК СМАРТ). None of these sibling entities have been pulled or otherwise investigated yet — this broadens the known corporate footprint of the Ефремов/Кот ownership group well beyond the «Метафора» radio-relay product, into a diversified holding spanning IT, digital-asset infrastructure and unrelated personal/leisure entities. Primary doc

«КИНГПРО», named as a resident maker of the «Секира Призрак» product, is ООО «КИНГПРО» (ИНН 9725168367, ОГРН 1247700607450) — registered 10.09.2024, OKVED 30.30.6 "производство беспилотных авиационных систем." Its founding charter (pulled directly from ФНС, a single boilerplate document with no state-secrets or defence-specific clauses, ₽100,000 charter capital) shows a family-controlled structure: sole founder (100%) Королев Алексей Александрович, General Director Королев Дмитрий Алексеевич — same surname, patronymics consistent with a close family relationship rather than an arm's-length appointment. Its secondary OKVED list is unusually broad for a week-old shell — integrated circuits, radio/TV transmitting equipment, space-vehicle production, wholesale computer/software trade — a kitchen-sink registration pattern seen elsewhere in this network on companies positioned to bid across multiple procurement categories at once. Its registered address — Kievskoe Shosse km 22, д.4 стр.1, Moscow — sits inside «Бизнес-парк «Румянцево»», a mixed-purpose complex that, unlike the pure-office Comcity park next door, includes actual production floors — and is independently confirmed as a real operational site, not a mailbox: an archived hh.ru vacancy for "Заместитель главного конструктора (БПЛА)" (Deputy Chief Designer, UAV), on-site, full-time, is posted under this same legal entity. «Смарт Текнолоджис» (documented above) sits one plot over, in the neighbouring Comcity office park (владение 6 стр.1 vs. КИНГПРО's дом 4 стр.1 — two distinct, adjacent developments, not the same complex) — a pairing that tracks the two companies' own profiles: КИНГПРО, the newer drone-OKVED entity with a confirmed on-site engineering vacancy, sits in the complex actually capable of production; Смарт Текнолоджис, which this brief's own БФО pull characterises as a systems-integrator/treasury operation rather than a manufacturer, sits in the office-only park. This is the first instance in this brief of two independently-resolved ЦБСТ-network entities sharing a physical cluster beyond a single shared office building already flagged elsewhere (Нарышкинская аллея, д.5) — worth tracking as a pattern going forward: registered addresses in this network are usually administrative, but production/design addresses, where confirmable via job postings, press, or (as here) the actual character of the building complex, are a separate and more meaningful signal of real operational overlap. Its own FY2024–25 БФО filings (pulled directly, FY2025 with explanatory notes) confirm КИНГПРО is still pre-revenue: zero revenue disclosed in either year, total balance tiny but growing (₽637k → ₽4.352m), financed by small long-term loans (₽535k → ₽3.376m) consistent in scale with founder-level, not institutional, funding. The FY2025 notes state this directly, in the company's own words: "Компания пока не начала свою деятельность, но проводит закупку ОС, заключает договора аренды" ("The company has not yet begun operations, but is purchasing fixed assets and concluding lease agreements") — a new ₽876k lease obligation appears on the FY2025 balance sheet for the first time, consistent with and corroborating the hh.ru vacancy finding above. The notes' own related-party section names Королев А.А. as sole beneficiary, matching the founding-charter finding exactly. In short: a real, still-ramping engineering start-up, not a dormant shell — but one with no output yet to independently verify. Primary doc

НПЦ БАСиРТК — a case study in tying a resident's channel, legal identity, financials, physical facility and personnel evidence together end to end. Its own channel, @UAS_development, is the entity's primary voice, not a third party — full scan 21.08.2024–17.08.2026 (415 posts). It is ООО «НПЦ БАСИРТК» (full name ООО «Научно-производственный центр беспилотных авиационных систем и робототехнических комплексов», ИНН 3300009049, ОГРН 1243300000789, registered 31.01.2024) — sole owner/GD Байгутлина Ирина Анатольевна (100%, ₽500k charter capital). Its OKVED list is unusually explicit for this network: alongside the standard 72.19 R&D primary code sit 25.40 "Производство оружия и боеприпасов" (weapons/ammunition manufacturing) and 84.22 "Деятельность, связанная с обеспечением военной безопасности" — most network entities register under vaguer software/electronics codes and leave the weapons link implicit; this one states it directly. RMSP status: микропредприятие, 2 employees (average headcount, prior year) — a company this small should not, on paper, carry meaningful revenue. Its own FY2024–25 БФО filings (pulled directly) show exactly the "sudden capitalization" pattern already documented elsewhere in this network: FY2024 revenue ₽11k, net loss ₽181k (pre-operational); FY2025 revenue ₽83.7m — a roughly 7,600× jump — net profit ₽2.688m, balance sheet growing from ₽344k to ₽72.645m on the back of ₽54.0m in receivables and ₽70.1m in payables. No related-party disclosure is made — the FY2025 notes explicitly invoke the small-business exemption from ПБУ 11/2008 — so the counterparties behind that revenue are not named anywhere in the filing, the same non-disclosure pattern already seen at Kaysant and Визионеро.

Physical facility, independently confirmed to exact coordinates: the channel's own 04.06.2026 post states the company became a resident of Промышленный технопарк «Муроммаш» (tp-mm.ru), created under Russia's electronics-industry development strategy through 2030; the technopark's own site confirms its managing company as ООО «ПРОМТЕХНОПАРК «МУРОММАШ»» (ОГРН 1043302407169, a pre-existing operator, not a shell stood up for this purpose) and gives the park's scale as 2.76 hectares, ~18,380m², 250 workstations — though its own current resident list (three named tenants, none drone-related) has not yet been updated to reflect the June 2026 residency, consistent with the pattern seen elsewhere of ЦБСТ-network residencies announced via Telegram well before appearing on any official site. This matches a formal EGRUL address change filed just weeks before this pull: Владимирская область, г.о. округ Муром, г. Муром, Ш. Карачаровское, д. 5, entered into the register 22.07.2026 — meaning the legal registration itself was updated to catch up with the physical move. Satellite imagery at 55.5606614732459, 42.05605953940559 (dated 2026) shows a large, recently-built industrial building with visible roof ventilation/skylight units and active construction debris nearby — consistent with a facility too new to have street-level imagery yet, but independently confirming this is a real, substantial production site rather than an administrative address. Primary doc

Direct front-line supply evidence and personnel identification. The channel's own Aug–Sep 2024 posts document a low-cost, largely field-assembled FPV drone, «Ковровец» (cardboard airframe, off-the-shelf motors), supplied directly to the 106th Guards Airborne Division (Tula) — including handing over raw components for troops to self-manufacture in the field, with a stated local-production arrangement through the Tula Suvorov Military School. A later post (16.10.2024) documents participation in a targeting demo for «Ступор», a branded counter-drone RF system already named in this brief's TASS-sourced Kursk air-defence material — the channel's own photo shows the physical «stupor»-branded hardware directly. Separately, a 29.06.2026 post with attached photo shows the company's технический директор — главный конструктор, Замятин Александр Юрьевич, receiving a formal «Благодарность» (letter of thanks) dated 22.06.2026, signed by генерал-лейтенант А.Киндяков, titled "Заместитель начальника Главного управления Генерального штаба Вооружённых Сил Российской Федерации по военно-политической работе" — i.e. a deputy head of ГУ ГШ (GRU), for military-political work. Independent verification, via a VK post from the group «Кубанский казачий хор» (vk.ru/wall-851904_25517), confirms the signatory's full name as Андрей Андреевич Киндяков, генерал-лейтенант, заместитель начальника ГРУ ГШ ВС РФ, and includes a photograph of him speaking at a concert — a verbatim quote from that post is preserved below for its own value as characteristic public rhetoric from a senior GRU figure, not because it bears on НПЦ БАСиРТК directly. Primary doc

Сегодня на концерте прозвучали великие слова: «Встань за веру, Русская земля!». Пришло то время, когда мы должны осознать, что происходит... Нас хотят разделить, разлучить, разодрать, посеять сомненья в вере в наше верховное командование и нанести стратегическое поражение России. Но это не удастся, поскольку есть на земле традиции... Я хочу сказать, что сила песни, сила слова — это наше секретное оружие!... здесь стоят люди, которые за дедов, за хаты, за родимый край, за деток малых, хлеба каравай бьются на своем участке фронта, как и наши ребята... И мы обязательно Победим! За Победу, за нашу Победу!Андрей Андреевич Киндяков, генерал-лейтенант, заместитель начальника ГРУ ГШ ВС РФ — «Кубанский казачий хор» VK post — verbatim

The channel's #сво tag is also, unusually for this network, a direct unit-identification vector. НПЦ БАСиРТК's operator posts scanned copies of formal military «Благодарственное письмо» (thank-you letters) under this tag, each carrying an official MoD seal and a named, ranked signatory — a level of detail most delivery-confirmation posts in this brief's other sources withhold. Two examples, 16 months apart, name the same unit and commander: a letter to Замятин А.Ю. (01.12.2024) and a second to Сухоненкова Надежда Андреевна, the centre's Административный директор (05.04.2026), both addressed from войсковая часть — полевая почта 71474, «Добровольческий корпус», signed by its commander, генерал-майор Д. Барило. The 16-month gap between two independently-named recipients confirms a sustained supply relationship, not a single delivery. The commander is independently identifiable: Барило Денис Александрович, генерал-майор, commander of the Russian MoD's «Добровольческий корпус» (also referred to in some coverage as the "Князь Владимир" corps) — assigned general-major rank by presidential decree 06.06.2023, previously commander of the 55th separate motor-rifle (mountain) brigade and deputy commander of the 2nd Combined Arms Army, Central Military District — documented independently across multiple accountability-tracking sources (komandiry.org, war-proekt.media). Primary doc

АО «НИИ «Вектор»» (ИНН 7813491943, ОГРН 1117847020400, St Petersburg) surfaces in this brief first as a delegation attendee — Chadaev's own post on a 60+-representative, 40+-organisation industry gathering co-hosted by АНО «НПЦ «Ушкуйник»» names it directly — and turns out to be a direct Rostec/Роселектроника subsidiary, confirmed via Rostec's own site, a second such direct state-holding-company link in this brief alongside Атлант Аэро's trust-managed stake. It is sanctioned across nine jurisdictions (Ukraine, Australia, Switzerland, the US, UK, EU, New Zealand — OFAC, EU asset-freeze registry and GUR's own designation among them), confirmed via an exact-ИНН OpenSanctions match, not a name-only fuzzy hit. A full-roster patent sweep of searchplatform.rospatent.gov.ru found 20 patents filed under the institute's own name, read in full rather than taken on title alone — the two most direct: a "беспилотный летательный аппарат-перехватчик" (unmanned aerial interceptor, RU2741142C2, filed 2020, and a second-generation version RU2860540C1, filed 2025) and a "способ радиопротиводействия беспилотному воздушному судну" (a method of radio countermeasures against a UAV, RU2839399C1, 2024) — plus a further anti-UAV-countermeasures patent (RU2858409C1, 2025) and a cluster of radio direction-finding/пеленгация patents spanning 2020–2026, evidence of sustained, current-generation counter-drone R&D rather than a one-off filing. Two of the institute's own patent descriptions name specific off-the-shelf foreign parts as their own reference implementation, not prior-art citations: RU2861850C1 (a swarm-UAV radio-direction-finding method) lists a u-blox M10 GPS module (Switzerland), a Pixhawk 4 flight controller, a Raspberry Pi 5 (UK), a Semtech-chipset LoRa transceiver (US) and a Multicomp-brand antenna (UK) as the devices its own design is built from; RU2763583C2 separately names a Texas Instruments ADC (US) as suited to its patented sub-sampling receiver. One open flag worth tracking: the institute's own EGRUL snapshot records it as "в процессе реорганизации" (undergoing reorganisation) since 14.05.2026 — current operating status not independently re-verified since. Its zakupki.gov.ru record adds a defence-prime customer relationship distinct from its own state-owned status: a ₽54.9m contract (28.03.2022) with АО «Концерн «Моринформсистема-Агат»» — Russia's principal naval C4I/combat-systems integrator — for an "аппаратно-программный комплекс, моделирующий функционирование автоматизированного канала радиотехнической разведки" (a hardware/software complex simulating an automated radio-technical reconnaissance, i.e. SIGINT, channel), found in a follow-up review of this entity's remaining unwired zakupki cards. Primary doc

АО «НПЦ «Электронные вычислительно-информационные системы»» (ЭЛВИС, ИНН 7735582816, ОГРН 1127746073510, Zelenograd) is the same delegation post's other direct Rostec/Роселектроника subsidiary named alongside НИИ «Вектор» (see the ExpoUAV finding above, which already independently confirmed this entity's sanctioned status) — a long-established fabless microelectronics/signal-processing design house (main ОКВЭД: integrated-circuit manufacturing). An exact-ИНН OpenSanctions match against designation records captioned "ELVEES R AND D Center JSC" independently confirms the sanction — the underlying primary designation documents haven't yet been individually read, so the exact jurisdiction list isn't restated here beyond what's already established above. Its own trademark, filed 2019, is on file (reg. 732875). The same full-roster patent sweep found 20 patents under its own name, spanning both chip-level IP (FFT processors, Reed-Solomon coders, GigaSpaceWire interfaces, 5G polar-code encoders — the signature of a real semiconductor design house, not a shell) and two directly on-topic counter-drone filings: RU117356S, a design patent for a "пушка для захвата беспилотных летательных аппаратов" (a capture gun for seizing UAVs, filed 2018) and RU2746394C1, a "система и способ детектирования беспилотных летательных аппаратов (БПЛА)" (a UAV detection system and method, filed 2020) — both read in full and matched by exact patentee name, not title alone. Primary doc

ООО «КРАВТ» (kravt-studio.ru, ИНН 7802693899, ОГРН 1197847166560, registered 14.08.2019 in Saint Petersburg) is a ЦБСТ resident confirmed directly by the channel's own 11.04.2026 post — "Резидент ЦБСТ запустил производство складного FPV-дрона с донаведением. Петербургская компания «Кравт» наладила выпуск квадрокоптера «Матрёшка»" — describing a folding-arm FPV drone with target-acquisition guidance, stored/transported in smart-charging tubular containers (up to 29 drones per shipping pallet), explicitly framed around combat readiness: "боец способен в короткий срок привести беспилотник в боеготовое состояние" ("the fighter can quickly bring the drone to combat-ready condition"). The post also compares «Матрёшка» to a competing US product (Vector's «Hammer F1»), a framing pattern (domestic ЦБСТ resident vs. named Western competitor) seen elsewhere in this platform's self-promotion. Corporately, КРАВТ is 100% owned by its founder, Королев Дмитрий Михайлович, personally (sole founder since registration, GD in place since founding) — not a subsidiary of anything. КРАВТ itself in turn owns two subsidiaries: 90% of ООО «КРАВТ УДМУРТИЯ» (ИНН 1800023369, registered 09.09.2024 in Glazov, Удмуртская Республика — electronics-component manufacturing, OKVED 26.11 — the remaining 10% held personally by Королев), and 100% of ООО «СПДББК» (ИНН 7838044584). КРАВТ УДМУРТИЯ appears to be a newly-stood-up satellite production site: its own EGRUL filing shows zero declared employees, zero revenue and zero expenses as of its most recent reporting date (01.01.2026) — consistent with a facility still ramping up rather than yet contributing output. Its own FY2022–25 БФО filings (pulled directly, units confirmed as Тыс. руб.) show a steadily compounding manufacturer: revenue of ₽69.0m (2022), ₽130.7m (2023), ₽172.8m (2024) and ₽300.6m (2025) — roughly doubling year over year, the same growth curve seen elsewhere in this network's manufacturer tier (Азимут, ЗИТ.ЦИ) — with net profit positive throughout (₽6.7m, ₽29.2m, ₽3.5m, ₽9.0m respectively) and a balance sheet growing from ₽37.1m (2022) to ₽437.6m (2025). One filing anomaly worth flagging: the FY2024 balance sheet jumps from ₽111.4m to ₽403.2m largely on the back of a ₽246.9m "Переоценка НМА" (intangible-asset revaluation) with no corresponding cash movement — a paper markup of intangible assets, not a real capital inflow; by FY2025 the company also shows ₽43.0m placed in bank deposits and ₽13.5m in loans issued to third parties, consistent with genuine accumulated profitability rather than balance-sheet engineering. СПДББК (the other, wholly-owned subsidiary) has not yet been investigated. КРАВТ itself is now confirmed sanctioned — an exact-ИНН OpenSanctions match (regime not returned by the API for this record). A full-roster patent sweep under КРАВТ's own name turns up not drone hardware but 20 patents for chlorine-dosing and water-treatment equipment (ХЛОРАТОР devices, modular water-purification stations, swimming-pool chlorine analysers) dating back to the 2000s — confirming this is a real, long-established industrial-chemical-equipment manufacturer that diversified into FPV drone production, not a wartime shell built around one product; by contrast, its subsidiary КРАВТ УДМУРТИЯ holds a 2025 patent for a UAV-specific power-supply system (RU2858174C1, co-inventor Королев Д.М., the same person as КРАВТ's own owner/GD) — the drone-specific IP sits in the satellite production entity, the water-treatment IP in the parent. Primary doc

АО «Стратим» (ИНН 7720901379, ОГРН 1237700447324, registered 03.07.2023 in Moscow) is one of the most extensively documented ЦБСТ residents in this brief's source material — a strike-drone manufacturer with a named product line spanning autonomous-targeting kamikaze drones («Русак-С», computer-vision-based terminal guidance), a revolver-magazine bomber quadcopter («Голубь», 12× VOG-17 grenades or one 82mm mortar round, combat-tested "hovering helplessly over an enemy EW system"), a compact "Герань"-style loitering munition («Тювик»), signal-relay drones («Пересмешник»), and a reconnaissance/artillery-spotting quadcopter («Воробей») — the latter two independently corroborated by an Izvestia investigative series (per ЦБСТ's own channel, April–June 2025). ЦБСТ's channel also reports Стратим instructors running a four-day frontline training course for troops directly at a Zaporizhzhia-direction position (09.04.2025) and Стратим's leadership receiving a Минпромторг award alongside ЦБСТ chairman Безруков and ЦБСТ GD Рубинштейн (14.08.2025) — placing the company inside the platform's own leadership-recognition circle, not just its resident roster. Its FY2025 БФО notes (pulled directly, mandatorily audited by ООО «ФинСоюзАудит») disclose, for the first time in this brief, a named ultimate-beneficial-ownership structure for a flagship ЦБСТ resident: of 15,000 total shares, АО «Хоук Холдинг» holds 51% (100% ultimately owned by Ястребов Сергей Владимирович), former GD Бежанов Иван Валериевич holds 37.2% directly, АО «АИН» holds 6%, technical director Махнёв Александр Игоревич holds 4.5%, and Назаров Олег Рамилевич holds 1.3%; the notes name all four — Ястребов, Бежанов, Махнёв, Назаров — explicitly as "конечные бенефициары" (ultimate beneficiaries). Current GD Костюнин Павел Андреевич (since 03.05.2024) holds no disclosed equity but is also GD of a related entity, АО «Стратим-Тех», which transacted ₽1.83m in purchases with АО «Стратим» in 2025. A separate, similarly-named ООО «Стратим» (ИНН 5260494520, 25% owned by АО «Стратим» itself, 75% by Костюнин personally) entered liquidation 02.03.2026. The company banks with ПАО «Банк ПСБ» (Промсвязьбанк) — the same defense-sector bank ЦБСТ's own channel reports forming a dedicated dual-use-project investment fund with (24.06.2025 post) — consistent with, though not proof of, that fund being a financing channel. Its own FY2023–25 БФО filings (units confirmed Тыс. руб.) show explosive, audited growth from a standing start: revenue of ₽1.0k (2023, effectively pre-revenue), ₽27.8m (2024) and ₽606.8m (2025), with staff headcount growing from 4 (2024) to 199 employees (2025); net profit was thin relative to scale — ₽2.7m (2024), ₽6.8m (2025) — a margin profile consistent with early-stage manufacturing scale-up rather than steady-state profitability. Net assets actually declined from ₽10.77m (end-2024) to ₽8.08m (end-2025) despite the revenue surge, a detail not explained in the notes. One filing inconsistency worth flagging, the same pattern seen with ОПКБ «Экспломет» above: the company's own FY2024 filing reported FY2024 revenue of ₽27.76m, but the subsequent FY2025 filing restates that same prior-year figure down to ₽12.3m, with no explanation given in either filing. Industry press outside the ЦБСТ ecosystem independently corroborates the broader trend Стратим sits in: an RT piece (11.03.2025) reports Russia-wide FPV-drone production scaling up specifically around autopilot/machine-vision terminal guidance in 2025, naming drones «Микроб», «Овод-С» (using a guidance system called «Площадь» — name-identical to, but not confirmed as the same product as, ЦБСТ resident АО «Площадь»'s targeting platform documented above), «Сорока», and a «Воробей» slated to gain autopilot in 2025 — the last two names overlapping with Стратим's own «Пересмешник»/«Воробей» line and worth flagging as a possible naming collision or genuine cross-reference, not yet resolved either way; military correspondent Алексей Кожевников is quoted: «Самонаведение пошло "в тираж" с прошлого года. Прежде всего автопилот необходим для результативных ударов по военной технике» — Алексей Кожевников, RT, 11.03.2025 — verbatim Primary doc

АО «НПП БАТ» (Научно-производственное предприятие «Беспилотные авиационные технологии», ИНН 9718238013, ОГРН 1237700769701, registered 10.11.2023 in Moscow) is confirmed as a ЦБСТ resident by name in the same 26.03.2025 ЦБСТ channel post covering the platform's extended session with "more than 40 residents": "Игорь Потапов (АО «НПП») отметил, что ЦБСТ стал эффективной площадкой прямого диалога между производителями и структурами, способными оказывать реальную поддержку" ("Igor Potapov (JSC 'NPP') noted that ЦБСТ has become an effective platform for direct dialogue between manufacturers and structures capable of providing real support") — the quoted "Игорь Потапов" matches this entity's sole founder and GD, Потапов Игорь Владимирович (100% owner since founding), exactly. Unlike every other manufacturer in this brief, though, the legal entity itself shows no operational substance: its own FY2024 and FY2025 БФО filings (pulled directly, units confirmed Тыс. руб.) report a single employee, zero revenue and zero expenses in both years, and a negative balance-sheet total of -₽4k — a dormant shell, not a functioning manufacturer, despite its OKVED (30.30, aircraft/spacecraft manufacturing) matching Стратим's and Экспломет's. Registered address is a residential apartment (ул. Автозаводская, д.4, кв.39). This is a useful counter-example for the brief's own methodology: a name can carry a confirmed, on-the-record ЦБСТ voice while the entity behind it has produced nothing yet — a reminder that "ЦБСТ resident" status alone, without independent financial or product confirmation, does not establish real operating capacity. Primary doc

ООО «КоптерЛаб» (ИНН 7720925267, ОГРН 1247700198734, registered 29.02.2024 in Moscow) is named directly as a "резидент ЦБСТ" by the platform's own channel (09.06.2026), which describes its ПАК «Форпост» counter-drone system: a complex that fuses radar feeds and interceptor drones into a single targeting picture, computes an intercept trajectory, and cues high-speed interceptors (up to 350 km/h) with target designation delivered straight to the operator's FPV goggles — with a "mothership" drone able to carry up to four interceptors aloft to meet an incoming threat. Corporately, КоптерЛаб is 80% owned by АО «АУРИН» (ИНН 9705214590, registered 15.12.2023, OKVED 70.22 — management consulting, not manufacturing) with the remaining 20% held by GD Федукович Кирилл Олегович personally. АО «АУРИН»'s own EGRUL record carries a significant red flag: its sole listed founder, Михайлова Ирина Валериевна, is marked with "ПризнНедДанУчр" — "сведения недостоверны (заявление физического лица о недостоверности сведений о нем)" ("information is unreliable — per a statement from the individual disputing the accuracy of information about them") — dated 12.08.2026, four days before this pull. This is the formal EGRUL mechanism an individual uses to disavow being recorded as a company's owner, typically indicating identity misuse or a nominee/strawman registration rather than genuine ownership; it means АУРИН's true beneficial owner is, as of this filing, formally undocumented. АУРИН's own General Director, by contrast, is fully identified: Клёцкин Владислав Николаевич, chair of the Ассоциация развития корпоративного спорта (Association for Corporate Sport Development) since 2022 and, more directly relevant here, Vice-President of the Russia Drone Racing Federation since 2023 — a genuine, if not ЦБСТ-specific, drone-industry credential that explains an otherwise-odd pattern: both АУРИН's and КоптерЛаб's own founding charters lead their OKVED lists with sports/education activity codes (спортивные клубы, дополнительное образование, торговля спортивной одеждой) ahead of the actual aircraft-manufacturing and software-development codes — a shared registration template tracing to Клёцкин's sports-federation background, not boilerplate noise. КоптерЛаб's own founding Устав (Протокол общего собрания учредителей №1, 22.02.2024, ₽10,000 charter capital, requested directly from ФНС) is standard post-2009 boilerplate that does not name founders or their split in the body text, but confirms the company was founded via a multi-party "Общее собрание учредителей," not a sole-participant decision — consistent with the 80/20 АУРИН/Федукович split existing from incorporation rather than a later stake sale. Independent corroboration beyond the ЦБСТ channel post: КоптерЛаб's «НСУ ФОРПОСТ» software is listed in Russia's official domestic-software registry (реестр отечественного ПО), entry №32198, dated 17.02.2026, and the company joined АЭРОНЕКСТ, Russia's largest UAV-industry professional association, on 17.03.2025 (per the association's own press release quoting Fedukovich) — no ЦБСТ/РОЙ.МАРКЕТ mention in that release, consistent with this being a separate industry-association track rather than evidence against the independently-sourced ЦБСТ tie. Its own FY2024–25 БФО filings (pulled directly, units confirmed Тыс. руб.) show КоптерЛаб scaling fast and profitably: revenue of ₽16.3m (2024) and ₽64.1m (2025), net profit of ₽0.69m (2024) and ₽34.9m (2025) — a striking 54% net margin in 2025 — and a balance sheet growing from ₽11.35m to ₽57.65m. Primary doc

ООО ОПКБ «Экспломет» (branded альфамарин.рф, ИНН 1215235283, ОГРН 1211200001650, registered 15.04.2021 in Йошкар-Ола, Республика Марий Эл) makes mine-detection and explosive-ordnance-disposal equipment — «Альфамарин 5000» tactical mine detectors, «Арена» capacitor-battery detonating machines, «Киберскан 3000» magnetometers, archway metal detectors, and sapper toolkits — under the tagline "Наш труд сохраняет жизни" ("our work saves lives"). It is not described as a ЦБСТ "resident" in the platform's own language, but the link is direct and personal: ЦБСТ's own channel reports (23.01.2025) that Экспломет's general director and chief designer, Фоминых Алексей Михайлович, sat on a TASS roundtable on humanitarian demining alongside ЦБСТ chairman Андрей Безруков (already documented elsewhere in this brief), a Hero of Russia and former North Caucasus Military District chief of engineering troops, and other figures — with ЦБСТ using the event to announce it is building its own "center of competence in humanitarian demining technology" on ЦБСТ's own base. The company's own FY2025 БФО notes go considerably further than the demining branding suggests: they state outright that in 2025 the organization was engaged "в т.ч. производством приборов для Министерства обороны РФ" ("including production of devices for the Ministry of Defense of the Russian Federation") — a direct, primary-source admission of an MoD customer relationship, not an inference from marketing language. The same notes disclose the company holds an active Минпромторг license (Свидетельство № Л008-00102-... от 21.07.2025, issued 26.03.2024) covering "разработк[у], производств[о], испытани[е], установк[у], монтаж, техническое обслуживание... вооружения и военной техники" and "хранени[е], реализаци[ю] и утилизаци[ю] боеприпасов" — development, production, testing, installation and maintenance of weapons and military equipment, and storage/sale/disposal of ammunition — plus resident status in the Сколково innovation-center program since 10.10.2023 (with an associated VAT exemption). Sole owner and beneficiary, per the same notes: Фоминых Алексей Михайлович, 100%; no dividends paid in 2025. Its own FY2022–25 БФО filings (pulled directly, units confirmed as Тыс. руб.) show a volatile but genuinely operating manufacturer: revenue of ₽48.3m (2022), ₽716.8m (2023), ₽210.5m (2024) and ₽782.4m (2025), with a loss year in 2024 (-₽114.6m net, driven by cost of sales exceeding revenue) followed by a recovery to ₽63.9m net profit in 2025. One filing inconsistency worth flagging: the company's own FY2023 filing reported a year-end balance sheet total of ₽606.1m, but the subsequent FY2024 filing restates that same 2023 year-end figure down to ₽375.0m — a ₽231m downward restatement with no explanation given in either filing; treat the FY2023 balance-sheet total as unreliable pending further pulls. A full-roster patent sweep independently confirms the product line at the IP level: 20 patents filed under Экспломет's own name, all metal-detector and EOD-equipment hardware (sensor heads, capacitor-battery detonating machines, arched/handheld metal-detector housings and control electronics) — a real, sustained engineering output consistent with the demining branding, not a marketing-only front. Separately, a leader-id.ru self-report (2026-08-24) lists Фоминых as "генеральный конструктор" (chief designer) at a second company, ООО «Метаскан» — an unconfirmed lead, not independently verified, but consistent with the pattern of demining/EOD-adjacent figures in this network holding roles at more than one entity. Primary doc

«Дронница» — the annual field-testing event where residents' products are physically demonstrated and combat-tested — has its own organizer identity resolved for the first time in this brief, via the event's own site (dronnitsa.ru and its year-specific mirrors, e.g. 2025.dronnitsa.ru) and a direct EGRUL pull. The event ("Всероссийский слёт операторов боевых беспилотных систем «Дронница»," running annually since August 2022 in Veliky Novgorod) is organized by the Координационный центр помощи Новороссии (КЦПН), a Donbas-war volunteer organization founded in 2014 by Алексей Марков ("Добрый"), Александр Любимов, Пётр Бирюков ("Аркадич") and Майя Мартынова, and headed throughout by Любимов, quoted in nearly every dronnitsa.ru post as "руководитель КЦПН" and "глава оргкомитета Дронницы." КЦПН's original charitable-fund legal vehicle (Благотворительный фонд «КЦПН», ИНН 2224171246, Barnaul) was liquidated in 2018; its current legal operator, per the site's own privacy-policy page and confirmed by a direct EGRUL pull, is Ассоциация благотворительной помощи «Взаимопомощь» (ИНН 4707039070, ОГРН 1164700050734, reg. 08.08.2016) — the EGRUL address (обл. Ленинградская, м.р-н Сланцевский, с.п. Старопольское, д. Шакицы, д.14) matches the site's disclosed operator address exactly. Its Председатель (chairman) since 26.03.2021 is Любимов Александр Сергеевич — the same person, EGRUL-confirmed rather than inferred; as an association, EGRUL records no separate founder list. Дронница's own site also discloses direct crowdfunding rails: a Sberbank card (2202205080218883, recipient "Майя Владимировна М." — matching co-founder Майя Мартынова) and a Bitcoin wallet (1GYsv19qxvYiqmSvGjV8HUyHquJyZjnS7E). The site's own news archive also gives, for the first time in this brief, an exact date for the ЦБСТ–Ушкуйник cooperation agreement already documented above without a precise date: a 06.09.2024 post reports the agreement signed that day at «Дронница 2024» between Алексей Чадаев (GD, АНО «НПЦ «Ушкуйник»») and Дмитрий Рубинштейн (GD, АНО «ЦБСТ»), witnessed alongside a separate Novgorod-region-government/АНО «Платформа НТИ» partnership signed the same day. Governor Андрей Никитин (through 2025) and врио губернатора Александр Дронов (2025) both speak at the event in an official capacity, and Дронница 2025's own coverage states the event hosted "свыше 100 российских предприятий" exhibiting hardware — with Дмитрий Рогозин appearing repeatedly (Oct. 2025, Sept. 2025) as head of an "апробационный отряд беспилотных систем «Барс-Сармат»" ("Bars-Sarmat" drone-testing detachment), a named figure not previously in this brief. The event's 2023 news posts also name a roster of exhibiting manufacturers not yet individually EGRUL-checked: КБ «Горизонт» (Moscow/Tambov joint venture — light frontline transport vehicles «Скакун», «Битюг», МТП «Тортилла-2», electric cart «КаталО»), КБ «Аякс Технологии» (VTOL recon/counter-battery drones, flagship «Аякс Х70» — checked and ruled out: ООО «Аякс-Технологии», ИНН 7805728050, St Petersburg, is confirmed dormant across all five available fiscal years via its own directly-pulled БФО filings — balance sheet never exceeding ₽5,000 (five roubles' worth of rounding, not thousand-scale error), zero revenue every year, registered at a residential apartment, and its own FY2025 explanatory note states plainly "поясняет, что в 2025 году финансово-хозяйственную деятельность не вело" ("explains that in 2025 it conducted no financial/economic activity"). This is not the entity behind the real, actively-exhibiting «КБ «Аякс Технологии»» drone maker — that company's actual legal identity remains unresolved, since "КБ" is a trade name, not a registered legal form, and its own site publishes no ИНН/ОГРН anywhere), группа компаний «Мобил-груп» (St Petersburg, 20+ years in radio infrastructure, claims the only genuinely domestic — not "reflagged foreign" — 4G/5G equipment in the ТОРП registry), «ДронНЕТ» (Central Black Earth region RF anti-drone jamming, «АМК РЭОП-ДронНЕТ»), «Локтар» (40-person volunteer comms/detection collective — drone detector «Дымка», scanner «Туман», relay kit «Шорох-У», scrambler «Скрежет», mapping tool «Фрактал», mesh-network software «Узор»), УЦ «Искра» (Nizhny Novgorod training center, donates directional UHF antennas rather than selling them), КБ «Око» (St Petersburg — «Привет-82» 82mm-mortar-payload kamikaze FPV, up to 30km range), and космическая компания VECTOR (Russian aerostat/stratospheric-drone developer — a name collision to flag against the unrelated US company "Vector," maker of the «Hammer F1» munition referenced elsewhere in this brief's КРАВТ paragraph; do not conflate the two). The same 2023 posts also give richer primary-source product detail for НПО «КАЙСАНТ» (already resolved above): its Дронница-2023 lineup is named as radio-jammer rifles «Аргус-Антидрон», dome suppression system «Аргус-Купол», mobile EW station «Аргус-Антифурия», and FPV drone «Аргус» — predating and consistent with the «Гермес»/«Аргус» dual-brand structure documented via ЦБСТ's own later channel posts. Primary doc

The @dronnitsa Telegram channel itself, fully scanned back to its 19.07.2022 founding, pushes this brief's Chadaev/Дронница origin story back more than a year before any of the corporate entities above existed. The channel's very first substantive post, same day it was created, states directly: "Это канал, посвящённый координации усилий по оснащению русских союзных сил современной беспилотной авиацией... Администратор — Алексей Чадаев @chadayev" — Chadaev was the channel's own founding administrator, more than 14 months before АНО «НПЦ «Ушкуйник»» was legally registered (19.09.2023) and over two and a half years before ЦБСТ's supervisory board first convened. A later post (14.08.2022) names the full organizing pair explicitly: "В оргкомитет слета входят: Александр Любимов, руководитель КЦПН, Алексей Чадаев — идеолог и автор идеи мероприятия" (Chadaev credited as "the ideologist and author of the event's idea," not merely a participant). Cross-checking the same channel's own Ushkuynik mentions independently confirms — does not revise — the brief's existing timeline: the earliest reference is 28.08.2023 (generic Дронница-2023 coverage), and by 10.10.2023 Chadaev writes of "работая над созданием НПЦ «Ушкуйник»," three weeks after the entity's actual EGRUL registration date. The channel's early 2022 posts also disclose an earlier, parallel delivery-fund alliance not previously documented in this brief: «ОПСБ», described repeatedly as "альянс телеграмм каналов ОПСБ" (a Telegram-channel alliance) and "Фонд ОПСБ," ran its own Belgorod-region donation drive with a separate Sberbank card (2202200872653710, recipient "Софья Андреевна Н.") distinct from КЦПН's own — jointly delivering equipment (Mavic 3 drones, tablets, a drone nicknamed "Охотник" to a reconnaissance unit on the Kharkiv axis) alongside "Alexey Chadaev's subscribers" from July 2022 on, the same donor-to-front-line pattern later formalised and scaled through Наша Правда. КЦПН's own 2022-era payment rails also differ from the ones currently on dronnitsa.ru: a Sberbank card 4276550055595329 (same recipient name, "Майя Владимировна М.," as the current site — continuity of the same financial controller across at least four years) and a different Bitcoin address, bc1q8a39pk0tdkdk8ydhrz2xl8c9l90evlzwgqnam4 (a SegWit/bech32 address, superseded by the legacy-format wallet the current site now displays) — both flagged here as historical payment infrastructure, not active accounts.
