One named lecturer from the very first Дронница (September 2022) is worth flagging for context rather than corporate significance: Игорь Мангушев (позывной "Берег"), who spoke on improvised counter-drone air-defence tactics, was killed by a car bomb in Luhansk in April 2023 — one of several prominent pro-Russian military bloggers assassinated during the war, and, on the evidence here, present at this network's very first event. Primary doc

Chadaev's own idea of unifying goods supplied to the front under a single brand predates ЦБСТ and РОЙ.МАРКЕТ by more than two years, and was first tested through an entirely different, mainstream business channel. On 01.11.2022 @chadayevru reports Корпорация «Синергия» (АО «Корпорация «Синергия»», ИНН 7743414861, Moscow — a name collision to flag against this brief's already-documented «КБ «Синергия»», the St Petersburg «Яким» product maker; do not conflate the two) launching a "Бизнес-мобилизация" campaign, with over 100 small/medium businesses joining a unified "Сделано для фронта" ("Made for the Front") brand, coordinated through regional ОНФ offices and «Опора России» (a national SME business association) via the portal мобилизация.рф. The corporation's own president, Вадим Лобов, is quoted crediting the brand concept directly: "Идея объединить товары, поставляемые военнослужащим, под единым брендом «Сделано для фронта» принадлежит политологу Алексею Чадаеву" — a role and campaign independently press-confirmed (opora.ru, and Corporation «Синергия»'s own site, both name Лобов as president and «Сделано для фронта»/мобилизация.рф as his initiative). Its own FY2023–25 БФО filings (pulled directly, including the FY2025 explanatory notes) correct this brief's earlier "consumer-goods conglomerate" characterisation: the entity is, in substance, the real-estate and facilities arm of Russia's «Синергия» private-business-education group — revenue is overwhelmingly space rental (₽42.4m of ₽55.1m FY2025 revenue), with the single largest tenant, at ₽31.8m of that rent, being the Автономная некоммерческая организация высшего образования «Московский университет «Синергия»» (ИНН 9702078886) itself, plus smaller lines for property management, corporate-seminar event organisation, and consulting. Its own FY2025 related-party note lists Лобов Вадим Георгиевич (ИНН 614000245280) as sole 100% beneficiary — not, per the filing, the day-to-day signatory, which is General Director Федяева Анна Юрьевна — alongside a roster of affiliated «Синергия»-brand entities: ООО «Синергия Диджитал», ООО «Мегакампус», АО «Синерджи Хаб», ООО «Синерджи Траст», the Moscow Financial-Industrial Academy («Московская финансово-промышленная академия»), and others. Financially it is a small business on a simplified tax regime (УСН): revenue held roughly flat (₽102.4m FY2023 → ₽65.4m FY2024 → ₽55.1m FY2025) while net profit collapsed from ₽6.4m (2023) to just ₽1.3m (2025), driven by a ₽150m long-term loan taken out in FY2024 (funding a capital build-out that shows up as ₽170.7m in fixed assets by FY2025) whose interest cost roughly doubled debt-service expense year-on-year. None of this changes the "Сделано для фронта" origin-story finding itself, which rests on the 2022 press quote, not this entity's later financials — the same brand-unification instinct that, over the following two years, scaled through Наша Правда/ЦБСТ into a state-defense-apparatus-backed marketplace. Five more named resident/exhibitor companies surface across the two channels' full scan: НТЦ «Корней», explicitly named "резидент НПЦ «Ушкуйник»" at Дронница 2025 — ground robotic platforms (НРТК) including an FPV-drone-carrying variant for «Князь-Вандал Новгородский», an amphibious model, and a tracked РК-2 variant fitted with an acoustic artillery-locating module (25km range); ГК «МВС» ("Мир высокотехнологичных систем"), presented at Дронница 2025 as a production partner spanning an electronics-manufacturing arm («VIRA»), a defence-plant component supplier, a Минцифры-accredited IT company, and a hunting-ground-turned-FPV-training-range in Novgorod region; «Адмирал», a VTOL drone (on the «Фтол-Аист» airframe) carrying two interceptor drones over a MESH relay network, already combat-tested in the SVO zone and commercially available; «Рокот Тех» (ООО «Рокот Тех», ИНН 7606140961, Yaroslavl — press-confirmed FPV-drone-propeller manufacturer, tens of thousands of units shipped for front-line use per bfm.ru/vestivrn.ru; grew out of the Yaroslavl branch of a volunteer project of the same name; note a similarly-named "Рокот-Тех," ИНН 7606139638, is an unrelated company since renamed ООО «Аркдекор» — do not conflate), a heavy-drone-and-propeller manufacturer, first-time Дронница exhibitor in 2024; and «Моторола-3М», an individual body-worn EW-protection unit developed by a "Лаборатория РЭБ" team operating inside the Мытищи volunteer charity «Верное дело» (a charity of this name exists in EGRUL, ИНН 9723257741, but among 21 same-named candidates the actual referent here is unconfirmed — don't cite that ИНН for this claim) — the same volunteer-to-manufacturer trajectory already documented elsewhere in this brief. Primary doc

Question two

The entity map

Ownership runs upward from the marketplace to a party-sponsored defence-tech body, and beyond it to two older foundations that are the actual source of funds. The vertical chain is documented in EGRUL extracts and the entities’ own published statements; the horizontal office cluster is address- and director-linked, which is weaker.

Two structural points are new in this revision. First, the chain does not stop at Наша Правда — that fund names its own founders in its published boilerplate, and one of them supplied the bulk of its money. Second, PSB’s co-founder status was registered in April 2025, more than a year after ЦБСТ was created, although both organisations had been describing PSB as a founder publicly since December 2024.

The layer above Наша Правда

Foundations (фонды) do not list their founders in a standard EGRUL extract the way an ООО lists participants — which is why the 15 August extract for Наша Правда shows a director and an address but no owners. The fund disclosed them itself, in a standard footer appended to its early Telegram posts:

Фонд поддержки патриотических инициатив «Наша Правда» в сотрудничестве с учредителями – Фондом «ДАР» и Фондом «Градислава» – и при поддержке партии «Единая Россия» реализует гуманитарные проекты, направленные на поддержку подразделений Армии РФ и населения в зоне проведения СВО.@nashapravdafond, 22.05.2023 and 08.06.2023 — verbatim

A year later the fund published its own first-year accounting, which identifies ДАР as the dominant source of its money:

С начала работы в 2023 году Фонд «Наша Правда» собрал более 1 773 млн руб. Учредитель Фонда НФ «Дар» направил на его нужды 1, 213 млрд руб. Еще более 560 миллионов рублей пожертвовали другие коммерческие и некоммерческие организации и граждане.@nashapravdafond, 28.04.2024 — verbatim, including original spacing

Фонд «Градислава» is identifiable with confidence: ИНН 7704278421, ОГРН 1117799007050, registered 22 April 2011, full name “Некоммерческий фонд содействия сохранению историко-культурного наследия «Градислава»”, director Иван Игоревич Карабинский. Its registered address is Котельническая наб., д. 25, стр. 1, офис 18 — the same office as Наша Правда, which occupies room 5 within it. For a declared cultural-heritage foundation its activity codes are conspicuously financial and property-oriented (64.9 principal, plus publishing, market research, social services).

This bears on an unexplained detail in Наша Правда’s registration file, which cites a lease agreement №18/25-22 dated 01.07.2022 — nine months before the fund legally existed — together with a sublease consent executed on its founding date. The natural reading is that Градислава held the original lease and sublet within it. That inference now has independent support: Градислава’s own subsidiary, ООО «Заволжское» (ИНН 3705066020, 99% owned by Градислава), was itself registered at г. Москва, наб. Котельническая, д.25, кор.1, оф.18 — the identical office — from 22.04.2011 until 04.09.2013, a full decade before Наша Правда existed. That is a second entity in Градислава’s own group occupying the same office years earlier, which is a materially stronger basis for the sublease reading than the lease-date coincidence alone, though it still falls short of a Rosreestr-confirmed chain of title.

Градислава’s ownership reach also now extends further than a name and an office: EGRUL confirms it holds 99% of Заволжское outright (уставный капитал ₽5.5m), and Заволжское’s own founder from 2011 to 2021 was Леонид Викторович Рубцов — the same name FBK gives as Градислава’s founder. Founder checks out against FBK’s account through a second, independent corporate vehicle, not just Градислава itself. Correction to earlier work in this investigation: a prior pass attributed Заволжское's directorship to Карабинский for 2013–2021; a fresh EGRUL pull shows the actual sitting director since 04.09.2013 (i.e. from the same date the registered address changed away from the Kotelnicheskaya office) has been Волков Михаил Вадимович, continuously through the present — Карабинский does not appear in Заволжское's own director history at all. That earlier attribution is withdrawn; only the founder match (Рубцов) and the shared-office history stand as independently confirmed.

Заволжское's own registered address today is г. Плёс, Ивановская область, Приволжский район — the same district FBK names for the Milovka estate near Plyos (valued around ₽20bn, alleged to have moved from ДАР to Градислава). That geographic match now has a second, independent corroboration: ДАР's own subsidiary Орион (below) is registered in the neighbouring town of Приволжск, in the same Приволжский district, and both Заволжское and Орион carry non-residential-property-management OKVED codes with revenue/expense patterns consistent with estate-maintenance overhead rather than ordinary commerce — Заволжское's own accounts show ₽1.095m revenue against ₽40.583m expenses, a 37× mismatch. Two independent subsidiaries, one per sister fund, both physically sited in the district FBK's Milovka claim names, is a materially stronger basis for that claim than either fact alone.

Заволжское's own audited-equivalent filings (FY2022–25, only FY2025 carries the company's own authored notes — earlier years are generic third-party summaries) sharpen this further. Revenue is negligible — ₽59,000 in 2024, ₽425,000 in 2025 — against admin costs alone of ₽31.9m and ₽38.35m those same years; accumulated losses grew from -₽10.5m (2023) to -₽42.4m (2024) to -₽81.8m (2025). The owner keeps it running with direct cash: "Вклады собственников" (owner contributions) of ₽29.06m (2024) and ₽42.9m (2025), booked to additional paid-in capital, which grew ₽86.8m → ₽115.9m → ₽158.8m over the same three years — a sharper version of the ДАР→Орион subsidy pattern below, with almost no offsetting revenue at all. Заволжское also runs its own small internal lending activity — ₽60.7m lent out in 2025, ₽57.5m of it repaid the same year, counterparty not named — the same unexplained-recipient treasury pattern seen at ДАР, Инвестментс and the Winter Olympic Fund. Its FY2025 notes (§11) also disclose an ownership split not previously confirmed: 99% held by Градислава (already known) plus 1% by an unnamed individual — the note gives only the percentage, not a name; unresolved.

"Усадьба Миловка": the estate, named directly in Градислава's own accounts

Pulling Градислава's own audited filings (FY2022–25, ИНН 7704278421) directly resolves what was previously the brief's single largest evidentiary gap — the FBK Milovka claim rested on district-level geographic inference and press reporting. It no longer does.

Градислава's own FY2025 filing states its property's OKTMO registration in full: 24620104106 (с. Миловка Плёсского Городского поселения Приволжского муниципального района Ивановской области) — с. Миловка (Milovka village), named directly, not inferred from district or town-level proximity. The same filing gives the basis for Градислава's own Kotelnicheskaya office: договор аренды №18/25-22 от 01.07.2022 with landlord АО «Фабрика им. Советской Армии» (ИНН 7709278792) — the exact lease number already inferred as the one Наша Правда sublets, now confirmed directly from Градислава's own filing as its own lease, with a landlord matching the Winter Olympic Fund's own landlord at the same building.

Градислава's FY2023 capital-expenditure notes go further still, naming the estate itself as an accounting line item:

Системы охранной сигнализации входных дверей с коваными элементами на объектах Усадьбы МиловкаФонд «Градислава», FY2023 ГИР БО filing notes §2.2, ₽60,241 thousand spent in 2023 — verbatim

The same table itemises the complex by name across 2022–2023 capital spending: a Главный дом (Main House, with its own facade-lighting and HVAC automation), a Гостевой дом (Guest House), a Мельница (Mill, with its own plumbing, drainage and power supply), an Егерский кордон (Gamekeeper's Lodge), a 63-person hotel building (own AC system, perimeter fencing, dispatch/security systems for the "гостиничного комплекса"), a checkpoint/guardhouse building, a pond, a non-residential building with its own swimming lane, a boiler house, gated CCTV and forged-iron entry-alarm security infrastructure, and dozens of individually custom-made solid-oak furniture pieces (₽100,000–750,000 each), wine refrigerators and high-end electronics. This is the itemised architectural signature of a large private estate — main house, guest house, hotel, mill, gamekeeper's lodge, checkpoint, pond, pool building, boiler house, gated security — inside a fund whose declared purpose is historic-cultural heritage preservation.

The money behind it moves in one large cycle: Градислава received ₽11.0bn in "добровольные имущественные взносы и пожертвования" (voluntary property contributions and donations) in 2023 — the donor is not named in any filing reviewed — pushing its cash balance to ₽12.27bn by end-2023. That balance was then spent down almost entirely: ₽12.95bn on "целевые мероприятия" (targeted programmes) in 2024 alone, dropping cash to ₽629.5m by end-2024 and to just ₽1.17m by end-2025. The ₽12.95bn figure is not itemised in any schedule reviewed — only broad categories are given — so what it specifically funded beyond the (much smaller, FY2023-dated) estate line items above is an open question. Its own filing states the underlying transactions were "по финансовой стабилизации дочернего общества, направленные на сохранение его имущества, являющегося памятником архитектуры регионального уровня" (financial stabilisation of a subsidiary aimed at preserving its property, which is a regional-level architectural heritage monument) — the estate's formal legal cover.

Градислава also withholds its own наблюдательный совет (5-person board) under the identical citation ДАР and the Winter Olympic Fund use: п.16 ПБУ 11/2008, on the grounds that disclosure "may нанести урон деятельности Фонда." Its balance sheet additionally carries ₽161.8m and ₽92.5m in fixed assets specifically categorised as "используемые для реализации законодательства РФ о мобилизационной подготовке и мобилизации, которые законсервированы" — mothballed assets held under Russia's mobilisation-preparedness legislation, a category not otherwise seen in this investigation and not further itemised in the schedules reviewed.

Фонд «ДАР» is identified, and the identification is now the most consequential fact in this brief. Vedomosti’s April 2023 report on the fund’s creation names it in full, together with the ownership behind it:

Учредителем «Градиславы» числился Леонид Рубцов, учредителем фонда ДАР является ООО «Левит» главы «Новатэка» Леонида Михельсона.Ведомости, «Для поддержки армии и людей в зоне спецоперации создан фонд «Наша правда»», 18.04.2023 — verbatim

“Фонд ДАР” is ФОНД РЕГИОНАЛЬНЫХ НЕКОММЕРЧЕСКИХ ПРОЕКТОВ «ДАР», ИНН 7705517400, ОГРН 1067799028923, registered 03.11.2006, director Виктор Ильич Давыдов since 2008 — the full legal name used by FBK, confirmed against the EGRUL extract. Its 2025 accounts show ₽25.1bn in assets against zero revenue, with OKVEDs spanning financial services, residential and non-residential construction, publishing and sport. Directly pulled ГИР БО filings for FY2021–FY2025 Primary doc add real texture to that headline number: the ~₽23.5bn balance is overwhelmingly a securities and deposit portfolio, not cash — ~₽11.6bn in unlisted securities (not traded on an organised market, issuers otherwise undisclosed) plus ~₽4.9bn in bank deposits, all at Банк ГПБ (АО)/Gazprombank, the same bank Наша Правда uses. One tranche of those securities is named directly in the FY2024 filing's own notes: ~₽2.1187bn in shares of АО «Сейм-Агро», pledged to an unnamed third party as collateral, unchanged across 2023 and 2024. Сейм-Агро's own FY2022 filing narrows the field: its notes disclose that Сейм-Агро shares are pledged to ООО «Росагролизинг», Russia's state agricultural-equipment leasing company, as collateral for an ₽387.09m lease/purchase package dated 07.08.2019 Primary doc — a strong candidate for the pledgee, though the filing doesn't name whose shares specifically and the amount is smaller than ДАР's ~₽2.1bn tranche, so treat this as suggestive rather than confirmed. Balance-sheet totals confirmed back to 2020 (₽18.23bn) show this capital base predates Наша Правда's 2023 founding and the SVO-marketplace period entirely.

ДАР's own primary-source filing confirms its founder directly, not just via Vedomosti's reporting: the FY2023 ГИР БО filing's notes state, verbatim, Primary doc

Учредитель: Общество с ограниченной ответственностью «ЛЕВИТ» Зарегистрировано МИФНС №16 по Самарской области 23 мая 2006 г., ОГРН 1066330007798, адрес (место нахождения): 443099, область Самарская, г. Самара, ул. Алексея Толстого, д. 117, офис 1, ИНН 6330030244, дата создания 23.05.2006 г.Fund «ДАР», FY2023 ГИР БО filing notes, compiled 18.03.2024 — verbatim

ООО «Левит» (ИНН 6330030244, ОГРН 1066330007798, Samara) is owned 78.29% by Leonid Mikhelson, chairman of Novatek and among Russia’s wealthiest men, and 21.71% by Leonid Simanovsky, a long-serving United Russia State Duma deputy and Novatek co-owner. Charter capital ₽118,371,196.

Correction to earlier work in this investigation

An earlier pass dismissed an OCCRP Aleph hit on «Левит» as a same-name collision, on the grounds that the records were Duma deputy Simanovsky’s asset declarations rather than anything tied to ДАР. That conclusion was wrong. The declared holdings of 21.7% and 11.07% match Simanovsky’s real 21.708% stake in exactly this company. The Aleph record was correct and was discarded in error; the “ДАР ↔ Левит ↔ Mikhelson” line is now supported independently by Vedomosti, FBK, EGRUL company registry data, and — as of this revision — ДАР's own audited financial statements.

A second, independent EGRUL trail reinforces the same link. ДАР currently owns 100% of ООО «Орион» (ИНН 7706664648, ОГРН 1077758220451, registered Приволжск, Ivanovo oblast) — and Орион’s own founder from its creation in 2006 until 14.12.2021 was ООО «Левит» itself. Левит founded a company that ДАР now wholly owns: a direct corporate thread between Mikhelson’s holding vehicle and ДАР that does not depend on Vedomosti’s reporting or on the founder-of-ДАР claim at all. This is not a passive shell relationship: ДАР's own FY2021–2025 filings show a real and growing money flow with Орион acting as a service provider and purchasing agent — ₽46.7m in 2021, ₽75.5m in 2022, ₽75.5m in 2023 for services/work performed, plus agent-purchased goods each year — with ДАР paying down real payables to Орион each period.

Левит's own filings: the size of the fortune behind ДАР, quantified

Левит's own audited annual filings (FY2022–25, ИНН 6330030244) turn "Mikhelson/Simanovsky holding vehicle" from an ownership percentage into a fully quantified picture. Its own notes state Левит's core asset directly: 222,202,526 shares of ПАО «НОВАТЭК», valued at ₽264.65bn at 31 December 2025 (up from ₽217.92bn end-2024, down from ₽325.54bn end-2023, tracking the share's own market price on MMVB). Net assets: ₽206.88bn (2025), ₽170.84bn (2024), ₽267.85bn (2023). Ownership is unchanged — Михельсон Л.В. 78.2902%, Симановский Л.Я. 21.7098% — under GD Шуликин Николай Константинович, 13 employees.

A related-party disclosure section is new to the FY2025 filing (the same pattern already seen at ДАР — richer narrative disclosure only arriving with FY2025-vintage filings network-wide) and it quantifies what the two owners actually took out of the company that year Primary doc: ₽15,880,926 thousand (₽15.88bn) in dividends paid in 2025, split proportionally to their stakes, plus a further ₽6,862,725 thousand (₽6.86bn) resolved for distribution on 12 January 2026 as a post-balance-sheet event — against 2025 net profit of ₽8,253,689 thousand. The same section names two entities new to this investigation: ООО «НОВА», which supplies Левит car-rental services (₽10.9m in 2025) and — per a post-balance-sheet event — became Левит's landlord under a new lease from 1 March 2026; and Благотворительный фонд «Виктория», a charity founded personally by Mikhelson (not via Левит or ДАР) that receives free use of part of Левит's Samara office. НОВА is now identified with high confidence: ООО «НОВА» (ИНН 6330037352, Новокуйбышевск, Samara oblast — the same oblast as Левит) is 100% personally owned by Михельсон Леонид Викторович himself, per a direct EGRUL pull, with a substantial ₽615.77m charter capital consistent with a genuine operating vehicle rather than a shell (GD Кайденко А.В. since 2019). This surfaced via an FNS name-search for Виктория Михельсон, not Левит's own filing — a coincidental cross-reference, not a documentary link stated anywhere, but the region match, the direct personal-ownership-by-Mikhelson match, and the scale together make this a strong identification. (An earlier, differently-numbered Samara НОВА candidate checked in an prior pass was independently ruled out — wrong OKVED, shell-like data-reliability flags — and should not be confused with this one.) Виктория is a distinct, unrelated entity to ДАР/Градислава/Наша Правда — flagged here only so it isn't later conflated with the network's own foundations. A subsequent OCCRP Aleph check (Russian Ministry of Interior NGO-affiliates registry) found no exact-name match for the charity itself, but did surface Михельсон Виктория Леонидовна listed as affiliated with a Samara cultural institution called «Музей «Виктория» — Искусство быть Современным» (2018–19) and a related targeted-capital arts fund, «Расширение Пространства» (2018, now independently EGRUL-confirmed: ИНН 9729271710, «Специализированный фонд целевого капитала для поддержки культуры и искусства «Расширение Пространства»») — a plausible personal namesake connection — shared surname, patronymic «Леонидовна» consistent with being a daughter of Leonid Mikhelson, and a Samara geographic fit with Левит's own base — that would explain the charity's name, but this is inference from a name/date match in a secondary registry, not a documentary link to the «Благотворительный фонд «Виктория»» named in Левит's filing — do not present it as confirmed. The same name search also surfaced ООО «ГЭС-2» (ИНН 7706809928) — the Moscow contemporary-art centre operated by Mikhelson's V-A-C Foundation — as another entity tied to Виктория Михельсон's name in FNS's own index, consistent with the family's cultural-philanthropy pattern rather than anything relevant to the war-supply network.

The filing also puts a hard number on a loss this brief had previously only described qualitatively: an off-balance-sheet write-off of ₽1,650,317 thousand (₽1.65bn), tied explicitly to "договору мены, купли-продажи акций Первобанка" (the barter/sale-purchase agreement for Первобанк shares), with recovery probability assessed by Левит itself as zero — direct documentary evidence of what the Первобанк→PSB nationalisation actually cost Mikhelson and Simanovsky (see below). Separately, Левит moved its deposit banking from ПАО Банк ПСБ (₽7.4–7.5bn, 2023–24) to Банк ГПБ (АО)/Gazprombank (₽7.8bn, 2025) — the same bank ДАР and Наша Правда use — and filed to change its registered address on 24 March 2026, from ул. Алексея Толстого, д.117 to ул. Некрасовская, д.2 in Samara; neither move is explained in the filing.

Орион: a documented private estate, not a property manager

Орион's own audited annual filings — pulled directly for FY2023, FY2024 and FY2025, all unqualified opinions from the same auditor, ООО «Машаудит», that audits ДАР and Наша Правда — read very differently from an ordinary building-management business, once the notes are read past the balance sheet.

Орион was not always called Орион: until 06.12.2012 its full legal name was «Управляющая компания Фонда «ДАР»» — literally "ДАР Fund Management Company" — its own filing's stated corporate history, not an inference. Every filing since FY2023 also carries a mandatory beneficial-ownership disclosure, unchanged across all three years:

Единственным бенефициарным владельцем Общества является Давыдов Виктор Ильич. (Единоличный исполнительный орган единственного участника ООО «Орион»)ООО «Орион», FY2023/2024/2025 ГИР БО filing notes §9 — verbatim, identical across all three years

Давыдов Виктор Ильич is Фонд «ДАР»'s own director, in that post since 2008 — so this discloses no outside beneficiary, but it is the first document in this investigation to use the formal term "бенефициарный владелец" for anyone in the network, naming the same individual the entity map already carries as ДАР's director.

The FY2025 filing's land-lease note (§4.3) lists five separate lease agreements with Приволжский district and regional authorities, all running to 2061, totalling roughly 1.36 million m² plus 458 hectares of forest — around 1,800 hectares combined. Two of the five state their purpose in the lease itself as «в целях осуществления деятельности в сфере охотничьего хозяйства» (hunting-economy activity); the remaining three are coded for agricultural production. Sitting inside an otherwise ordinary property-management revenue breakdown, the FY2024 and FY2025 filings both carry a separately itemised "Содержание животных" (animal husbandry) line — ₽48.0m revenue / ₽48.1m cost in 2024, ₽57.25m / ₽52.15m in 2025 — plus a "Сопровождение культурно-рекреационных мероприятий" (cultural-recreational event support) line running ₽14–20m a year back to at least 2023. Read together with the land-lease purposes, this is the accounting signature of a private hunting estate operated inside a nominally ordinary management company, not evidence of any weapons or marketplace activity.

The related-party note (§17/§20) shows the actual cash: Орион's sole related-party counterpart is ДАР itself, primarily via a "ТО и эксплуатационный контроль" (maintenance and operational control) services contract running continuously since 2014. The payment has grown every year for which a filing was pulled: ₽75.5m (2022) → ₽76.4m (2023) → ₽80.8m (2024) → ₽109.6m (2025) — roughly +45% over three years, the largest single jump (+36%) in the most recent year.

Орион also holds a small equity stake — 1% of ООО «Гарант Клуб» (ИНН 7729610279, Moscow, ₽20,000, confirmed unchanged since 2012) — that leads somewhere unexpected. The other 99% of Гарант Клуб is held by ООО «Финконсалтингк» (ИНН 7709869763, Moscow, OKVED matching Гарант Клуб's own non-residential-property-rental code), which is in turn 100% owned by Фонд поддержки зимних олимпийских видов спорта — the Winter Olympic Sports Support Fund, a vehicle originally organised around Sochi 2014 funding.

The Winter Olympic Fund: a second, independent route to the same building — and a documented sibling link

Pulling the Olympic Fund's own audited filings directly (FY2023–25, ИНН 7706470191) turns what looked like one small equity stake into the strongest single corroboration in this brief, because it was reached by a completely different path than everything else here — through Орион's minority stake, not through ДАР or Наша Правда — and still lands in the same place.

Its registered address is Котельническая наб., д.25, стр.1, офис 16 — the same building as Наша Правда and Градислава (both office 18). Three organisations reached via three different corporate paths (Наша Правда/Градислава via ДАР's own boilerplate; the Olympic Fund via Орион → Гарант Клуб → Финконсалтингк) all sit in one Moscow building. Its lease is held with АО «Фабрика им. Советской Армии» as landlord (Договор аренды №16/25-21 от 01.08.2021, бессрочный).

Its own filings name a sole beneficial owner: General Director Бровченко Сергей Руфинович (in post since 11.11.2014) Primary doc — sharing a patronymic with Александр Руфинович Бровченко, General Director of Фонд «Наша Правда» (already in this brief's executives table). The two are documented as brothers by investigator.org.ua, independent of the patronymic match. A named individual now sits at the head of both a war-supply fund and a ₽42.9bn treasury vehicle reached through a different ownership chain — this is the clearest person-level link in the entire structure.

The scale is larger than ДАР itself: total assets ₽42.9bn at end-2025, up from ₽39.7bn (2024) and ₽18.4bn (2023) — more than doubling in two years. Almost entirely financial investments (₽12.37bn long-term, ₽23.46bn in short-term deposits by end-2025), generating ₽4.79bn in interest/other income and ₽1.197bn in profit tax paid in 2025 alone, against just 4 staff and ₽47.1m in total operating costs. This reads as a large, active treasury operation, not a passive or dormant shell. And its own notes withhold related-party detail under the identical legal citation ДАР itself uses to withhold its board Primary doc:

Характер взаимоотношений и персональные данные связанных сторон не могут быть раскрыты на основании п.16 ПБУ 11/2008, так как деятельность Фонда связана с реализацией программ в том числе другими некоммерческими организациями средства пожертвования на которые поступают от лиц, имеющих ограничения на раскрытие информации. Раскрытие указанных данных может оказать негативное влияние на деятельность Фонда.Фонд поддержки зимних олимпийских видов спорта, FY2025 ГИР БО filing notes §3.10 — verbatim

A capital-projects note in the FY2023 filing (§2.2, image-scanned and rotated — required re-rendering and OCR to read) shows a completed ₽93.99m "Автоматизированная система противодействия БЛА" (automated counter-drone system), commissioned during 2022, sitting in the same schedule as ordinary building capex — a staff amenities block, ventilation upgrades for buildings labelled only "ГК" and "КСЛ," and a perimeter-fence reconstruction. This is consistent with the fund operating a real, physical, secured complex somewhere; which complex, and where, is not established by anything reviewed so far — the "ГК"/"КСЛ" labels are not tied to an address in any filing pulled.

Below the Olympic Fund: an actively redeveloping Moscow property and a multi-billion-ruble internal bank

Гарант Клуб's own founding-document history (four charter versions, 2008–2025, requested via nalog.gov.ru, obtained 18.08.2026) traces its origin to before this ownership structure existed: its original 06.10.2008 charter names a single individual founder, Долгушевский Алексей Михайлович, with no evident tie to ФКК, Орион or the Winter Olympic Fund network visible at that point. The 1% Орион stake is independently confirmed unchanged since 2012, so the transition from Долгушевский's sole ownership to the current 99%/1% structure happened sometime in that roughly four-year window — not pinned down further by any filing reviewed. The charter's stated business purpose (hotels, real estate, construction, securities) is unchanged in substance from 2008 through the June 2025 "новой редакции" restatement — consistent with the OKVED-mismatch pattern already documented, rather than a new signal. Primary doc

ФКК and Гарант Клуб's own audited filings show the chain isn't dormant at either level. Гарант Клуб (99% ФКК / 1% Орион) holds ₽420–442m in investment property at its Бауманская 33/2 office and generates real rental income (₽40.35m in 2025) — but runs deeply and consistently negative equity: -₽604m (2025), -₽626m (2024), -₽624m (2023), financed almost entirely by a ₽1.4584bn short-term loan with a static principal (unchanged since before 2024, only interest accruing). Its related-party note (§4.1) names only ФКК, Орион and its own GD Репнев С.В. as related parties — no outside lender — so ФКК is very likely, but not explicitly named as, the source of that loan. Its OKVED was "Деятельность гостиниц" (hotel operations) until 08.12.2025, only recently reclassified to generic property management, and its own going-concern note describes active redevelopment already underway: new construction, geological/engineering surveys commissioned, "разработка современной комплексной концепции развития для модернизации существующих и строительства новых строений." A fresh EGRUL pull identifies a third entity at the exact same address: ООО «Винград» (ИНН 9701066447, registered 2017, 99.5% owned directly by ФКК, 0.5% by a named individual, Швецов Алексей Александрович) is registered at Бауманская 33/2, стр.1, пом.2/1 — literally the same building — under the OKVED code "Выращивание винограда" (grape cultivation), an obvious purpose mismatch for a central-Moscow commercial address, consistent with the same OKVED/activity-mismatch pattern already seen elsewhere in this network (Кольская верфь, Боевые технологии). ФКК's other named portfolio company, ООО «Стройстандарт» (ИНН 7733629755, 99.98% ФКК / 0.02% its own GD Аккерман Е.В.), is a real, older entity (registered 2007, predating ФКК itself) with a plausible OKVED match — non-residential property rental/management — at a separate Moscow address (Большая Татарская 35, стр.3), acquired by ФКК rather than founded by it. A fourth portfolio company, not previously identified, surfaced by checking what else ФКК's own GD Крылов А.О. is tied to: ООО «Оржицкие артезианские воды» (ИНН 9709055542, water/soft-drinks wholesale, OKVED 46.34.1) was personally founded by Крылов in October 2019 — while he was still ФКК's sitting GD — registered at Большой Дровяной пер., д.8, стр.1 — ФКК's own office address. It is now 99% owned by ФКК directly, with the remaining 1% (since January 2025) held by Швецов Алексей Александрович — the same individual who holds the 0.5% minority stake in Винград and now also serves as this company's GD, establishing him as a recurring minority-co-owner/nominee-director figure across at least two of ФКК's portfolio companies, the same role Аккерман Е.В. plays at Стройстандарт.

ФКК itself states its purpose in its own FY2023 filing, verbatim: "основные виды хозяйственной деятельности... предоставление процентных займов" — providing interest-bearing loans, in its own words. Its balance sheet confirms the scale: ₽19.7bn total assets (down from ₽23.6bn in 2023), holding ₽5.85bn in long-term loans issued and ₽5.86bn in equity stakes, against ₽5.54bn in its own long-term loans payable — order-of-magnitude consistent with the Olympic Fund's own ₽4.975bn long-term loan entry, though neither filing names the counterparty directly. No "Связанные стороны" section exists anywhere in ФКК's FY2025 filing at all. ФКК's FY2023 notes also disclose real estate in Санкт-Петербург acquired for resale, and its FY2024 capital-projects table itemises an apparent private residential compound under construction — two 3-storey houses (~460–483 m² each), outbuildings, and eight separate land plots under internal site numbering only, with no address given anywhere in the filing. A ₽11.59bn one-off "reализация иного имущества" transaction in 2024, and a single year in which a ₽21.96bn short-term loan was fully repaid while a fresh ₽18.37bn long-term loan was drawn, show tens of billions of rubles cycling through ФКК within individual years — consistent with its stated role as the network's internal lender, on a scale larger than any other entity examined in this brief besides the Olympic Fund itself.

A fresh EGRUL pull (via OCCRP Aleph, cross-checked against ФНС) adds names to two things this brief had only as aggregates. ФКК's own GD for most of its life was Крылов Андрей Олегович (2011–2022) — not previously named — and its equity-stakes line (₽5.86bn, above) includes at least two named portfolio companies: ООО «Стройстандарт» and ООО «Винград» — both examined directly below, with their current ownership, capital and address details. FNS's own tax-disclosure figures (a different, non-audited basis from the filing above) show ФКК's declared revenue swinging from ₽1.1bn (2018) to a peak ₽12.5bn (FY2024) against similarly large expenses — continuously loss-adjacent on this measure across the whole period, and 100% owned by the Olympic Fund without interruption since 29 Dec 2012. CONFIRMED via primary document: ФКК's own original 2011 founding charter is titled, on its cover page, "Устав Общества с ограниченной ответственностью «Финансово-Консультационная Компания Фонда «ДАР»»" — approved 12 January 2011 by decision of the company's sole founder (Решение №1) and registered with ФНС 24 January 2011. This is a direct textual link between ФКК and ДАР, pulled from ФКК's own учредительные документы (state-issued copies of founding documents, obtained via nalog.gov.ru), independent of and confirming the earlier Aleph lead. The charter itself, like all post-2009 уставы, does not name the founder in its body text — that detail sits in the underlying Решение №1, which was not separately requested — so who specifically held ФКК before the Olympic Fund's own confirmed 100% stake began (29 Dec 2012, per ФНС's ownership history) remains formally unconfirmed, though the name leaves little real doubt about ФКК's origin as ДАР's own vehicle. Initial charter capital was ₽100,000, matching ФНС's ownership-history record exactly. Notably, the charter itself provides for a Наблюдательный совет (Supervisory Board) — unusual governance structure for a plain ООО, and the same board terminology used by ДАР, Наша Правда, Градислава and the Olympic Fund.

ДАР fully disclosed its own supervisory board for three consecutive years, then stopped. Its FY2021, FY2022 and FY2023 filings all name the same five people Primary doc: chair Илья Владимирович Елисеев (matching the sanctioned individual discussed below), and members Виталий Юрьевич Головачёв, Татьяна Николаевна Медисова, Алексей Владимирович Четвертков and Екатерина Святославовна Трубина. By the FY2025 filing, that same section is withheld outright, with the fund stating its own reason:

Информация о персональном составе не раскрывается в соответствии с п.16 ПБУ 11/2008. Фондом принято решение об ограничении раскрытия информации в связи с возможным влиянием раскрытия на источники пожертвований и их объемы.Fund «ДАР», FY2025 ГИР БО filing notes §3.13 — verbatim

Translated: board composition is withheld under an accounting-standard exemption, and the fund states outright that disclosure might affect “the sources of donations and their volume.” ДАР named its board without hesitation through FY2023 and stopped by FY2025 — a shift in disclosure posture worth noting on its own, independent of who the withheld names would be.

What ДАР and «Градислава» are, according to FBK and to Western governments

Both foundations are central to Navalny’s FBK investigation «Он вам не Димон» (March 2017), which alleges they hold assets beneficially belonging to Dmitry Medvedev — then prime minister, now Deputy Chairman of the Security Council and Chairman of United Russia. FBK’s account of the two funds matches this brief’s independently-obtained registry data at every checkable point:

  • FBK gives ДАР’s capital as ₽33bn from Novatek shareholders Mikhelson and Simanovsky — the same two names, in the same roles, that the registry gives for ООО «Левит».
  • FBK names Leonid Rubtsov as Градислава’s founder and Ivan Karabinsky as its director — both confirmed independently here, Karabinsky through registry data and through Наша Правда’s own supervisory board.
  • FBK states Karabinsky was paid by ДАР and used a darfund.ru address — i.e. the two “separate” foundations shared staff.
  • FBK describes the Milovka estate at Plyos (valued around ₽20bn) being transferred from ДАР to Градислава — the same pattern of asset movement between the sister funds that this brief inferred independently from the lease predating Наша Правда’s existence.
  • FBK writes that Градислава «не имеет ничего общего с культурно-историческим наследием» — a conclusion reached separately here purely from its OKVED profile.

The network’s central figure is Илья Владимирович Елисеев (b. 19.12.1965), Medvedev’s Leningrad State University law-faculty classmate and Deputy Chairman of the Management Board of Gazprombank, who chairs ДАР’s supervisory board. He is sanctioned by the US (OFAC, 8 May 2022), UK, Canada, Australia, New Zealand and Ukraine. OpenSanctions records his designation description as “an authorized representative of former Russian President Dmitry Medvedev and nominal owner of his assets” — meaning the Medvedev attribution is not FBK’s alone; it appears in the reasoning of Western sanctions authorities. Eliseev has publicly denied that ДАР is connected to Medvedev.

Why this is the story, if it holds

The chain now runs: a foundation identified in sanctions listings as holding the assets of United Russia’s chairman → funds ₽1.213bn into a war-supply foundation → which co-founds ЦБСТ with the state defence bank → which owns the company operating a marketplace wired into Defence Ministry payment infrastructure. And Medvedev is not a distant figure at the far end of it: ЦБСТ’s own channel states he «стоял у истоков создания АНО «ЦБСТ»», and he appeared personally at ЦБСТ events twice in 2025. Every link is separately sourced, but the two ends of the chain are joined by investigative and sanctions reporting rather than by a Russian state document, and the brief should say so plainly.

The Promsvyazbank connection is historical, and ended badly

One claim circulating in search results — that Левит “controls Promsvyazbank” — is false as stated, and the real history is worth getting right because PSB sits on the other side of this chain as ЦБСТ’s co-founder. Левит held 64.06% of Samara’s Первобанк, with Simanovsky’s wife Sofya Simanovskaya holding a further 12.99%; Promsvyazbank acquired Первобанк in November 2015, making Mikhelson and Simanovsky PSB shareholders. PSB was then placed under Central Bank administration on 15 December 2017, taken from the Ananyev brothers, and transferred to full state ownership by a law Putin signed in March 2018, becoming Russia’s designated defence-industry bank. Russian press from that period frames Mikhelson and Simanovsky as parties trying to recover something from the collapse. PSB is today 100% owned by the Russian Federation through Rosimushchestvo. The Mikhelson–PSB relationship is a prior commercial one that was wiped out by nationalisation — it is not evidence of present common control, and must not be written as though it were. Левит's own FY2025 filing now puts a figure on that loss: ₽1.65bn written off as an unrecoverable receivable tied explicitly to the Первобанк share exchange/sale agreement, held off-balance-sheet with recovery probability assessed as zero Primary doc.

The real subsidiary structure is bigger than press or Telegram sourcing showed

A direct pull from ФНС’s EGRUL API (not an aggregator) on 15 August 2026 surfaced holdings and events that appear in no press coverage or Telegram post found this session:

  • ЦБСТ owns ООО «Системный интегратор» (100%, ИНН 4632306583), registered 16.07.2025 in Kursk — a region under sustained front-line pressure since the 2024 incursion — with secondary OKVEDs explicitly for «производство радиоэлектронных средств связи» and «производство радиолокационной, радионавигационной аппаратуры и радиоаппаратуры дистанционного управления»: radio-comms and radar/remote-control production codes, not the publishing/research/consulting profile ЦБСТ itself carries. The subsidiary's own first-year (FY2025) filing confirms the 100% stake directly in its own words and adds a hard cash-flow fact: ЦБСТ funded its entire first year of operation with ₽21.119m in "финансовая помощь единственного участника" (financial aid from the sole participant), against ₽16.14m in operating costs and zero revenue from its stated core activity (software development, OKVED 62.01) Primary doc.
  • ЦБСТ also owns two joint-stock companies, «Спецтех Инвестиции» and «Спецтех Первый» (100% each), both run by Дмитрий Рубинштейн — ЦБСТ’s own former general director — and both registered with АО «РТ-Регистратор» as share registrar. “РТ” is Rostec’s own corporate initialism, and RT-Registrator services predominantly Rostec-group and defence-adjacent joint-stock companies; this is a structural signal worth independent verification, not proof of Rostec ownership.
  • Наша Правда owns «Инвестментс», though the exact stake changed within a year — its FY2024 related-party note states 100%, acquired via a notarised purchase deed dated 20.09.2023 (№ 77/1899-н/77-2023-2-435, bought not founded), while the FY2025 filing shows 90.91%; something diluted the stake roughly nine points during 2025, not yet explained by any filing reviewed. (ИНН 9707004743, GD Григорьев Д.Е.), a financial-services vehicle that functions as an internal lender to the group: its own cash-flow statements show ₽41.0m lent to subsidiary companies in 2023, ₽69.3m in 2024 and ₽23.2m in 2025 — roughly ₽133m over three years — under the explicit line “предоставление займов дочерним компаниям”. The recipients are not named in any filing reviewed, including Наша Правда's own FY2024 related-party disclosure, which names only the two founder funds and the two subsidiaries and stops there.
  • «Инвестментс» absorbed АО «Борей» (ИНН 9701280698) on 26.12.2025, a reorganisation begun 19.08.2025. Борей’s own OKVED profile explicitly includes 25.40 «Производство оружия и боеприпасов» (weapons and ammunition production) alongside electronics, comms and metalworking codes, and used the same АО «РТ-Регистратор». Борей’s general director was swapped seven months before the merger — from its founding director to Григорьев, «Инвестментс»’s own GD — and Борей’s final year of independent operation shows ₽0 revenue against ₽55.1m in expenses, plus real unpaid tax arrears (НДФЛ, insurance contributions, penalties) as of May 2025. «Инвестментс»’s own 2025 accounts show the absorption’s effect directly: total equity went from −₽334,000 to −₽56.7m in the merger year, and short-term liabilities roughly sextupled — consistent with Борей’s debts landing on «Инвестментс»’s balance sheet rather than disappearing.
  • ЦБСТ is called Наша Правда's own “дочернее предприятие” (subsidiary) in the latter's FY2024 related-party note Primary doc — stronger language than the “co-founder” framing used elsewhere (ЦБСТ was co-founded by both Наша Правда and ПАО «Банк ПСБ»). Both characterisations come from primary documents; they are not necessarily contradictory under Russian accounting rules, but the brief carries both rather than picking one.
  • «Инвестментс»'s FY2024 notes name a loan recipient and a sole creditor that no other filing discloses — and the creditor is ФКК. A short-term ₽12.3m loan at 16.5% (issued March 2024) plus a 99%-equity stake went to АО «ХК Гумич» (ИНН 9726051114, «Холдинговая компания «Гумич», registered Aug 2023, Варшавское ш. д.28А — a different building on the same Warsaw-highway corridor as ЗИТ.ЦИ) — both the loan and the equity were fully liquidated in December 2024 (loan plus ₽1.509m interest repaid; the equity stake, bought for ₽50m, sold for ₽65m, a 30% return, via a paid sales agent). The same note discloses the reverse flow: «Инвестментс»'s own borrowing is entirely from a single lender, ООО «ФКК» — three loan agreements dated 11.09.2023, 21.08.2024 and 24.10.2024, totalling ₽122.2m at 6–8% interest, with ₽9.33m in accrued unpaid interest by end-2024. This is the first primary-source evidence of a direct financial pipeline between ФКК (the Olympic-Fund-owned consultancy already tied to ДАР's 2011 founding charter) and the Наша Правда/«Инвестментс» side of the network — previously the only documented link between those two halves ran through shared office space and board membership, not money. Primary doc
  • Гумич's own FY2025 БФО filing (pulled directly, 18.08.2026) names who bought Инвестментс's 99% stake when it exited in December 2024 — and it's a Rostec subsidiary, independently confirmed via that entity's own EGRUL extract the same day. Current shareholders are Гаврилов Александр Валерьевич (25.0001%, also the company's GD) and ООО «РТ-Развитие бизнеса» (74.9999%, ОГРН 1147746392200, ИНН 7704861136) — a direct EGRUL pull confirms its sole participant, recorded 04.12.2025, is ГК «Ростех» itself (ИНН 7704274402, ОГРН 1077799030847), holding 100% at a nominal share value of ₽45.1bn. This is a second, independent primary-source-documented tie between the Наша Правда/«Инвестментс» financing side of this network and Ростех, distinct from the existing Технодинамика trust-management/Атлант Аэро chain elsewhere in this brief. Гумич itself now carries ₽179–191m in long-term borrowed funds against only ₽75.1m in contributed capital (уставный + добавочный) and a growing accumulated loss (-₽38.4m end-2025) — the same small-shell/large-financed-liability pattern already documented across this network, now sitting under Rostec's own investment arm rather than Инвестментс. Primary doc
What this does and doesn't establish

None of this shows the Спецтех/Системный интегратор/Инвестментс/Борей entities are themselves weapons producers in operation — Борей had zero revenue, and there is no evidence any of these companies has shipped anything. What it does establish is that the group's real corporate footprint includes defence/dual-use-coded manufacturing entities and an active internal financing arm moving tens of millions of rubles to affiliates — one of whom, АО «ХК Гумич», is now named for FY2024, though the ₽133m three-year loan total still has unnamed recipients for most of it — and that ФКК itself, not just Наша Правда, is directly bankrolling «Инвестментс» to the tune of ₽122.2m. The reason for sealing МПТМ's ownership register (below) remains the single most consequential open thread this pull created.

МПТМ and Наша Правда's own numbers

Both entities' FY2025 statements were pulled directly from ГИР БО (the state accounting-statements registry), not from an aggregator, and both undercut the marketplace's promotional framing.

МПТМ, in its first full year of operation: revenue of ₽1,002,000 against cost of sales of ₽1,310,000 — a gross loss of ₽308,000 on the core marketplace business. The bottom line only turns positive (₽18,000 net profit) because of ₽768,000 in non-operating income. The entire balance sheet is ₽4,592,000, of which ₽4,500,000 is a single long-term loan — МПТМ has essentially no assets beyond that loan, ₽1,024,000 of inventory and ₽329,000 cash. No fixed assets, no intangibles, no audit required (small-entity exemption).

Наша Правда, by contrast, is handling real money at scale: ₽984,892,000 received in 2025 (down from ₽1,232,513,000 in 2024), of which ₽592,889,700 was disbursed as “целевые пожертвования” (earmarked donations passed onward). Its bank balance at Газпромбанк fell from ₽314,049,235 (end 2023) to ₽109,860,117 (end 2024) to ₽194,720,594 (end 2025). Its own operating overhead is real and growing: ₽77,291,740 in general/admin costs for 2025 (up from ₽48,282,380 in 2024), against a staff of 22, including ₽42,185,020 in payroll, ₽5,926,500 on security services and ₽6,227,000 on vehicle rental. Its auditor, ООО «Машаудит», issued an unmodified opinion on 09.04.2026. Director Бровченко’s and the wider management team’s combined 2025 compensation was ~₽22.2m.

Наша Правда's own board, and what its warehouse actually holds

Наша Правда's FY2024 filing names its governing bodies in full Primary doc — a level of disclosure ДАР itself had already stopped providing by the time of its own FY2025 filing (above). Наблюдательный совет: Виктор Ильич Давыдов (ДАР), Илья Владимирович Елисеев (no employer listed — the same sanctioned individual who chairs ДАР's board, here holding a direct seat one level closer to the fund that actually procures and transfers equipment), Иван Игоревич Карабинский (Градислава), Дмитрий Владимирович Устратов («Фонд Поддержки нац. морских проектов» — a fourth foundation in this network, since independently confirmed via its own Minjust filing as the same Устратов Дмитрий Владимирович, GD since 30.04.2021), and Татьяна Сергеевна Антонова (no employer listed). Попечительский совет: Сергей Юрьевич Тихонов (ДАР), Роман Каллистратович Манецкий (Градислава), Марина Александровна Дукал (ДАР).

The same filing's inventory notes (§6, account 41) itemise roughly 10,400 units of equipment under an accounting note that states its purpose without ambiguity Primary doc:

В Дт 41 и 10 счетов указана сумма МПЗ, которые числятся за предприятием и находятся на складе по адресу: Московская область, г. Балашиха, ул. Чехова, дом 3 и г. Москва, Котельническая набережная, дом 25, строение 1, офис 18, комната 5. Данные средства по сч.41 были закуплены для безвозмездной передачи войсковым частям в зону СВО. Средства на счета 10 закупались для обеспечения жизнедеятельности нашей организации.Fund «Наша Правда», FY2024 ГИР БО filing notes §6 — verbatim

The itemised list behind that note is granular: counter-drone/EW jammers, thermal-imaging scopes, body armour, tactical communications headsets (branded, from manufacturer НПО «Милитех» — not previously identified in this investigation), FPV-drone components and batteries, and off-road vehicles, alongside medical supplies. Some individual line-item quantities in the source table are implausibly large relative to neighbouring entries and should be treated as possible OCR/scan artefacts rather than cited as exact figures without checking against the source PDF directly — but the note's own language, and the ~10,400-unit scale of the list as a whole, is not in doubt.

A war correspondent's own delivery record: eleven dated field deliveries

Военкор Z Лисицын ZOV-TV (@evgeniy_lisitsyn, a Roskomnadzor-registered war-correspondent channel, reg. 12.09.2022, 57K subscribers, run by Евгений Лисицын), fully scanned for this brief (9,836 posts, 17.08.2024–16.08.2026; no wildberries.ru/ozon.ru links found anywhere in the channel — expected, as this is a news/documentation channel, not a fundraising or procurement one), turns out to be a first-hand, independently dated visual-evidence source for the Наша Правда/ЦБСТ delivery partnership already documented above — eleven posts naming Наша Правда directly, 03.09.2024 through 07.08.2026, of which nine describe distinct delivery events with named units and itemised equipment (the other two are a holiday greeting and a tribute to the fund's logistics staff, with no equipment detail), consistently framed as joint hand-offs from "Единая Россия и фонд «Наша Правда»" (frequently alongside ЦБСТ by name) rather than either institution acting alone. The reporting names a wider circle of United Russia and government officials present at these hand-offs than documented elsewhere in this brief: Бекхан Барахоев and Виктор Водолацкий (State Duma deputies, 03.09.2024 and 06.06.2025 respectively), Зураб Макиев (State Duma deputy, present at four of the nine dated deliveries below, most recently both 07.08.2026 hand-offs), Денис Мирошниченко (Chairman, LNR People's Council, 06.06.2025), and Александр Хинштейн (врио губернатора Курской области, 14.05.2025 and 30.07.2025) — the first direct evidence in this brief of a sitting regional governor personally present at a Наша Правда/ЦБСТ hand-off. Lisitsyn is also identified, in his own posts, as Sидякин's "брат" (a term of political-fraternity, not blood relation, used repeatedly) — underscoring how tightly this channel's access is tied to Александр Сидякин specifically, already documented on ЦБСТ's supervisory board above.

Two new product names surface that are not yet resolved to a legal entity: a comms system called «Тень», described (14.05.2025) as the base station for «Скворец» drones supplied to the "Барс-Курск" volunteer detachment — a plausible match for this brief's still-open «СХ» ("Тень" spec-comms) lead, though not confirmed as the same entity — and a drone called «Ключ», delivered the same day to the 1st Guards Engineer-Sapper Brigade alongside «Арена 5М» demining detonators (ООО «Метаскан», already resolved above). A jamming/relay pairing, «Шорох» and «Грань», delivered 09.04.2025 to the 132nd Brigade, corroborates and field-confirms this brief's already-resolved «Локтар» (Дронница-exhibitor, still-open above) product line — «Шорох-У»/«Шорох» is named directly in Локтар's own Дронница-2023 self-description as a comms-relay complex; «Грань» is not otherwise documented and remains open. The same 09.04.2025 post also confirms field delivery of «КингПро» hardware (this brief's still-open «КИНГПРО» lead) to the 58th Army, гр. «Центр», 76th Guards Air Assault and 9th Motor Rifle brigades, alongside ЦБСТ's «Стратим» product line («Русак», «Голубь-Т», «Секира 13», «Воробей») — the first primary-source confirmation that «КингПро» hardware has actually reached named units, not just been announced. What the Minjust filings add: a second reporting channel, and a pass-through pattern

Every entity above also files a separate annual disclosure with the Justice Ministry (Форма №УН000Х, "Отчет о деятельности НКО") on top of its ГИР БО accounting statements — a second, independent reporting channel to the state, distinct from the tax-registry filings cited above. Pulling these directly for ДАР, Наша Правда, Градислава and ЦБСТ (FY2024–25) adds detail and, in one case, an independent corroboration of an existing finding.

ДАР's own Minjust filing corroborates the board-withholding pattern a second, independent way. Its FY2025 Форма №УН000Х report — a different filing regime from the ГИР БО notes quoted above — likewise leaves the Наблюдательный совет section blank, naming only director Давыдов. The same filing states ДАР conducted zero "программная деятельность" in 2025 — no programmes, no events, no collection boxes — while its accompanying spending section shows ₽1.6bn disbursed as "оказание финансовой помощи некоммерческим организациям" (financial aid to other NGOs) plus a further ₽141m to legal entities directly. Taken together this is consistent with ДАР functioning purely as a funder that moves money onward, not an organisation that runs its own activities. Of its FY2025 receipts, ₽1.083bn is coded as "целевые поступления... от граждан Российской Федерации" (targeted donations from citizens) — a striking figure with no donor-level breakdown given in the filing; treat as an open, unverified claim about the scale of individual giving, not confirmed fact.

Наша Правда's own Minjust filings show where a large share of its money comes from and where it goes, at a level of granularity the ГИР БО statements above don't provide: ₽1.04bn in 2024 and ₽923.5m in 2025, both years coded "от российских некоммерческих организаций" (from Russian NGOs) — and in 2025, ₽900.96m disbursed the same year, with ₽897.6m of that going to legal entities rather than individuals. Neither the upstream NGO source nor the downstream legal-entity recipients are named in these filings. By contrast, Наша Правда's own citizen-donation figure for 2025 was a comparatively modest ₽10.5m — a notably different income-mix presentation from ДАР's ₽1.083bn citizen-donation claim the same year.

ЦБСТ's own Minjust filing shows a third funding pattern again: its FY2025 report shows zero targeted or citizen donations — its entire ₽137.1m in receipts is coded "иные денежные средства... от российских юридических лиц" (other funds from Russian legal entities), with payer identities not disclosed. Its largest expense line was ₽128.7m for "оплата услуг сторонних организаций" (third-party service payments), vendors also not named. ЦБСТ's own filing additionally confirms, from its own primary source, direct equity participation in both ООО «Системный интегратор» (from 16.07.2025) and ООО «МПТМ» (from 22.01.2025) — independently corroborating the ownership already shown in the entity map, and giving МПТМ's exact participation start date for the first time.

ЦБСТ's own ГИР БО/Ресурс БФО filing (FY2024–25, pulled directly, distinct from the Minjust filing above) fills a gap every entity below it in the chain had already had closed: a small-entity, non-audited "упрощенная бухгалтерская отчетность," whose own notes state it does not apply ПБУ 11/2008 (the related-party-disclosure standard) at all — a fifth distinct non-disclosure posture in this network, alongside the cited-exemption pattern (ДАР, Градислава, the Olympic Fund) and the simply-omitted-section pattern (ФКК). The filing shows ЦБСТ's first-ever investment line (₽4.64m, FY2025 only) and a new, unitemised FY2025 spending category — ₽48.679m "на целевые мероприятия" (targeted programmes), up from zero in FY2024 — against cash falling from ₽152.0m to ₽3.4m over the same year, consistent with the spend-down already shown by the Minjust figures above.

All four entities' FY2024–25 Форма ОН0002 filings (the separate FZ-255 "foreign influence" disclosure) report zero funds or property from any foreign source — consistent across ДАР, Наша Правда and ЦБСТ, and worth noting as a completeness check even though it is, by its nature, a negative finding.

The ₽12bn fund’s manager was described only as “новая дочерняя структура ЦБСТ” at announcement, unnamed in both the PMEF report and the later summary. A direct ФНС/EGRUL pull now shows ЦБСТ’s actual current holdings run well beyond МПТМ: ООО «Системный интегратор» (100%, Kursk), and two joint-stock companies, АО «Спецтех Инвестиции» and АО «Спецтех Первый» (100% each, one Спецтех Инвестиции registration voided as erroneous by the registering authority). Registration dates rule both out as the fund's original manager: a fresh EGRUL pull (16 Aug 2026) shows Спецтех Инвестиции was registered 27.11.2025 and Спецтех Первый 10.04.2026 — five and ten months, respectively, after the June 2025 PMEF announcement. Neither has yet filed annual accounts (bo.nalog.ru returns nothing for either — resolved as simply too new to have a filing due, not a defence-classification access restriction: neither carries the "ограничение доступа" flag МПТМ's own founder field does). Either could plausibly have been stood up afterward for some other or related purpose, but the fund's original manager remains unidentified. Спецтех Инвестиции's own founding charter, requested directly from ФНС, adds a suggestive detail without closing the question: its stated purpose is explicitly to act as управляющий товарищ (managing partner) of инвестиционные товарищества (investment partnerships) and to manage holding companies and "финансово-промышленные группы" — functionally exactly the kind of role the ₽12bn fund would need, even though its registration date still rules it out as that specific fund's original vehicle. Whether it's a second-generation successor to an unidentified original manager, or an unrelated vehicle built for a similar purpose, is not established.

ООО «Системный интегратор»'s own founding documents, pulled directly from ФНС in full (both its original 08.07.2025 Устав and its amended 15.10.2025 redaction), date a substantive legal transformation this brief had not previously documented: the company acquired formal state-secret-handling status between the two filings. The original charter is boilerplate — no clause dealing with classified information anywhere in its thirteen sections. The amended version, approved by "Решением единственного участника... № 3 от «15» октября 2025 года" and entered in EGRUL 13.11.2025, adds an entire new compliance regime not present three months earlier: "Общество проводит работы, связанные с использованием сведений, составляющих государственную тайну, в соответствии с законом Российской Федерации от 21.07.1993 № 5485-1 «О государственной тайне»" (§2.3.1, verbatim — "The Company conducts work involving the use of information constituting a state secret"), a clause conditioning any participant's access to that information on formal clearance under 2024 government procedure (§4.5), and a clause making the Generaldirector personally responsible for "организацию работ, создание условий и защиту государственной тайны в Обществе" (§8.7) — plus a matching liquidation-clause addition requiring preservation of classified material and its physical carriers in the event of winddown (§13.3). This directly corroborates and dates finding 10's "unreported Kursk production entity with radar/EW OKVED codes": a 100%-ЦБСТ-owned company registered mid-2025 was not merely assigned a defence-adjacent activity code at founding, but was formally reconstituted three months later to handle classified work — the clearest documentary marker in this brief that a ЦБСТ subsidiary has moved from paper registration to active, secrecy-regulated defence production. Neither charter names any participant or founder in its body text (consistent with the standard post-2009 Устав format already documented at ФКК above); ownership is established via the separate EGRUL pull cited earlier in this section (100% ЦБСТ), not via the charter itself. Primary doc

АО «АУРИН»'s own founding charter (14.11.2023, requested directly from ФНС) adds no comparable finding — it is standard non-public-JSC boilerplate (consulting, holding-company management, and financial-services activity codes; no state-secret, licensing or classified-work clause anywhere in its ten sections) and, as expected for a post-2009 Устав, does not name its sole founder in the body text. It does not resolve or add to the founder-disavowal flag already documented on АУРИН's own EGRUL record above (Михайлова Ирина Валериевна, "сведения недостоверны," 12.08.2026) — that flag concerns a later EGRUL filing event, not this charter, and remains unresolved.

The ЦБСТ Supervisory Board

ЦБСТ published the full membership of its first supervisory board meeting on 14 February 2025. It is the clearest single statement of what this organisation is: a party structure, a state bank, two ministries, the Presidential technology apparatus and the Defence Ministry’s innovation directorate, in one room.

Sidyakin's own account (United Russia's "Переговорка" podcast, published 21.04.2025) fills in the narrative: he showed Medvedev drone work at an "Армия" forum, and Medvedev told him to formalize it into a real program — the direct personal origin of ЦБСТ's creation, not just a later endorsement. Андрей ТурчакThen Secretary of United Russia’s General Council; later Head of the Altai RepublicDelivered fund-purchased equipment to units on at least seven occasions from Jun 2023; the channel tracks his move to Altai in real time. Primary doc
Военкор Евгений ЛисицынEmbedded war correspondentDocumented Наша Правда front-line deliveries on at least nine occasions from Apr 2024. His footage is the richest untapped visual-evidence source identified so far. Primary doc
Устратов Дмитрий ВладимировичGeneral Director, Фонд поддержки национальных морских программ, since 30.04.2021Confirmed directly in the fund's own FY2025 Minjust filing — the same name and person as the "Фонд Поддержки нац. морских проектов" employer field on Наша Правда's own board roster, resolving the fourth-foundation lead person-to-person. A Russian Ministry of Interior NGO-affiliates registry (via OCCRP Aleph) additionally lists him as a member of the Winter Olympic Sports Support Fund itself, 2016–2019 — a third network tie for the same person, predating both his Наша Правда board seat and his Морских Программ directorship. Primary doc

The supply side

Who actually makes the goods

ЦБСТ describes itself as convening 525 enterprises and having supported more than 850 developers over two and a half years. The channel names a substantial subset of them, with products and in many cases named principals. Four are confirmed by ЦБСТ itself to be under EU sanctions — the channel reported the May 2025 designations and framed them as validation: “их техническое превосходство рассматривается как угроза в условиях военных действий”.

Two resident principals survived assassination attempts within eight days of each other in the summer of 2026, both attributed by ЦБСТ to Ukrainian services.

CompanyPrincipalProducts and notes
НПЦ «Ушкуйник»Алексей Чадаев«Князь Вандал Новгородский» fibre-optic FPV, stated capacity 5,000/month. EU-sanctioned May 2025. Built a strike-statistics system handed to the MoD. Chadaev appointed Advisor to the Minister of Transport, Jan 2026.
«Уралдронзавод»Владимир Ткачук«Упырь» FPV. Car bomb near Yekaterinburg, 5 Aug 2026 — Tkachuk critically injured, his driver-bodyguard killed.
«Овод» (Tula)Андрей Черезов (founder); Андрей Иванов (chief designer)«Овод», «Провод»/«Овод-Про» heavy FPV — serial deliveries confirmed to MoD warehouses. Cherezov shot three times in Tula, 29 July 2026, survived.
ООО «Авирон» (Moscow)Лапин Игорь Николаевич«Чебурашка» (first Russian single-board FPV), «Кощей 2.0» heavy transport, «Сокол» 18″ strike drone, «Пеппа» comms. Naval ground control station in use by army and navy. ИНН 7743451768, OKVED 26.51.2. Its own FY2024–25 filings independently confirm the GD name and show the growth curve: revenue ₽24.0m (2024, first operating year) → ₽273.9m (2025), net result flipping from a ₽3.2m loss to a ₽75.4m profit; long-term debt (₽9.9m at end-2024) fully repaid by end-2025. Small-entity/non-audited status. Primary doc
КБ «Гермес»—Spec-comms; «Гусь» interceptor; «Артемида-10»; short-range counter-drone MANPADS. Orbited its own satellite from Vostochny in a 52-satellite launch, Dec 2025.
ГК «Логос»—«Дикобраз» vehicle-mounted EW, disguised as civilian roof boxes; 400+ units fielded. Supplied to war correspondents from VGTRK, Channel One, Zvezda and NTV.
АО «Стратим» (Moscow)—«Русак-С» autonomous kamikaze drone, «Голубь» revolver-magazine bomber, «Тювик» mini loitering munition; revenue ₽1k→₽606.8m, 2023–25; named UBOs disclosed (Ястребов 51% indirect, Бежанов 37.2%).
АО «НПП БАТ» (Игорь Потапов, Moscow)—Named by ЦБСТ as a resident voice at its March 2025 session, but own filings show a dormant shell: 1 employee, zero revenue 2024–25.
ООО «КоптерЛаб» (Moscow)—ПАК «Форпост» radar-fused counter-drone interceptor system (up to 350 km/h). 80% parent АО «АУРИН» has a founder-disavowal flag on EGRUL — true owner undocumented.
АО «Технодрон» (Moscow)Николай Перфильев«Скворец» FPV drone family, incl. naval variant — the product name recurring most often across both channels. Founded 2024; 133 employees; ₽447.5m VAT + ₽300.5m profit tax paid FY2025. Fibre-optic «Скворец» supplied to the front; «Скворец ВМФ» entered Black Sea Fleet service 2025, developed with ЦБСТ support. EU-sanctioned, 20th pkg, Apr 2026.
ООО «Атлант Аэро» (Taganrog)Хутешен Алексей АлександровичFPV drones + «Орион» UAV components. 854 employees, ₽59.08bn declared income. 51% held via trust management by Rostec subsidiary АО «Технодинамика». EU-sanctioned, 20th pkg, Apr 2026.
ООО «Прометей» (Taganrog)Коновалов Роман ВладимировичBrand-new (registered 05.06.2026) aircraft manufacturer (ОКВЭД 30.30.39). Shares founder Шевченко Кирилл Игоревич with Атлант Аэро — a real corporate link between two same-city aircraft makers, not a naming coincidence. Хутешен Алексей Александрович (above) is a 25% founder here too. Found via a ФНС director/founder cross-reference on Хутешен, not ЦБСТ's own channel. Primary doc
ООО НПК «Атлант Аэро» (Taganrog)Хутешен Алексей АлександровичA direct sibling to the sanctioned Атлант Аэро above, registered 15.12.2023 — twelve days before it — under the same Хутешен/Шевченко ownership. Declared ОКВЭД is "Ремонт металлоизделий" (metal-goods repair), a plausible airframe-adjunct rather than the aviation code its sanctioned sister carries. Primary doc
ООО ИК «АВИАОК» (Taganrog)Степанов Валерий ЮрьевичLong-established (founded 2011, not a wartime shell); unlike the rest of this cluster, declares a genuine communications/radio-electronics-equipment manufacturing ОКВЭД block (26.30.11–26.30.19) alongside its nominal software-development code. Its own FY2021–25 БФО filings show real activity: small alternating profits/losses on undisclosed (simplified-filing) revenue through 2024, then ₽19.4m revenue / ₽674k profit, FY2025 — the only year revenue is disclosed. Surfaced one hop further out, via a director/founder screen on Степанов himself (director of ДС-БАРС-Т, a Хутешен-cluster corporate founder). Primary doc
ООО «Гаскар» (Moscow)Константинов Евгений АлександровичAircraft manufacturer, 477 employees, ₽10.02bn declared income. 100% owned by ООО «СИБНК» (see below). EU-sanctioned, 20th pkg, Apr 2026.
ООО «СИБНК» (Сибирская навигационная компания, Moscow)Ступников Андрей ГенадьевичГаскар's 100% parent; drones are a minority (19%) segment vs. cabling systems (79%). Project head Пыльский arrested Sep 2024, large-scale fraud, ₽164m frozen — undisclosed in FY24/25 filings, which state no legal proceedings exist.

Карпинского, ИЯИ РАН, ААНИИ) and St Petersburg city government IT-systems work. ООО «Боевые технологии» (Nizhny Novgorod)Потехин Владимир АлександровичAutomatic detect-and-jam counter-drone device; cited by Kommersant. ИНН 5262393781, OKVED 46.52 (electronics/telecom wholesale) plus ration-pack production and textile-wholesale codes — its founding-document history (pulled directly, 18.08.2026) confirms this isn't just a leftover secondary OKVED code: the entity was registered 18.12.2023 under the name ООО «Рационы питания» ("Food Rations LLC"), sole-founded by Потехина Светлана Алексеевна, and only renamed to «Боевые технологии» by a September 2024 charter amendment. Its own FY2024–25 filing independently confirms the GD's full name and, unusually for this network, discloses the ownership split directly: Потехин 66.67%, Потехина Светлана Алексеевна 33.33% (family-held, no external investor). Revenue ₽26.8m (2024, first year) → ₽164.7m (2025, 6.1×), but net profit collapsed from ₽3.06m to just ₽88k — gross profit of ₽41.3m was almost entirely absorbed by ₽38.7m in commercial expenses, a margin-compression pattern distinct from Авирон's parallel revenue scale-up. «Аляска»—«Алёша» FPV. Shown to Putin personally at the Zapad-2025 exercise, Mulino range.
ООО «Смарт Текнолоджис» (Penza)—«Метафора» inter-network repeater bridging incompatible radio systems.
ООО «КРАВТ» (kravt-studio.ru, St. Petersburg)—«Матрёшка» folding-arm FPV drone in smart-charging, heated tube launcher/containers; revenue ₽69m→₽300.6m, 2022–25.
АО «РОСС» (Moscow)Максимов Юрий АлексеевичИНН 7704097425. Soviet-successor entity (reg. 1992), renamed from «Радиоэлектронные и оптические спецсистемы» in 2021. Declared primary activity is consumer electronics, but its own EGRUL secondary codes include lawful-intercept/SORM communications equipment and «производство оптических прицелов и приборов определения координат целей» — optical weapon sights and target-coordinate devices, added 05.02.2021 — corroborated by a Minpromtorg aviation-technology development/production licence (2022) and an FSB cryptographic-means licence (2025). Its zakupki.gov.ru record shows a ₽31.5m single-source contract (Nov 2020) with the Federal Customs Service's own radio-electronic security directorate for an IR-band television surveillance system, fully executed (delivery act and payment order both on file), alongside 18 further smaller contracts to ФСБ regional directorates, Минобороны войсковые части and МЧС. Originally named in ЦБСТ resident material only as maker of «Взгляд»/«Наблюдатель» surveillance products, without a legal-entity match — the contract's own generic item description ("телевизионная система наблюдения ИК-диапазона") does not itself name either product, so the brand-to-entity link is plausible but not established. Partners with «Скворец» manufacturers; ONF claims 5,000+/month capacity scaling toward 10,000, ~10,000 units delivered.
