Кольская верфь registered only Dec 2024, OKVED is recreational/sport shipbuilding — a notable purpose mismatch, same pattern as other network entities' OKVED/activity gaps.
ООО «СУПЕРХРОНО» (Vologda)Струлев Игорь ЛеонидовичResolves this brief's previously-unnamed «СХ»/«Тень» lead: closed spec-comms system «Тень», letting FPV operators control drones from 500m–25km away without radio-signature exposure, used by «Скворец» FPV drones of the 810th Guards Naval Infantry Brigade (primary source: mil_hub channel, 24.09.2025, naming «СуперХроно» directly as "резидент ЦБСТ"). ИНН 3525389520, ОГРН 1163525102619, registered 07.11.2016 (a long-established firm, not a wartime shell), ул. Возрождения д. 34 оф. 50, ОКВЭД 26.11 (electronic-component manufacturing). FY2021–2025 БФО (nalog.gov.ru, user-supplied, 21.08.2026) shows a company far smaller than its combat-unit reach would suggest — pre-revenue through 2021, then revenue of just ₽900k (2022), ₽540k (2023), ₽1.40m (2024), ₽3.38m (2025), a scale mismatch worth flagging alongside this brief's other OKVED/activity-gap cases. No founding-document bundle available yet (nalog.gov.ru daily request limit hit).
ООО «ГОУДРОН» (godrone.ru, Moscow)Козлов Дмитрий Сергеевич«Набат v3» drone detector, third iteration, tested 04.08.2026 — 200–8800 MHz detection range, also intercepts analog video signal. Resolved 21.08.2026: ИНН 9721203814, ОГРН 1237700265846, registered 07.04.2023. FY2023–2025 БФО (nalog.gov.ru, user-supplied, 21.08.2026) shows revenue ₽86.3m (2023) → ₽178.3m (2024) → ₽55.9m (2025, a sharp decline), net profit thinning from ₽18.1m to ₽9.3m to just ₽512k over the same period. A full scan of its own channel (@GoDrone, 2,707 posts) turns up a direct, first-person confirmation of an ongoing ЦБСТ working relationship — commenting on ЦБСТ's «Яким» combat-testing detachment (09.12.2025): "Мы работаем с ЦБСТ не первый месяц и хорошо знаем, насколько быстро команда умеет превращать идеи в реальные инструменты для фронта" — verbatim, cross-linking to ЦБСТ's own channel post.
VA Technologies—«Сова» GPS/GNSS-denied autonomous navigation system for БпЛА, computer-vision-based, matches onboard camera imagery against satellite maps (03.08.2026). Not yet EGRUL-matched.
ООО «ИНТЭК БЕСПИЛОТНЫЕ СИСТЕМЫ» (uav.intecgroup.ru, Tomsk)Калиновский Никита Владиславович«Метеор-2» monitoring/reconnaissance quadcopter, shown at «Дрон Экспо» Kazan (09.07.2026), positioned as a domestic DJI Mavic analogue. Resolved 21.08.2026: ИНН 7000013316, ОГРН 1247000000488, registered 24.01.2024 — the drone-specific arm of the wider Группа компаний ИНТЭК (Tomsk), alongside sibling entities ООО «ИНТЭК ПРОМЫШЛЕННЫЕ СИСТЕМЫ» and ООО «ИНТЭК-ИНЖИНИРИНГ», both also directed by a Калиновский. Its FY2024–2025 БФО (nalog.gov.ru, user-supplied, 21.08.2026) shows no recorded revenue in either year (₽10k net loss in 2024, break-even in 2025) — the entity appears pre-revenue/newly spun-off, suggesting the actual «Метеор-2» commercial activity may run through a sibling ИНТЭК entity rather than this one.
ООО «ТИТАН - СЕВЕРО-ЗАПАД» (St Petersburg)Красковский Вячеслав Александрович«Эгеон» portable charging-station cases (ПЗС), production launched 17.04.2026. Resolved 21.08.2026 via эгеон.рус's own site: ИНН 7838380755, ОГРН 1077847345179 (registered 25.04.2007 — a long-established firm, not a wartime shell), Конногвардейский бульвар д. 3, operating under a generic ФНС Типовой устав №20 template (no bespoke founder disclosure). Its FY2021–2025 БФО filings (nalog.gov.ru, user-supplied, 21.08.2026) show steady revenue growth — ₽14.05m (2021) → ₽19.04m (2022) → ₽20.49m (2023) → ₽28.47m (2024) → ₽41.08m (2025) — but a swing from thin profitability (₽148k–₽2.56m net profit, 2021–23) into losses coinciding with the «Эгеон» launch: net loss ₽9.54m (2024), ₽6.67m (2025). Note possible name proximity to ЦБСТ's own unrelated «Титан» anti-drone turret product (see prose) — not established as connected.
Still open — named as residents but not yet resolved to a specific legal entity: «Динарус» (MANET drone-swarm networking — now resolved, see prose below), «Инстех» + Asel Labs (SPb, «Антагонист» drone — now resolved, see prose below), «Аэроплатформы» (aerostat testing — now resolved, see prose below), Лаборатория Гагаринг (comms relay — now resolved, see prose below), ООО «ЯРЛ» (non-metallic sapper kit — now resolved, see prose below), КРАМС (ЦБСТ residency now primary-source confirmed directly from its own channel, Kuzbass region confirmed via site+press+governor visit, still no legal entity — see prose below). ООО «НПО НЕБО» (ИНН 4800023371, product: «Скарабей»/СД138 octocopter, ₽190,000 disclosed price) is now resolved — see TSG prose above. «СХ»/«Тень» is now resolved — see «СуперХроно» row above. «КИНГПРО» (Секира Призрак) is resolved — see prose above (Physical infrastructure section) for ООО «КИНГПРО», ИНН 9725168367. «Аэромакс» is now resolved — ООО «Аэромакс» (ИНН 7725347482), subsidiary of ГК «Аэромакс», the 18.05.2026 rebrand of АО «Кронштадт» («Орион-Э» maker) — see EU-sanctions prose above.

АО «НИИ Современных телекоммуникационных технологий» is now resolved and independently EU/UN-sanctioned — АО «НИИ СТТ» (ИНН 6730046954, ОГРН 1026701461160, г. Смоленск, ул. Ново-Ленинградская, 10, founded 26.12.2002), found via an OCCRP Aleph search of the same Global Sanctions Database used for «Аэромакс». The EU's designation (CELEX 32025D0394/32025D0931; also UN-designated 19.07.2025 and listed on France's asset-freeze registry) states verbatim: "JSC 'Research Institute of Modern Telecommunication Technologies' specialises in the production of radio and television transmitting equipment. It also produces weapons and ammunition. The products of JSC 'Research Institute of Modern Telecommunication Technologies' are used by Russia in its war of aggression against Ukraine, in particular its unmanned aerial vehicle Merlin-VR." — naming a specific drone product (Merlin-VR) not previously documented in this brief for this résident. No primary EGRUL extract has been pulled — a nalog.gov.ru founding-document/EGRUL request for АО «НИИ СТТ» also returned "not digitized," the same wall as Ушкуйник-СП and Атлант Аэро — a primary EGRUL extract has now been pulled independently (nalog.gov.ru, user-supplied, 20.08.2026), confirming GD Богомолов Андрей Владимирович (director since 19.05.2016, personal ИНН 673103142483) and ₽5,000,000 charter capital directly. The extract's own registration history reveals a real defense-industrial lineage: the company was originally incorporated 26.12.2002 as ЗАО «НИЛ-9» ("Научно-исследовательская лаборатория №9" — Scientific Research Laboratory No. 9, a Soviet-style institute-naming convention), only appearing under its current «НИИ СТТ» name by the time of a 2016 charter restatement — the exact renaming date isn't captured in the extract's document log, but the "НИЛ-9" lineage itself is new to this brief. As a joint-stock company (АО), EGRUL does not disclose shareholders directly — ownership is tracked instead by a share registrar, АО «Независимая регистраторская компания Р.О.С.Т.» (a major nationwide Russian registrar), meaning the founders/owners remain genuinely unknown pending a separate registrar-level pull, not just an EGRUL gap. The company's OKVED and licensing record is the most heavily defense/security-coded of any entity in this brief: alongside the radio/TV-transmitter OKVED already known, the extract adds 25.40 "Производство оружия и боеприпасов" (weapons and ammunition manufacturing) and 26.51.2 (radar/radionavigation equipment) as registered activities, plus a striking license portfolio — an FSB license (since 2011, renewed 2015) for developing/producing/selling "специальные технические средства, предназначенные для негласного получения информации" (covert surveillance devices), a separate 2019 FSB cryptography/encryption license, a Минпромторг arms-development/production/testing/repair/disposal license (Л008-00102-77/00345416) and a second Минпромторг license specifically for military-equipment servicing, and — unique among every entity documented in this brief — an active Роскосмос space-activity license (originally granted 2017, most recently updated 2024). External research corroborates and sharpens the EU listing's Merlin-VR claim considerably: Russian tech press (N+1, Focus.ua, others) covering the drone's September 2021 unveiling describes it as a hybrid-engine reconnaissance UAV (10-hour endurance, 5km ceiling, 6.5kg payload, claimed low acoustic signature) succeeding an earlier institute project called «Филибастер» ("Filibuster") — a predecessor name not otherwise documented anywhere in this brief. Two Merlin-VR units have been shot down and examined by Ukrainian forces — June 2022 and 18 January 2023 (per Ukraine's General Staff) — with the June 2022 teardown finding the drone's actual engine was a standard internal-combustion unit rather than the claimed hybrid system, and, more significantly, foreign dual-use components inside: a French Lynred (Ulis) PICO1024 thermal imager and an Israeli-made lens — direct physical evidence that this EU/UN-sanctioned entity's own product depends on Western-sourced optical/thermal-imaging components, a live sanctions-circumvention/export-control angle distinct from the entity-identification work elsewhere in this brief. Only two examples are known to have been recovered across the full war to date, consistent with limited production or restricted deployment. No БФО filing exists for this ИНН under any year — independently reconfirmed via a direct query of bo.nalog.gov.ru's own open search API (20.08.2026, empty result), and kad.arbitr.ru's arbitration-case search returned a legal-access block (HTTP 451) from this pull, so no court-case cross-check was possible this pass. A batch of user-supplied kad.arbitr.ru filings (21.08.2026) partially fills that gap: АО «НИИ СТТ» appears as a defendant in disputes with Минобороны России (Дело №А40-257028/2021, ₽7,502 penalty claim under a 04.12.2020 state contract) and with МВД России (Дело №А40-85482/2020, penalty under state contract №1920188200102007708025358/12-2019/ЦСиСС, and Дело №А40-269303/2025, a related МВД unit — ФКУ «НПО "Специальная техника и связь" МВД России» — awarded ₽110,687 in penalties, 16.12.2025), and as a repeat plaintiff against АО «Кронштадт» (Дела №А40-204416/2025 and №А40-186273/2026, ₽220.6m and ₽38.1m respectively — a striking pair given «Кронштадт»'s own EU-sanctions/«Аэромакс» tie documented above, meaning two sanctioned drone-industry entities are also suing each other over money) and against a Smolensk-region subcontractor, ООО НПП «Новые технологии телекоммуникаций» (Дело №А62-7648/2025, contract-termination suit). One case matches the "18 ЦНИИ" lead directly: ФГУП «18 Центральный научно-исследовательский институт» Минобороны России (ОГРН 1037700091736) filed Дело №А40-119204/2019 against АО «НИИ СТТ» over a ₽1.23m penalty under a 2017 contract — the filing was returned for a procedural defect (missing contract copy) rather than substantively decided, but the pairing itself is a real institutional link: 18 ЦНИИ is a Minoborony research institute overseeing telecommunications/EW standards, procuring directly from this sanctioned entity. A follow-up batch of filings (21.08.2026) does surface one: Дело №А40-74708/2020, ФГКУ «Войсковая часть 68240» (the FSB's «Центр специальной техники», per its agentura.ru profile — the same unit named in the request) as plaintiff against АО «НИИ СТТ», decided in part in the plaintiff's favor at first instance (29.07.2020), appealed unsuccessfully through cassation, and refused a further hearing by the Supreme Court of the Russian Federation (Определение №305-ЭС21-8269, 15.06.2021) — a genuine FSB-НИИ СТТ contractual link, though the underlying claim amount and subject aren't recoverable: every substantive document in the case, including the Supreme Court's own determination, is fully redacted to header-and-caption text only (a closed-session marking, "Судебный акт принят в закрытом судебном заседании"), leaving only the case card (Электронное правосудие "Карточка дела," which lists parties/courts/judges/procedural-step dates but no claim content) as a usable source — consistent with routine redaction of FSB-counterparty commercial litigation rather than anything specific to this entity. Two further case numbers turned up in the same batch, №А40-168087/2019 (₽7.59m, per rusprofile's earlier summary) and №А40-209485/2024 (three-instance history through July 2025), but both are redacted to bare headers with no case-card equivalent supplied, so their parties remain unconfirmed — not established as FSB cases, just unresolved. kad.arbitr.ru's own search API remains legally blocked (HTTP 451) for a direct party-name query that could otherwise settle this. АО «НИИ СТТ»'s zakupki.gov.ru contracting record adds a further, separately-sourced FSB customer link, distinct from the «Войсковая часть 68240» litigation above. Its largest recurring customer cluster there is «Войсковая часть 35533» — 45 contracts, ₽168.4m combined, 2013–2015 — which independent cross-referencing now identifies as ЦНИИСТ / «НИИ-1», a Balashikha-based special-technology research institute with FSB lineage. The identification rests on five independent sources: a live spark-interfax.ru corporate-data pull states the entity's ОКОГУ (governing-body classifier) field directly as "Федеральная служба безопасности Российской Федерации" — a structured registry field, not a text mention; a specialist KGB-directorate structural-history site (shieldandsword.mozohin.ru) traces ЦНИИСТ's institutional lineage to a 1954/55 KGB reorganisation; an environmental/accreditation registry (onlineecology.com) independently confirms the same ИНН (5012009279) as a testing laboratory accredited specifically for transport-security inspection equipment; a saby.ru pull confirms the entity is still active as of 01.09.2026; and a collectibles-marketplace listing for a "90 лет НИИ-1 ФСБ России" commemorative badge independently pairs the same "НИИ-1" designation with FSB and в/ч 35533. If the identification holds, this raises АО «НИИ СТТ»'s decade-plus в/ч 35533 relationship from routine radio-electronics supply to a sustained vendor relationship with an FSB internal technical institute — though it should be read alongside, not as a substitute for, the already-established «Войсковая часть 68240» litigation link, since the two are separate FSB counterparties. «Динарус» is now resolved, with a direct ЦБСТ tie confirmed from ЦБСТ's own channel, not inferred. The same 09.06.2026 ЦБСТ channel post already cited above for «Площадь» names «Динарус» explicitly as a MANET (Mobile Ad-Hoc Network) drone-swarm-control platform and states outright that its developer, компания «Динарус», was a finalist of Cohort II of ЦБСТ's own «Акселератор проектов двойного назначения» (dual-use projects accelerator) — a primary-source residency confirmation, the same class of evidence already used elsewhere in this brief. The company's own site (dinarus.ru) names the legal entity directly and its own downloadable "Карточка предприятия" (company requisites card) supplies full corporate detail without needing a separate EGRUL pull: ООО «Центр Динарус» (ИНН 5022077503, ОГРН 1245000015952, КПП 772701001), registered address 117218, г. Москва, ул. Большая Черёмушкинская, д. 34, помещ. 2/1Н/2, GD Тютина Ольга Анатольевна, banking with АО «Альфа-Банк». A direct query of ФНС's own egrul.nalog.ru open search API independently confirms the same director and ОГРН, plus the registration date — 01.02.2024, a young company. ФНС's bo.nalog.gov.ru open БФО-summary endpoint confirms one filing exists, FY2025 (filed 26.03.2026): ₽920,000 revenue — small, consistent with a company not yet two years old. The site frames the platform as a broader commercial MANET/radio-module technology licensed to partner manufacturers (emergency response, industrial IoT, mining, rail, tactical radio use cases listed alongside the UAV-swarm application ЦБСТ showcased), OKVED 62.02 (computer-consultancy) rather than a hardware-manufacturing code — consistent with a software/IP-licensing business model rather than its own drone production. Founders/ownership structure remains undisclosed — but a founding-document pull (nalog.gov.ru, user-supplied, 21.08.2026, two filings: original 24.01.2024 charter plus a minor 2025 address-format amendment) at least confirms the company began with multiple founders, not a sole one — the charter was approved by a "Общего собрания учредителей" (plural founders' meeting), with charter capital of ₽100,000 at incorporation (since presumably unchanged, no later capital-increase filing found); no names appear in either document, the same non-disclosure pattern as every other post-2009 charter reviewed in this network, and the sole amendment on file is a routine legal-address reformatting, not a substantive change. The same ЦБСТ post also names two further leads not yet otherwise documented in this brief, flagged here for a future pass rather than resolved: «Ориент. Коршун», an autonomous secure-comms coordinate-sharing system for UAV/combat-unit operational groups, developed by "малая технологическая компания Orient Systems (4GNSS)" — the developer is now resolved to two linked legal entities: its own site (orsyst.ru) names ООО «ОРИЕНТ СИСТЕМС» (ИНН 7743939185, ОГРН 5147746083491, registered 12.09.2014) as the operating company and a sibling, ООО «4ГНСС» (ИНН 7743128980, ОГРН 5157746074272), both sharing an office in the Сколково Innovation Center (Большой бульвар, 42к1/3.302); a founding-document pull (nalog.gov.ru, user-supplied, 21.08.2026) for the first entity shows a single sole founder, Шелаев Денис Владимирович, and a 2015 amendment raising charter capital to ₽20,000 — notably, the original 2014 charter includes confidentiality clauses specifically covering "сведения, составляющие государственную тайну" (state-secrets-classified information) and access-form ("форма допуска") requirements for participants, an unusual provision for what's otherwise a generic wholesale-electronics/software-consulting purpose clause, suggesting a defense-adjacent business line predating the ЦБСТ tie by roughly a decade. FY2021–2025 БФО filings (pulled directly) show a small but real and steadily growing commercial operation: revenue ₽82.1m (2021) → ₽90.6m (2022) → ₽108.1m (2023) → ₽120.7m (2024) → ₽33.2m (2025, partial-year filing), net profit ₽29.2m/₽17.3m/₽26.8m/₽56.9m/₽18.9m respectively — none of which independently confirms the «Коршун» product specifically, since the company's own public-facing site (a civilian GNSS-receiver business — geodesy, agriculture, hydrography, mining) makes no mention of it; the ЦБСТ channel post remains the only source for that product, consistent with a defense line kept off public marketing, the same pattern already seen at Asel Labs above. ООО «4ГНСС»'s own founding-document history adds a real corporate-lineage finding: a founding-document pull (nalog.gov.ru, user-supplied, 21.08.2026, four charter versions 2015–2022) shows the company was originally incorporated 23.11.2015 under a different name, ООО «КомНавРус» — "Ком" almost certainly short for "коммерческая," "НавРус" for "навигация России" (Russian navigation) — only renamed to «4ГНСС» by a 04.02.2022 decision (registered 11.02.2022), a rebrand that predates this brief's ЦБСТ connection by roughly three years. As with the sister entity, no founder name appears anywhere in the charter text across any of the four versions (sole-founder/sole-participant language throughout, standard post-2009 non-disclosure). FY2021–2025 БФО filings (pulled directly) show a larger and similarly healthy commercial trajectory than ООО «ОРИЕНТ СИСТЕМС»: revenue ₽65.6m (2021) → ₽96.7m (2022) → ₽132.0m (2023) → ₽152.2m (2024) → ₽134.1m (2025, partial-year), net profit growing from ₽13.3m to a ₽48.0m peak in 2024. A Telegram channel scan (@orient4gnss) has been requested to look for further corroboration. Neither entity's public financials or founding documents mention «Коршун» specifically, consistent with the off-books-defense-line pattern noted above rather than contradicting it. And an unnamed "интеллектуальный комплекс противодействия системам радиосвязи и БпЛА" (counter-comms/counter-drone complex) demonstrated at the same event without an attributed manufacturer. «Аэроплатформы» is now resolved — ООО «Аэроплатформы» (ИНН 5027293811, Люберцы, Московская область; registered 26.02.2021), identified via list-org.com by matching OKVED 30.30.2 ("Производство аэростатов и беспилотных летательных аппаратов") against the résident tag "aerostat testing," and confirmed against a full-text ЦБСТ Telegram post already in this project's scraped database (channel 2159491523, msg 2184, 04.06.2026 — also republished on ТАСС per the post's own text) describing the company's high-altitude free balloons and SVO-tested tethered relay balloons in explicit military-delivery terms: "такие аппараты можно применять для доставки в нужную локацию и сброса БпЛА, планирующих авиабомб, военных грузов" ("such craft can be used to deliver and drop UAVs, glide bombs, military cargo") to any point up to two days' flight away. GD Власов Артём Аркадьевич; founders are Власов (51%), АО «Стратолинк» (35%, director Оксман Николас Николаевич, same individual as a 14% minority founder here), and Оксман himself (14%). The director is now primary-source confirmed — a direct query of ФНС's own official egrul.nalog.ru search API (no captcha, no auth required for basic search) independently returns the identical name, ОГРН 1215000018650 and registration date 26.02.2021, upgrading this from a secondary-aggregator finding; the extract document itself remains behind the same session-gated download wall as elsewhere in this network. Full FY2021–2025 БФО filings, pulled directly (20.08.2026), tell a striking growth story: the company was functionally dormant for its first four years — effectively zero revenue and a few thousand rubles' profit/loss each year (₽40k loss in 2021, ₽2k profit in 2022, ₽102k revenue/₽58k profit in 2023, ₽0 revenue in 2024) — before a sudden real commercial year in 2025: ₽5.305m revenue, ₽2.189m net profit (a 41% margin), with total balance-sheet assets jumping from ₽56,000 to ₽2.269m over the same year. This timing lines up closely with the company's ЦБСТ-résident product debut (the June 2026 ЦБСТ channel post already documented above), consistent with a shell that sat mostly inactive until it found a real customer. The FY2025 notes state the company is exempt from ПБУ 11/2008 (related-party disclosure) as a small enterprise — no ownership/related-party detail beyond what's already known from list-org. A more precise address is now available from the same filing pull: г. Люберцы, п. Малаховка, ул. Калинина, д. 30, стр. 3, оф. 5. КРАМС now has a documented product lineup, a confirmed direct ЦБСТ tie, and two associated legal entities. A full scan of its own channel (@krams_pro, 137 posts, Jul 2024–Jul 2026) gives two unambiguous primary-source residency statements, not just a self-identification: a 10.12.2025 post titled "КРАМС - резидент АНО «ЦБСТ»" states outright "Компания «КРАМС» является резидентом АНО «Центр беспилотных систем и технологий»", and a 25.03.2026 post confirms participation in "3 поток Акселератора спецтехпроектов ЦБСТ" (cohort 3 of ЦБСТ's own accelerator programme). The channel's product lineup spans 7"/9"/10"/13"/15"-frame FPV quadcopters (branded Крамс 7/9/10/13/15), a 34"/36" heavy multirotor under development («КРАМС-34»), a hexacopter («ЛЮСТРА-10»/«САЯН-30»), Mavic-3-compatible payload-drop systems («Колун», «Дуплет»), a low-cost improvised munition-drop kit («ЛЕГО»), in-house Li-ion battery-pack assembly, carbon/textolite/aluminium frame manufacturing at scale (3,000+ frames shipped per batch by 2026, aiming for 10,000/month), and a field-hardened laptop/monitoring-case product line — reporting 14,000+ payload-drop units and tens of thousands of frames shipped since the war began, cites Росгвардия testing certificates, and describes itself in one post as effectively "научно-исследовательское производство" (an R&D-driven manufacturer, not just an assembler). Geography is now resolved for the first time: krams.pro's own contacts page gives a region, Кемеровская область-Кузбасс, independently confirmed by the listed phone number's own carrier-registration region; the channel independently corroborates this with a 16.12.2024 post describing equipment/curriculum support for the Киселёвский политехнический техникум (Kiselyovsk, Kemerovo Oblast) and a 09.02.2025 post noting a visit from "губернатор области." Press coverage independently confirms and dates that visit: Sibdepo and the Novokuznetsk city administration's own site (admnkz.info) report Kuzbass governor Илья Середюк personally visiting КРАМС's production facility (05.02.2025), citing up to 300 drones/month, 10 multirotor types, 12,000+ payload-drop units and 15,000+ carbon frames produced, training conducted at 7 military-base centres plus classrooms in Kuzbass and Buryatia, and staff drawn partly from Novokuznetsk technical-college students — corroborating the channel's own production claims from an independent, official source. No legal-entity name, ИНН, or address appears in any of these sources — not the channel, not krams.pro (whose privacy-policy page is unfilled placeholder text), not the press coverage. A bare-name EGRUL search for «КРАМС» returns 21 nationwide entities, none in Kemerovo Oblast — the closest candidate checked, ООО «КРАМС» (ИНН 9701247080, Moscow), is a construction-services shell (OKVED 43.29, no UAV/electronics activity, in tax arrears and briefly flagged for involuntary EGRUL strike-off in 2024) and is not this entity; a coincidental-name-collision, same category as this brief's earlier Аякс-Технологии and discarded-«Аэромакс»-candidate cases. The consistent absence of any legal suffix ("«КРАМС»" branding only, never "ООО «КРАМС»") across every source checked was itself suggestive of a sole-proprietor (ИП) structure rather than an ООО — but a reverse search of krams.pro's own listed contact phone (+7 923 618-75-94) surfaced two Kemerovo Oblast entities sharing a director instead: ООО «ПРОММАШ» (ИНН 4217210280, ОГРН 1244200002970, г. Новокузнецк, ул. ИНСТЕХ's own site, instechno.ru, self-describes as "Группа компаний ИНСТЕХ" (a group of companies) reselling the full aSel Labs product line alongside its own in-house counter-UAV/security-systems development, targeting state-sector and municipal ("умный город") customers — its registered OKVED, 43.21 ("electrical installation work"), is evidently boilerplate and doesn't reflect this actual business, the same registration-code-vs-reality gap seen elsewhere in this brief. Neither company site mentions «Антагонист» by that name, but tracing this brief's original still-open entry back to its actual source resolves it cleanly: a 13.03.2026 ЦБСТ channel post (channel 2159491523, msg 1966, already in this project's database) covering the «Безопасность ТЭК» forum at ВДНХ states directly, "Необычный дрон-перехватчик на форуме «Безопасность ТЭК» продемонстрировали петербургские компании «Инстех» и Asel Labs. The post describes «Антагонист» as an X-wing quadrotor interceptor with autonomous target-acquisition/attack-pattern selection, a fragmentation warhead or net-launcher payload, ₽10,000 per reusable shot, and positions it as the top tier of a four-layer counter-drone system called «Аура» ("сетецентрическая платформа управления — разведки — подавления," a networked command-recon-suppression platform) — none of which appears on either company's own commercial site, consistent with a defense-oriented product line kept off public marketing. aSel Labs itself is now also directly identified as a legal entity, separate from both ИНСТЕХ (its OEM reseller partner, above) and the Moscow dealer running assellabs.ru (a second, similarly-named "Asel Labs" storefront — confirmed via its own site footer as ИП Фёдоров Павел Вячеславович's domain, the same "Горизонт" dealer already named on aSel Labs' own contacts page, not a separate company): a Wildberries seller lookup (seller ID 250130605, wildberries.ru/seller/250130605) resolves via WB's own supplier-lookup API directly to ООО «АСЕЛ ЛАБС» (ИНН 9718278129, ОГРН 1257700126958, КПП 771801001, registered 17.03.2025, Москва, пл. Сокольническая, д. 4А, помещ. 11/4), independently confirmed via ФНС's egrul.nalog.ru open API (GD Проценко Артём Витальевич, same ОГРН/date) with OKVED 27.90 ("производство прочего электрического оборудования," i.e. general electrical-equipment manufacturing) — a plausible match for drone-detector hardware, unlike ИНСТЕХ's boilerplate electrical-installation code. This project's own Telegram database independently corroborates aSel Labs' product circulating through SVO-adjacent volunteer-procurement channels well before this ООО's March 2025 registration (a 13.08.2024 post selling the detector "Только для СВО" at ₽90,000 via a Podolsk contact, and an 24.08.2024 post from channel 1737759730 — the same ЦБСТ-adjacent channel later confirmed for «Гагаринг» — describing a detector unit and a "Vigor" booster handed to a frontline "Южный фронт" volunteer group), meaning either an earlier, unregistered/informal operation predates the ООО, or sales ran through a different, not-yet-identified legal vehicle before March 2025. A founding-document pull (nalog.gov.ru, user-supplied, 21.08.2026) is a single document, a plain-template Устав approved by "Протоколом № 1 от 12 марта 2025г. учредительного собрания" days before registration, confirming the charter capital directly: ₽1,000,000. No founder names appear anywhere in the body — standard for this document type — and no amendments exist yet, consistent with an under-a-year-old company. The charter itself is entirely generic boilerplate (standard ООО governance clauses, no defence/weapons-specific purpose language), offering no independent corroboration either way for the counter-drone business described on the company's own site and the ЦБСТ channel post above. The FY2025 БФО filing (pulled directly, 21.08.2026) is the company's first and only filing, confirming it as functionally pre-revenue as of that filing: no revenue line at all, ₽300,000 cost of sales, ₽19,000 other expenses, a net loss of ₽319,000 for the year, and a balance sheet of ₽619,000 cash plus ₽63,000 receivables against ₽681,000 equity — exactly the ₽1,000,000 charter capital minus the year's loss, arithmetically consistent and confirming no debt/liabilities yet. GD Проценко Артём Витальевич signs both statements, matching the EGRUL record. Nothing here contradicts the counter-drone manufacturing narrative — a company incorporated in March and still burning founder capital by year-end is an ordinary early-stage profile, not a red flag. The sourcing ЦБСТ post itself is a direct, primary-source ЦБСТ-channel confirmation of coverage — but a narrower one than this brief's other résident confirmations: unlike the same post's explicit "КБ «Гермес» (резидент ЦБСТ)" label for a different company, «Инстех» and Asel Labs are described only as exhibitors at the forum, without the word "резидент" attached — so the tie is "featured directly by ЦБСТ's own channel," not "self-identified or ЦБСТ-labeled résident," a real but more modest distinction worth preserving rather than rounding up. No post in this project's database names aSel Labs, ИНСТЕХ, or assellabs.ru as a РОЙ.МАРКЕТ vendor specifically — the WB marketplace presence is a distinct commercial channel from РОЙ.МАРКЕТ itself and doesn't on its own establish that tie; worth checking against a fresh РОЙ.МАРКЕТ vendor listing, not folded in as confirmed. Лаборатория Гагаринг is now resolved — ООО «Гагаринг» (ИНН 2312321660, Краснодар; registered 04.09.2023), identified via its own site (gagaring.ru, a self-described "маркетплейс электроники для БПЛА и FPV" operated by "Гагаринг. Лаборатория электроники") and confirmed against multiple ЦБСТ Telegram posts already in this project's database (channels 1737759730 and 2159491523, 02.05.2026 and 06.06.2026) naming it directly as a résident: "Инжиниринговая компания «Лаборатория Гагаринг» интегрировала в модуль связи «Кузнечик» режим «Подхват». Он позволяет удвоить радиус боевой работы дронов и наземных роботизированных платформ" ("...integrated a 'Podkhvat' mode into its 'Kuznechik' comms module, doubling the combat operating radius of drones and ground robotic platforms") — with the encrypted control channel pitched as immune to enemy interception, and coverage cross-posted from ТАСС and «Первый Технический». GD/co-founder Барашков Александр Сергеевич (50.5%), co-founder Александров Алексей Витальевич (49.5%); main OKVED 26.11 (electronic-component manufacturing) with additional codes spanning 30.30/30.30.39 (aerostat/UAV production) and 62.01 (software), consistent with the site's own description as both manufacturer and marketplace aggregator. Separately, this project's database already documents the same «Лаборатория Гагаринг» (via its earlier channel handle @my_el_lab) as a direct equipment donor to «Проект Архангел» in April 2024 — a supply relationship, not a corporate link, but worth noting given «Архангел»'s own adjacent-network status in this brief. Update: a primary EGRUL/founding-document pull has now been made (nalog.gov.ru, user-supplied, 19.08.2026) — both the original 04.09.2023 charter (Решение №1/2023 Единственного учредителя, sole-founder incorporation, generic purpose clause, ₽10,000 capital) and the 05.03.2025 restated version (Протокол Общего собрания учредителей (участников), confirming the shift to the two-founder structure already seen in EGRUL — Александров joined between incorporation and restatement) have been reviewed via OCR; charter capital is unchanged at ₽10,000 across both versions, and neither carries drone/EW-specific purpose language, the same pattern as every other founding document reviewed in this network. A full scan of @my_el_lab (3,510 posts, Aug 2024–Aug 2026, «Лаборатория Гагаринг»'s earlier channel handle, requested by user 19.08.2026) substantially expands this profile. The company's own stated production scope is broader than the «Кузнечик»/«Подхват» comms tie already documented above — a May 2026 post states it develops "БПЛА (включая БПЛА самолетного типа), БЭК, наземные РТК, РЭР, РЭБ, системы связи" (drones including fixed-wing, unmanned boats, ground robotic platforms, EW/ELINT and comms systems) and that it "сотрудничаем с Министерством Обороны, добровольческими отрядами и другими силовыми структурами" (cooperates with the Ministry of Defence, volunteer detachments, and other security agencies using its products). Separately, the company runs a state-accredited training centre («Учебный центр «Гагаринг»», est. per a 23.05.2026 post) issuing government-standard certificates ("свидетельство государственного образца") for UAV operator, UAS maintenance technician, and radio-electronics repair specialist roles; named instructors include Анна Тараносова, Артём Толстоусов, Александр Смородин and Сергей Иванкин, running a counter-drone marksmanship course («Последний рубеж») for, among others, Black Sea Fleet personnel (300+ trained per an August 2025 post). GD Барашков Александр Сергеевич is also a recurring guest on Vladislav Shurygin's Соловьёв LIVE programme "На войне как на войне" (at least two appearances, 20.06.2025 and 25.07.2025), discussing the UAV/EW industry and, in his own words, "конкуренция в системе" and procurement dysfunction. In a blunter September 2024 post, Барашков personally accused a body he names "УПМИ" of falsely claiming to procure the company's «Юрка» detector for the Army, and alleged that defence-innovation support funnels overwhelmingly to established primes — "Все бабло идёт по проторенным дорожкам: ростехи, калашниковы, антеи и алмазы и иже с ними" ("All the money goes down the well-worn paths: the Rostecs, Kalashnikovs, Anteys, Almazes and the like") — and dismissed a Народный фронт technology council as staffed by the same insiders. A later December 2024 post partially walks this back, noting improved dealings once a Krasnodar peer company's relationship with "УПМИ" (unexpanded acronym, not otherwise identified in this scrape — likely an MoD procurement/innovation body) began working. Presented here as the company's own on-record public statements, not independently verified allegations of wrongdoing. Update: primary ГИР БО/Ресурс БФО filings for FY2023–2025 have now been pulled directly (nalog.gov.ru, user-supplied, 19.08.2026), resolving the unit ambiguity above in list-org's favour: the filing itself explicitly states "Единица измерения: Тыс. руб." and — as a direct sanity check — reports charter capital as "10" against this same unit, exactly matching the known ₽10,000 EGRUL figure. Revenue and profit have grown explosively: ₽1.25m revenue / a ₽514k loss in FY2023 (a partial year, incorporated 04.09.2023), ₽84.5m revenue / ₽15.4m net profit in FY2024, and ₽2.159 billion revenue / ₽664.2 million net profit in FY2025 — a roughly 25× revenue jump year-on-year, on a company that never recorded a capital increase in either charter version reviewed above. The FY2025 balance sheet shows total assets of ₽1.041 billion (inventory ₽347.8m, receivables ₽345.1m, cash ₽162.3m) against retained earnings of ₽679.0m, all signed by Барашков Александр Сергеевич as руководитель. This scale of growth is consistent with — and gives hard financial confirmation to — the company's own public narrative above of rapid wartime-driven expansion from a "гаражники"-era startup. A later June 2026 post from Барашков (t.me/my_el_lab/3859, 29.06.2026) gives concrete R&D figures matching this growth: "Мы вкладываем значительные средства в развитие и технологии, порядка 2-5% от выручки. У нас в штате уже более 20 инженеров... 'Кузнечик' образца марта 2026 года — это уже совершенно другая машина по сравнению с той, что была в 2023" ("We invest significantly in R&D, around 2-5% of revenue. We now have more than 20 engineers on staff... the March 2026 'Kuznechik' is already a completely different machine compared to the 2023 one") — confirming continuous product iteration on «Кузнечик» since its 2023 introduction and a 20+ engineer headcount consistent with the FY2025 financial scale above. The training-centre operation now has its own site, gagaring-school.ru ("Учебный центр Гагаринг"), which links directly back to t.me/my_el_lab in its footer, confirming the same operation as the parent electronics business. Its offer-agreement template (/oferta) is otherwise unfilled (blank name/address/ОГРНИП fields) but carries one populated field — ИНН 434540800305, belonging to ИП Куртеев Александр Сергеевич, a name not otherwise connected to ООО «Гагаринг» or Барашков anywhere else in this brief. A primary ЕГРИП extract and reestr-МСП record (nalog.gov.ru, user-supplied, 19.08.2026) show ИП Куртеев Александр Сергеевич registered since 13.09.2007 (ОГРНИП 307434525600161), resident in Сочи/Дагомыс (Краснодарский край, same region as Гагаринг though a different city), a микропредприятие whose main OKVED — added 11.01.2016, years before ООО «Гагаринг» existed — is 85.42.9 ("дополнительное профессиональное образование", i.e. supplementary vocational training), alongside legacy retail codes (tea/coffee, general goods) from the original 2007 registration. This supports rather than resolves the white-label-platform reading above: a long-established small training-services IP is a plausible independent course-platform operator, not obviously connected to Барашков or ООО «Гагаринг» by anything in these two documents — still flagged as unresolved, not a confirmed link. Site visuals for both addresses (user-supplied screenshots, 19.08.2026): ООО «Гагаринг»'s registered address, ул. им. Фадеева, д. 429/3, Краснодар, is an ordinary residential apartment-block entrance with no visible company signage — consistent with a registered-office-only address, not a production floor (Гагаринг's actual manufacturing/office space, if separate, is not yet located). Цезаря Куникова, 24к2, with ground-floor glass storefronts and upper-floor office space, confirmed via Yandex Maps' own listing as "Further education"/"Educational center" with a direct phone number, +7 (929) 832-38-88, and a link back to gagaring-school.ru. Барашков also directs a five-member industry association, «АРРС», that ООО «Гагаринг» itself co-founded. The user pointed to аррс.рф, which blocks direct fetches (403 at the server level); its full name — Ассоциация разработчиков перспективных систем радиоэлектронной разведки, радиоэлектронной борьбы и связи (АРРС, "Association of Developers of Advanced ELINT/EW/Comms Systems") — surfaced from a January 2025 РЭБ how-to guide already in this project's scraped database, self-identifying its author as an АРРС member. list-org.com resolves it to a real legal entity: ОГРН 1242300011469, registered 01.03.2024, address ул. Лавочкина, д. 7/1, Краснодар — the same Пашковский district as ООО «Гагаринг»'s own registered address. General Director: Барашков Александр Сергеевич (same individual as Гагаринг's GD). Its five founding members are ООО «Гагаринг» itself (ИНН 2312321660), ООО «АКР «Бегак»» (Авиационно-космические разработки «Бегак», ИНН 2632116476, Пятигорск, director Бегак Александр Аркадьевич), ООО «Прайм» (ИНН 7735565264, Moscow, director Баранова Ольга Владимировна — no obvious defense/UAV tie by name alone, not yet otherwise researched), and two individuals, Сиразатдинов Никита Вадимович and Климов Максим Александрович — both new names, not otherwise appearing in this brief. Барашков's own ИП history has now been pulled directly (primary ЕГРИП extract + reestr-МСП record, nalog.gov.ru, user-supplied, 19.08.2026): ИНН 252201766438, ОГРНИП 313251103800105, originally registered 07.02.2013 not in Краснодар but in Приморский край (МИФНС №9), with tax jurisdiction only moving to Краснодар (ИФНС №4) on 20.03.2018. His registered activity history is a long, generic small-business record predating «Гагаринг» by a decade and unrelated to electronics/defense at the time: freight/courier transport and cargo handling (2013, 2015), then "управление финансово-промышленными группами" and business consulting (added 2016), briefly even "деятельность танцплощадок, дискотек, школ танцев" (dance-hall/disco/dance-school activity, added 2018), before retail codes were added in 2024–2025. No УАV/electronics-specific OKVED appears on the ИП itself — that activity runs entirely through ООО «Гагаринг» — consistent with his own public narrative of a pre-war "гаражники" background unrelated to defense electronics before the war created the opportunity. Categorised as a микропредприятие, entered the reestr-МСП on 10.11.2023, resident in Краснодар. АО «Росс» is now resolved, with a direct ЦБСТ-network delivery link. Its site, ross-jsc.ru (a JS-rendered React app whose content isn't in the initial page load — pulled from the bundled JS instead), describes an optical/radio-electronic special-systems developer active "с 1992 года" (since 1992), fulfilling over 80 Гособоронзаказ (state defense order) R&D contracts, holding an ФСБ state-secrets-work license (Moscow/Moscow Oblast, reg. №41275), an aviation-technology development/production/repair license (№14943-АТ), and — notably — a license "на осуществление разработки, производства, реализации и приобретения в целях продажи специальных технических средств, предназначенных для негласного получения информации" (development/production/sale of technical means for covert information-gathering, reg. №100), quality-certified to ГОСТ Р ИСО 9001-2015 and ГОСТ РВ 0015-002-2012. Address: ул. 2-я Машиностроения, д. 25, стр. 5, Moscow. GD: Максимов Юрий Алексеевич; also listed: Финансовый директор Дробаха Инга Сергеевна, and Тарасенко Ирина Анатольевна / Обрезанова Ирина Вадимовна in unspecified other roles. Products directly confirm the brief's tagged names: «Взгляд» is listed on-site as a "Система видеонаблюдения" (video surveillance system, product page /products/nabl/vzglyad), and «Наблюдатель»/«Наблюдатель-Н»/«Наблюдатель-ДР»/«Наблюдатель-Н-ДР» are a family of day/night optical-electronic reconnaissance complexes with laser rangefinders (detection ranges up to 17km for vehicles, 7km for personnel). The company's flagship product line is the МПАК (Мобильный Привязной Аэростатный Комплекс, mobile tethered-aerostat surveillance complex), in production since 2012, reportedly used by Russian MoD forces in Syria and for security at the 2014 Sochi Olympics, 2018 World Cup, 2013/2019 Universiade, and MAKS airshow. The ЦБСТ-network tie is direct, not inferred: this project's own scraped database holds a @nashapravdafond post (channel 1905141464, msg 240, 21.02.2024 — i.e. predating even ЦБСТ's own 2024 founding, from its co-founder fund) describing the fund personally delivering «Взгляд» and «Наблюдатель» day/night optical reconnaissance complexes "производства АО «Росс»", alongside EW-jamming vehicle kits, to a reconnaissance unit of the «Центр» group of forces, with troops trained on-site and equipment verified by spectrum analyser. ИНН now confirmed: 7704097425 (КПП 772201001, user-supplied), resolved via list-org.com to АО «РОСС» (full legal name «Радиоэлектронные и оптические спецсистемы»), registered 19.08.2002, ₽1,000,000 charter capital, GD Максимов Юрий Алексеевич — matching the site exactly. Main OKVED 26.40 (consumer electronics manufacturing) plus 26 additional codes spanning comms equipment, optical devices, aircraft equipment and software. FY2022 financials (most recent on file): ₽637.7m revenue, ₽50.4m net profit, ₽368.2m total assets — a modest but real, long-established defense-electronics manufacturer, not a shell. bo.nalog.gov.ru's open БФО-summary endpoint returns no filing at all for this ИНН (empty result), so a more recent year isn't available via that route; a primary ГИР БО/Ресурс БФО pull (nalog.gov.ru) would be needed for FY2023–2025 figures. Хутешен Алексей Александрович's own corporate footprint extends well beyond Атлант Аэро: a ФНС director/founder cross-reference on him returned 14 distinct entity ties, each pulled and independently confirmed via a full EGRUL выписка rather than taken from the aggregator summary alone. Beyond Прометей, НПК Атлант Аэро and АВИАОК (above), the remaining ties resolve into a cluster of eight smaller entities — Атлант Сервис, Атлант Логистик, Пром-Тех, НПК Зевс, Оптические Системы, Аметист, Эверест, ПК Стальной Купол and ДС-БАРС-Т — carrying real-estate, wholesale and construction ОКВЭД codes rather than anything aviation-specific, and added to this investigation's registry at a lower tier on that basis. Two structural patterns recur across them. Three (Оптические Системы, Аметист, ПК Стальной Купол) share one exact registered address — б-р Яна Райниса, д.41, Южное Тушино, Moscow — which a direct НСПД geoportal lookup confirms is an ordinary residential apartment tower, not a business premises of any kind: a classic mass-registration mail-drop signature. And one person, Беленкова Анжелика Евгеньевна, directs two of them (Аметист and Эверест) simultaneously — a nominee-director pattern. Пром-Тех separately carries a ФНС "недостоверность сведений" (unreliable-data) flag entered 05.06.2025; its own EGRUL change-log identifies the flagged field as its registered address, forcing a legal-address change three months later — to another residential apartment, in a different city. «ТСГ» — a previously-parked lead, now fully resolved: the ЦБСТ-resident exhibitor at an Отряд «Яким»/«Барс-Курск» field test (KurskCity.ru, ~May 2026) showing six products (Шелкопряд, Подсолнух, Незабудка, Брянск-1, Подснежник-800, Дирижёр) with no findable legal name at the time is ООО «ТИ ЭС ДЖИ ГРУПП», trading as TSG Group (тсг-групп.рф, ИНН 3257085996, Bryansk region — consistent with the "Брянск-1" product name) — GD Абрамова Татьяна Александровна. Its founding charter and a 2025 restated version have now been pulled directly (nalog.gov.ru, user-supplied, 19.08.2026): the original устав (Протокол №1, 07.03.2023) and the current one (Протокол №13/2025, approved 07.05.2025) both open with "Общим собранием учредителей" — plural founders convening a general meeting, not a sole-owner structure, though neither charter names individual participants inline (that detail sits in a separate решение/список участников not included in either ФНС bundle). Charter capital is unchanged at ₽10,000 across both versions despite the company's real, independently-documented growth to a ₽68m balance — consistent with reinvested profit/founder loans funding expansion rather than a capital injection — and, as with every other founding document reviewed in this network, the purpose clause carries no drone/EW-specific language. The company's own Telegram channel (@tsg_group71, 488 subscribers) and site both describe it as a developer/manufacturer of "РЭБы, БПЛА, оптические катушки, намоточные станки и комплектующие" (EW systems, drones, optical spools, winding machines and components), explicitly pitching a "full production cycle" and service to "leading state structures and major private companies." Its product line matches the earlier field-test sighting directly: «МГЛА СУ.13 Стационар» (stationary counter-drone jammer, 300–2900MHz + 5700–5900MHz bands, 40–80W per amplifier), «Подсолнух» (training drone, 130km/h, 2000m ceiling, 60min endurance), «Незабудка» and «Брянск-1» (interceptor drones, 200–300km/h), «Шелкопряд» (fibre-optic winding machine) and CNC foam-cutting equipment, and «Дирижёр» (a combined drone command station / firmware-programming control unit). Its own FY2023–25 БФО filings (pulled directly) show fast, real growth on a small base: balance ₽4.07m (2023) → ₽58.96m (2024) → ₽68.26m (2025), net profit ₽25.2m (2024) and ₽9.49m (2025) — no revenue line is disclosed in the simplified filing format used, and, as with most entities in this network, no related-party detail is given (small-business exemption from ПБУ 11/2008 explicitly invoked). A photo from its Дронница 2025 exhibit shows a camouflage-skirted product case branded "TSG Group" sharing a tent with a neighbouring exhibitor, ООО «НПО НЕБО» (product: «Караван», a heavy multirotor/octocopter, per its own display poster) — flagged as a new, not-yet-investigated open thread rather than an established connection; sharing a Дронница tent indicates physical proximity at one event, not corporate linkage. Primary doc

A full pass of TSG Group's own site (every product, news, services and contact page, not just the homepage) adds real detail and one important non-conflation flag. Name-collision flag: TSG's own «Стрибог» is an agricultural spraying drone (18L tank, 30kg MTOW, 10–15 hectares/day) made in Bryansk — a different product, city, and legal form from this brief's separate, still-open «Стрибог» thread (ИП Настенков Виталий Геннадьевич, St Petersburg, FPV combat drones «Жужелица»/«Гастелло»); the two must not be conflated. Full specs now confirmed for two products previously only bare-named: «Незабудка» (1.2kg interceptor, ram-strike kill method, 600×650×120mm, 200km/h max / 80km/h cruise, 20km range, 40min endurance, thermal+digital-camera guidance, 5-minute interceptor/recon module swap) and «Подснежник-800» (800×330×80mm recon drone, 1.5kg MTOW, 400g payload, 150km/h max/70km/h cruise, 20km range, 1.5hr endurance, hand-launch or catapult, glide or parachute recovery, compatible with SIYI/Air-Link/EDGE/CRSF/SBus payload ecosystems). Two counter-drone jammer variants beyond the МГЛА СУ.13 already noted: «МГЛА-СТ.10» and «МГЛА-СТ.12» (roof-box-mounted mobile EW, 400–6000MHz / 300–2900MHz bands respectively, both bundled with an «Asel»-branded drone detector — a possible link to this brief's still-open «Инстех» + Asel Labs thread, not yet confirmed as the same Asel), plus two stationary sector-jamming systems («ПАК «МГЛА» СК 2» and «СПК 26», 100–6200MHz range, AI-based automatic detection) and a ground control station («НСУ», 40km radio range). The /services page carries three more verbatim, signed testimonials beyond the БАРС-Брянск one already known, each independently identifying a named unit or state body: "От имени личного состава полка «Ахмат-Россия»... С уважением, Командир войсковой части 11051!" — directly tying the Ahmat-Russia regiment to a specific field-post number (в/ч 11051), tasked per its own letter with "защита и охрана Государственной границы на Российско-Украинском участке" (border defence on the Russia-Ukraine section); and a separate letter of thanks from the Director, СГУП «ЦСН-«Защита» Брянской области» (a Bryansk-region state unitary enterprise) for "содействие в обеспечении средствами РЭБ" (support supplying EW equipment) — a rare instance in this brief of a regional state enterprise, not just a volunteer brigade, directly thanking a resident manufacturer in writing. Primary doc

ЦБСТ's own channel independently names TSG Group a resident by name, cross-posted into TSG's own channel (t.me/tsg_group71/1187, 21.05.2026) — a stronger primary source than the KurskCity.ru press coverage this thread was originally resolved from: "Значительная часть выставки была представлена изделиями резидента ЦБСТ — компании TSG Group", describing the same Отряд «Яким»/«БАРС-Курск» field exhibition with matching specs (Незабудка 200km/h, Брянск-1 300km/h, both 600–800g warheads; Подснежник-800 backpack-portable, ~2hr flight; Подсолнух 2000m ceiling, 130km/h). A full scan of both @tsg_group71 (1114 posts) and its Дронница-2025 tent-neighbour @nponebo — ООО «НПО НЕБО» (573 posts, back to 17.08.2024) — surfaced one genuinely new lead and confirmed the flagged connection is real, not just physical tent-sharing: a НПО «НЕБО» post names «Архангел» (t.me/projectArchangel) as the training facility where its own hardware was field-tested — this is a name collision, not the ЦБСТ-accelerator finalist «Архангел» (ИНН 9714079108) documented elsewhere in this brief; @projectArchangel traces to a separate entity (ИНН 9500026652, occupied Zaporizhzhia oblast) with no confirmed ЦБСТ tie of its own — out of this brief's scope, parked in [[roymarket_archangel_adjacent_network]] rather than investigated further here. НПО «НЕБО» itself is now resolved: a 18.12.2025 post in its own channel names it directly as ООО «НПО НЕБО», a newly-admitted resident of Технопарк-Липецк (5-year resident status, per the technopark's own director Шацких Александр Владимирович) — cross-checked directly against the technopark's public resident registry and ФНС's own БФО open-search endpoint: ИНН 4800023371, ОГРН 1254800002786, registered 21.04.2025 at the technopark's own address (Липецк, ул. 9 Мая, вл. 27), OKVED 30.30 ("Производство летательных аппаратов, включая космические, и соответствующего оборудования" — aircraft/spacecraft manufacturing) — a direct, on-the-nose match for its drone business, unlike most of this network's mismatched OKVED codes. A first БФО filing already exists for FY2025 (filed 30.03.2026): revenue ₽1.007m — small, consistent with an early-stage manufacturer only months old. Its flagship product: «Скарабей» (СД138 heavy octocopter, 7kg empty/17kg loaded, 10kg max payload, 20km stable-control range, munitions-drop system with contact-initiator option, auto EW-evasion manoeuvre) with a directly disclosed base price of ₽190,000 — one of the only entities in this brief to publish pricing openly. Two loose ends, not yet chased: a separate НПО «НЕБО» system named «Аватар», shown deploying ТМ-62 anti-tank mines, with no further detail found; and a national (not ЦБСТ-specific) news repost naming Денис Лямин as head of a newly-created unmanned-systems military branch (05.08.2026) — flagged for context, not asserted as network-relevant. НПО «НЕБО»'s founding charter has now been pulled directly (nalog.gov.ru, user-supplied, 19.08.2026) — upgrading this entity's ownership finding from a secondary-aggregator source to a primary document: the charter (dated 15.04.2025, approved by "решение единственного учредителя №1") independently confirms a single-founder incorporation with ₽10,000 charter capital, consistent with list-org's Загорский finding above, and carries no EGRUL access-restriction markers of any kind — fully open, matching the same clean-disclosure profile already noted for ООО «Аэромакс». Its purpose clause is entirely generic ("извлечение прибыли," no aviation/drone/defence language anywhere in the document) — the same boilerplate-charter pattern already documented at «Архангел» and elsewhere in this network, where public branding runs well ahead of what the founding paperwork actually states. Primary doc

НПО «НЕБО»'s ownership resolves to a single named individual, unredacted: Загорский Александр Сергеевич (ИНН 502714210755) holds 100% (₽10,000 charter capital) and serves as sole General Director — an aggregator cross-check (list-org.com, by company ИНН, ФНС data unavailable this pass due to api-fns.ru's exhausted quota) that also confirms the full OKVED list (30.30 primary — aircraft/spacecraft manufacturing — plus 19 secondary codes spanning wholesale trade, R&D, and vocational training) and a registered contact email, uruslugi.net@mail.ru. This is a plausible match for the channel's own listed admin contact, @ZagerZ28 ("Zager" as a shortened transliteration of "Загорский") — noted as a name-pattern observation, not independently confirmed identity linkage. Secondary source

Name-collision note: «Архангел» is used by at least three unrelated legal entities in this space — the ЦБСТ-accelerator finalist above (ИНН 9714079108, Moscow); ООО «АРХАНГЕЛ» (ИНН 9500026652, occupied Zaporizhzhia oblast, Kalashnikov-partnered drone/training operation behind @projectArchangel); and ООО «Архангел-Центр» (ИНН 5032379630, Odintsovo, an unrelated paid Wagner-veteran-branded combat school). Only the first is a confirmed ЦБСТ resident; the other two are parked in [[roymarket_archangel_adjacent_network]], out of this brief’s scope. Primary doc

A full pass over all 2,291 @ANO_CBST posts (16 Aug 2026) brings the named-resident count to roughly 38 of ЦБСТ's claimed 525 — about 7%, likely close to the practical ceiling this source can give: cbst.ru itself (all 13 pages on the site checked directly) carries no resident roster of any kind, and open-web search for "ЦБСТ резиденты" surfaces none either. Generic single-word company names among these (e.g. «Гермес», «Кравт», «Стратим») are not resolvable via ФНС name-search alone — each returns 100+ same-named companies nationwide with no way to pick the right one without an independent identifier such as a procurement-contract ИНН.

Recipient units named in fund and ЦБСТ posts

Across three years the two channels name recipient formations openly and repeatedly. This is a self-published order-of-battle exposure, and it is directly usable as evidence that the supply chain terminates at combat units rather than at generic “humanitarian aid”.

Armies: 3rd, 20th, 49th, 58th. Brigades: 4th, 5th, 6th Cossack, 9th Guards Motor Rifle, 18th, 24th Separate Guards Spetsnaz (GRU), 27th, 76th Guards Air Assault, 80th, 100th, 114th, 132nd Gorlovka, 136th, 205th Guards Cossack, 810th Naval Infantry. Regiments: 11th and 12th Guards Tank, 71st, 87th, 114th, 166th (Pliyev), 237th Tank, 242nd, 292nd SP Artillery, 345th, 423rd, 440th Separate Helicopter, 503rd. Named formations: «БАРС-Курск», «БАРС-11», battalion «Сомали», «СИГМА Запад», отряд «Яким», 1st Guards Engineer-Sapper Brigade, 17th Tank Regiment UAV subunit. Unit numbers in plain text: в/ч 12676 (Kherson oblast), в/ч 33000 (Luhansk), в/ч 25953.

Question three

Digital and operational infrastructure

Where the physical layer is thin, the technical layer is revealing. The live site’s Content-Security-Policy header names its actual operational partners — this was the single highest-yield recon step of the session.

A second, unrelated marketplace independently confirms the same manufacturer roster is live and selling. «Налетай» (налетай.рф), built by the state transport-leasing company АО «ГТЛК» and operated by ООО «ЦТП» (ИНН 9714054618, registered 17.07.2024), is a fully open, unauthenticated storefront — no login required to browse its public catalogue API. Cross-referencing its own 302-manufacturer facet list against this brief's entity roster by exact ИНН confirms nine of them are actively listing product on the platform: ООО «Гаскар», НПО «Кайсант», АО «НПП «Радар ММС»», ООО «Авирон», АО «Эколибри», ООО «Клеверкоптер», ООО «КоптерЛаб», ООО «КРАВТ» and ООО «Рокот Тех». None of the nine display a company or brand logo on the platform. НПО «Кайсант» — already EU-sanctioned and confirmed ЦБСТ-tied — sells its kinetic FPV interceptor drone «Гусь-7» not under its own name but through a personal ИП storefront belonging to its own founder and general director, Черепкова Нина Сергеевна, a pattern consistent with, though not proven to be, deliberate visibility reduction. Primary doc

Question four

Data sources still unmined

Beyond OCCRP Aleph and EGRUL — which are now largely exhausted for this entity set — these are ranked by expected yield for the specific open questions above.

High yield

ЕИС / zakupki.gov.ru

State procurement under 44-ФЗ / 223-ФЗ. Contracts name counterparties, values, delivery addresses and technical specifications. Azimut almost certainly has a substantial contract history; ЦБСТ and ЗИТ.ЦИ likely do too.search by ИНН: 7701583410 · 7714390623 · 9702064851 · 9714068459

ГИР БО — bo.nalog.ru

Official annual financial statements. МПТМ’s first-year (2025) figures should publish in 2026; revenue and asset structure would show whether the marketplace has any real turnover behind it.free, no account needed

kad.arbitr.ru

Arbitration case files. Litigation exposes counterparties, contracts, lease disputes and — critically for the warehouse question — landlord/tenant cases that reveal actual occupied premises. RESOLVED 2026-09-05: the ₽83.27m case (А40-119451/26, real-estate category) once flagged here as possibly Инвестментс's own is not — all 4 archived filings name the defendant as ООО «Атлантика. Финансы и Консалтинг» (ИНН 7724943210), an entity with no established ownership or corporate tie to Инвестментс or any other ЦБСТ-network entity. A lease-invalidity/unjust-enrichment claim by ИП Тарановская Т.Д. (via her bankruptcy trustee), still pending as of a 20.07.2026 обеспечительные меры order. No further action for this brief.closed — false lead, name/ИНН mismatch confirmed by primary documents

war-sanctions.gur.gov.ua

Ukrainian military-intelligence database of UAV components and the companies supplying them. Directly overlaps this project’s subject matter and has a dedicated UAV-companies section./en/uav/companies

Роскомнадзор operator registry

Personal-data operator registrations. A consumer platform must register; the entry discloses the actual locations where data is processed, which frequently differ from the legal address.pd.rkn.gov.ru — search «МПТМ»

Федресурс (ЕФРСФДЮЛ)

Pledges, leasing, net-asset notices, reorganisations, audit reports. Equipment leasing filings are a reliable way to locate operational facilities.fedresurs.ru

Targeted

Роспатент / FIPS

Trademark filings for «РОЙ» and «РОЙ.МАРКЕТ» would date the project’s real inception and name the filing entity and representative — useful if МПТМ was not the original filer.

ФНС «Прозрачный бизнес»

Headcount, taxes paid, special-regime status, and — directly relevant — whether Нарышкинская 5с2 carries a mass-registration address flag. Settles how much the shared-address evidence is worth.pb.nalog.ru

Реестр лицензий Минпромторга

Independent verification of the Azimut weapons/ammunition licence (Л008-00102-77/00006787) and its current scope and status.

crt.sh / Certificate Transparency

Subdomain enumeration for roimarket.ru and cbst.ru — staging, admin and API hosts routinely leak here. Was unreachable from this environment; worth retrying.

hh.ru and job boards

Vacancies disclose office and warehouse locations, headcount and internal tooling. Several МПТМ-adjacent postings were already found on jobers.ru and dreamjob.ru.

Росреестр / ЕГРН

Building ownership at Нарышкинская 5с2, and — once technopark sites are named — their landowners. Paid extracts.

2GIS / Yandex Maps + panoramas

Tenant listings and street-level imagery for physical verification: signage, loading bays, whether any address is plausibly operational.

Telegram, via this repo’s own scraper

Done for @ANO_CBST and @nashapravdafond (2,889 posts, May 2023 – Aug 2026) — the source of most of this revision. Still unscanned: @roy_market, and ЦБСТ’s newer MAX messenger channel, opened Jan 2026.python3 scripts/tg_scan.py --full --days 2000 --only <handle>

Фонд «ДАР» identification

The single highest-value open item. Наша Правда’s устав (charter), obtainable through EGRUL’s document archive, would name the founding foundations definitively — a standard extract does not, because foundations have no participant block.

Rosreestr — Котельническая наб. 25

Ownership and lease history of office 18. Would confirm or refute the inference that Градислава held lease №18/25-22 of 01.07.2022 and sublet it to Наша Правда nine months later.

Лисицын video archive

The war correspondent who filmed at least nine Наша Правда front-line deliveries. Locating his channel and archive would supply dated visual evidence tying specific equipment to specific named units.

Media sources

Coverage of the 13 August 2026 relaunch was broad and fast, spanning state agencies, business press and exile outlets — itself a signal that the launch was publicised deliberately.

Discipline

What is not established

  • No warehouse address has been found — and the evidence suggests none exists in the conventional sense. Do not assert one.
  • Which Azimut entity is Western-sanctioned is unresolved. Two same-named companies share one director. This is the same trap as the Tula/Moscow ЦБСТ name-collision caught earlier — resolve before publishing.
  • Оупен Лоджистикс’s role is co-location only. It is tempting to cast it as the logistics arm; nothing in the documents supports that. MetaShip is the documented logistics integrator.
  • Technopark locations are unnamed. The 100,000 m² figure is a plan, not built stock.
  • The Medvedev attribution is investigative and sanctions-derived, not documentary. No Russian state record says Medvedev owns ДАР or Градислава — by design, since that is the arrangement’s whole point. What exists is FBK’s 2017 investigation, Western sanctions listings describing Eliseev as his nominal asset-holder, and Eliseev’s own public denial. Attribute it precisely to those sources; do not state it as established fact in Russian law.
  • The Градислава lease is now confirmed directly from Градислава's own filing (superseding the earlier caveat here), but the Rosreestr title chain is still unchecked. Градислава's FY2025 notes state its own lease is №18/25-22 of 01.07.2022 with АО «Фабрика им. Советской Армии» — the exact lease Наша Правда's registration file cites — which resolves the sublease inference from circumstantial to directly-sourced. What remains unconfirmed is the underlying Rosreestr title chain for the Milovka property itself.
  • The ₽12.95bn Градислава spent on "целевые мероприятия" in 2024 is not itemised in any filing reviewed. Only broad categories are given. The named Milovka estate line items (Главный дом, Гостевой дом, hotel, mill, gamekeeper's lodge, etc.) are dated to FY2023 and total far less than ₽12.95bn — so a large part of the 2024 figure funds something not yet identified. Do not assume it is entirely estate-related without further sourcing.
  • Градислава's "mothballed mobilisation-legislation" assets (₽161.8m + ₽92.5m) are unidentified. The balance sheet categorises them under Russian mobilisation-preparedness law but does not itemise what they are.
  • Why МПТМ's ownership register was sealed (15.07.2026) is unknown. The founder field has read "доступ ограничен" since that date. This could be a routine 129-ФЗ protective filing or something more deliberate; no public statement explains it, and the brief should not speculate on motive beyond noting the timing.
  • Who received «Инвестментс»'s ~₽133m in intercompany loans (2023–2025) is now partly disclosed: its own FY2024 notes name АО «ХК Гумич» as a FY2024 borrower (₽12.3m, fully repaid with interest the same year) and a 99%-equity holding (bought ₽50m, sold ₽65m, both in Dec 2024) — resolving one recipient for one year, though most of the ₽133m three-year total across 2023 and 2025 remains unnamed. Гумич's own FY2025 filing further resolves who bought that 99% stake: ООО «РТ-Развитие бизнеса» — confirmed via its own EGRUL extract as 100%-owned by ГК «Ростех» itself — a second, independent Rostec tie for this network. The same notes disclose the reverse flow: «Инвестментс»'s own borrowing is entirely from a single lender, ООО «ФКК» (₽122.2m across three 2023–24 loan agreements at 6–8%) — the first documented money flow directly between ФКК and the Наша Правда/«Инвестментс» side of the network. Nor is it disclosed why Борей — a zero-revenue, tax-delinquent, weapons-coded shell — was absorbed into «Инвестментс» rather than liquidated separately.
  • Why Наша Правда's stake in «Инвестментс» fell from 100% (end-2024) to 90.91% (end-2025) is unexplained. No filing reviewed names a new investor or describes the transaction.
  • «Фонд поддержки национальных морских программ» is now confirmed as a real, independently-filing entity (ИНН 7714389530, GD Устратов Дмитрий Владимирович since 30.04.2021 — matching the name on Наша Правда's own board list exactly), ₽4.729bn in assets, structurally a pure pass-through funder like ДАР. Its own FY2021–25 БФО filings (pulled directly) show it was near-dormant through 2022 (balance under ₽1.3m) before ₽2.253bn in cash donations landed in a single year, FY2023 — the donor is not named in any filing reviewed. What remains unestablished: who its own founders/donors are (no founder rows populated in its Minjust filing, and the FY2023 БФО notes state only that donations were received, not from whom), and who the unnamed NGO recipients of its ₽583m 2025 disbursement are.
  • АО «Сейм-Агро» is now confirmed as a real, operating greenhouse business (10.5 ha complex, 198 employees, revenue growing toward ₽801m) rather than a shell — its own filings identify ООО «Росагролизинг» as the pledgee of a Сейм-Агро share pledge, a strong candidate for the "unnamed third party" holding ДАР's ~₽2.1bn tranche, but the exact match is unconfirmed (different amount, unspecified whose shares) — its own founder/shareholder register remains sealed via EGRUL regardless. Do not assume any connection to ЦБСТ's Kursk-region subsidiary beyond the shared oblast name.
  • Who ultimately controls or benefits from the Winter Olympic Sports Support Fund is not established. Its own address (shared with Наша Правда/Градислава), its director's documented sibling relationship with Наша Правда's director, and its ₽42.9bn scale are all primary-source-confirmed — now independently corroborated a second way via its own Minjust filing, which also shows FY2025 spending of ₽1.067bn booked almost entirely as "sports facility maintenance" against a property schedule disclosing no sports facility at all (only a small rented Moscow office). What that ownership structure means beyond the corporate/family facts themselves — who ultimately benefits, what the "sports facility" spending actually funds, and whether the fund's origins in Sochi-era multi-donor financing still describe its current control — is not established by anything reviewed here.
  • The Olympic Fund's "ГК"/"КСЛ" buildings and its ₽94m counter-drone system (commissioned 2022) are not tied to any specific address. The capital-projects note names the project and its cost but not a location; treat this as a real physical facility somewhere, not as evidence of any particular site.
  • Who holds Заволжское's 1% minority stake (alongside Градислава's 99%) is not disclosed anywhere reviewed, including its own founding-document history requested directly from ФНС. Its original 2013 Устав (approved by a multi-founder "Общее собрание Учредителей," confirming more than one founder existed at incorporation) predictably doesn't name participants in the charter body itself — standard for post-2009 Russian уставы — and Заволжское switched to a fully generic government model charter (Типовой устав №20, Приказ Минэкономразвития №411) on 19.09.2023, which by design carries zero company-specific text at all. The names would sit only in the separate Протокол №1/Договор об учреждении, neither of which nalog.gov.ru's founding-document service provides. Treat this as a closed avenue, not an open task, unless a source for those specific documents turns up.
  • Loan counterparties across the network are consistently unnamed. Инвестментс (~₽133m 2023–25), the Winter Olympic Fund (₽52.4bn short-term flow in 2025 alone) and now Заволжское (₽60.7m in 2025) all run internal lending activity whose recipients are never identified in any filing pulled. This is a recurring pattern worth noting as a pattern, not evidence of any specific wrongdoing on its own.
  • ДАР's securities portfolio (~₽11.6bn) has no issuer names disclosed beyond the Сейм-Агро tranche. Do not describe the portfolio's composition beyond what is stated here.
  • Some individual line-item quantities in Наша Правда's FY2024 equipment inventory look implausibly large next to neighbouring entries in the same table — treat as possible OCR/scan misreads and verify against the source PDF before citing an exact per-item figure. The note's language and the list's overall ~10,400-unit scale are not in question.
  • The АО «РТ-Регистратор» connection (Борей, Спецтех Инвестиции, Спецтех Первый) is a structural signal, not proof of Rostec ownership or control. RT-Registrator services many joint-stock companies; independent confirmation of the relationship's significance has not been done.
  • Mikhelson and Simanovsky are documented as owners of Левит, which founded ДАР. That is registry-grade. It does not establish that either man directed, approved or knew of ДАР’s ₽1.213bn transfer to a war-supply fund seventeen years later, and the brief does not claim they did.
  • Левит does not control Promsvyazbank. A widely-repeated formulation to that effect is wrong. The relationship was the 2015 sale of Первобанк into PSB, terminated by PSB’s nationalisation in 2017–18. PSB is 100% state-owned today.
  • Александр Осадчук may or may not be the sanctioned GRU officer of that name. ЦБСТ lists an MoD innovation-directorate head; a US-designated commander of unit 74455 shares the name. Treat as two possible people until verified.
  • Дмитрий Песков on the supervisory board is the technology official, not the Kremlin press secretary. The distinction is easy to get wrong and would be a serious error in print.
  • Resident-company claims are ЦБСТ’s own. Production capacities, deployment numbers, revenue totals and “first in Russia” assertions come from the organisation promoting them. The EU sanctions listings and the two assassination attempts are independently newsworthy; the performance figures are not independently verified.
  • “Азимут” in the ЦБСТ channel is a different thing. Three hits there refer to a Ukrainian aerostat-mounted optical system, not to the Moscow Azimut companies. Do not merge them.
  • Media facts here are press-attributed, not verbatim. Article text was retrieved through a summarising fetch. Any direct quotation in a published piece must be re-verified against the original. Telegram passages marked “verbatim” were extracted from stored post text and are exact.
